- Heat views use ThermalGrid wave/gradient/island/influence, thermal switch and five-step legend.
- Grids: diagonal 9×6 entry, fluid through tablet, 48px desktop cells, card/70 borders, reduced motion.
- Lab shares server Google locations/geocoding and browser maps with coordinate markers; with the browser key a 2D/3D switch (shown to everyone; 3D opens only for signed-in Premium users, guests get a lock and a sign-in hint) loads the photorealistic maps3d view (lazy, Maps JS + Map Tiles API) in a panel that grows on 3D and shrinks back before returning to 2D (camera centred at Open-Meteo ground elevation, never sea level; re-frames on resize via models/map-3d-camera.ts, plus a top-left recenter button clear of Google's top-right map-type control; pin RELATIVE_TO_MESH and drawn when occluded; the old saved Campobasso default migrates to the real piazza), 2D stays default.
- Dashboard presentation/loading never mutates or refetches demo data.
- Every button and button-like link plays the shared ui-sounds tap on touch devices, in the installed app and in browser tabs alike (phones 0.75, tablets softer and voiced crisper via TABLET_CLICK_TONE high-pass/shelf/low-pass, silent on desktop; interactive-latency AudioContext): mouse on button-down, touch/pen on the lift of a still tap on the same control (pointerup within 10 px, the following click stays silent; a click without that lift still taps once; never on touch-down or scroll; audio woken on touch-down and on return to the app), keyboard on click; the AI camera taps too, except shutter/record/scan (own sounds), which opt out with data-no-ui-click like the system camera.
- Ring animates; mascot skips initial load/reduced motion; sounds never block; Recharts respects reduced motion without changing values.
- Lab has touch-friendly grids; vineyard scope syncs comparison, grid and controls.
- Keep pill selectors on one swipeable row; auto-reveal the selected option on small screens.
- Lab controls precede grids on phones; top-align compact panels elsewhere; desktop tools use grid gutters without resizing cells.
- Touch devices (hover: none, coarse pointer) disable text selection and long-press callouts except in fields.
- No pinch-zoom on phones or tablets: viewport user-scalable=no, base touch-action pan-x pan-y on every element, iOS gesture/scale blocks and ctrl+wheel blocked on touch devices (desktop keeps ctrl+wheel zoom); only [data-map-gestures] maps (the 3D view, gestureHandling GREEDY, one-finger move) keep their own pinch/rotate.
- Long values in label · value rows (profile, history detail) wrap anywhere so they never push past the screen; in the profile card a value over 24 characters (the email) sits whole on its own line under its label, and the email is a mailto link that opens the phone's email app (same colour as the other values, only a faint underline). The page footer note ("I risultati del simulatore sono indicativi…") is text-sm, 15 px from sm up.
- Semantic tokens keep light/dark legible; explicit document themes persist; the app opens dark (the PlayStation-app canvas: html renders class "dark", themeBootScript drops it before paint only for a saved light choice, theme-color follows); dashboard cards/charts never overflow on mobile/tablet.
- Header/favicon use optimized transparent robot PNGs.
- Cases keep dated evidence separate from Query weather and daily source research; authenticated jobs write the public read-only cache.
- Urban dashboard stage and log persist per signed-in user (owner-only RLS); guests keep them in memory.
- Auth in the project's own Supabase (email/password + Google OAuth); schema in supabase/migrations; avatar prefs in user-owned profiles and private avatar storage; guest Lab media persists per flow in device-local IndexedDB, and stays listed (beside the account's files) after signing in, so photos taken before the sign-in never disappear; a failed account listing is retried on the next read; the account listing reads every page (1000 files a call, the storage's default of 100 had hidden everything past Gruppo 5) and signs links 100 at a time (marks per photo are never kept in the account's user data: it rides in every request's token, and sim_hidden / media_archive there grew it until the storage refused the listing and the history showed no photos — takeLegacyMarks moves them to the meta files, clears them from the user data and refreshes the session; a listing that fails shows "Non riesco a leggere le foto e i video del tuo account" with the reason and Riprova in the history, and is retried by itself after 8 s; a batch refused as a whole is signed file by file; a file still without a link stays listed with its previous link or none and the list is read again next time, so a refused signature never empties the history; StorageRing draws nothing at 0, not a dot); uploads queue at module level (lab-media-uploader.ts) and survive unmount; signed in, useMediaSync (lib/use-media-sync, mounted by the root) uploads the device's guest files to the account (syncDeviceMedia: same day kept from the guest id's leading timestamp, the local copy removed once uploaded, its archive mark moved with moveArchived, a toast "Porto n foto e video nel tuo account…") on mount, on sign-in, on return to the app and every 60 s, and otherwise relists both flows (refreshMedia) so other devices' photos appear; signed links younger than 50 minutes are reused so a refresh never reloads pictures or restarts a video; guest ids start with Date.now(); signed in without a connection (navigator.onLine false, or an upload failing for the network) a photo or video is kept on the phone instead (lab-media-uploader keepOnPhone: the same device store as a guest's, listed at once, a toast "Senza rete: n file tenuti sul telefono · Li porto nel tuo account appena torna la connessione") and useMediaSync sends it up as soon as the browser's online event fires; offline the sync does nothing and a failed account listing keeps the account's files already listed, so nothing vanishes while the connection is down; the history does the same (lib/use-history: signed in and offline, or when the account save fails, addHistory keeps the entry on the device — one toast per time offline, "Senza rete: la cronologia resta sul telefono" —; syncDeviceHistory, run first by useMediaSync on mount, sign-in, return, every minute and the online event, upserts every device entry into the account by id (ignoring duplicates, with the same column fallbacks), removes them from the device once saved (removeLocalHistory), says "n interventi ora sono nel tuo account" and fires mdt-history-synced so useHistory reads the account again, as it also does on online); a real account has 50 GB of cloud space for its photos and videos (lib/use-cloud-usage: useCloudUsage sums the sizes of the account's files of both projects — device files count only once uploaded —, bytesText in Italian units, usageTone turquoise, amber past 80 %, red past 95 %): a ring round the account picture in the header fills with the share in use (StorageRing, a faint track, at least a sliver once something is stored; the button's label says "spazio nel cloud … di 50 GB") and the account panel shows it as a bar (StorageBar under the account card: "Spazio nel cloud · … di 50 GB", a progressbar, "n foto e video nel tuo account · liberi …"); guests and the guest session have neither; the 50 GB are enforced (models/lab-media-store CLOUD_QUOTA, cloudUsed, fitsInCloud): an upload that would pass them is not sent — the file is kept on the phone like an offline one (the batch reserves the bytes of files still on their way, released once each answers) and one toast says "Spazio nel cloud esaurito · n file restano sul telefono: hai usato … di 50 GB. Elimina qualche foto o video per liberare spazio." —, syncDeviceMedia sends up only what fits (no start toast when nothing does), and the bar adds "Quasi pieno" past 95 % and "Spazio esaurito" under 50 MB left; beside the bar a red "Svuota" follows the professional profile like the history (the municipal officer's piazza only, the agronomist's vineyard only, both without a profile; the bar keeps the account's whole use of the 50 GB, the line under it counts "n foto e video della piazza / del vigneto nel tuo account"; disabled with nothing of that project stored) and asks in place "Svuotare lo spazio della piazza / del vigneto? Elimino dal tuo account tutte le n foto e video della piazza / del vigneto (…), anche quelli archiviati. Non si possono recuperare." Annulla | Svuota, then removeMedia deletes that project's account files and the bar says "Spazio liberato: n file eliminati della piazza / del vigneto dal cloud" (or to check the connection); the device's own files are untouched; under it (signed in) and in Preferenze privacy (for everyone, a "Cache" card) a "Cache del dispositivo" card (views/v10/DeviceCache DeviceCacheCard on lib/device-cache: deviceCacheBytes — the browser's usageDetails.caches, else the caches' declared sizes —, "… di pagine e mappe salvate", and "Svuota cache", which deletes every Cache Storage cache and the looked-up crests and logos (mdt-crest-v1, mdt-gnss-logo-v1, mdt-service-logo-v1) and says "Cache svuotata: liberati … Pagine e mappe si ricaricano dalla rete."; it never touches simulations, history, settings nor the photos waiting on the phone, and the service worker stays registered); other guest data session-only.
- The account panel on phones unfolds as a circle from the account button; when it is opened from further down the page (the dashboard's "Accedi per sbloccare", a locked tool) the button is off screen, so it unfolds from the tap that opened it (lastTap, else the screen's centre) — grown from the hidden button the circle never covered the screen and the panel stayed invisible behind its veil.
- Shared dialog scopes account overlay; phone icon reveal reverses on close, other dialogs unchanged.
- Tablet/desktop: page and full-document dark backdrop scroll together; dialogs stay modal with fixed backdrop on phones — centralized in the shared dialog.
- AI sensor placement: auth-protected server fn (controllers/sensor-ai) via Sensor.Community + OSM within 1,5 km of the drawn area's middle when there is one (else the place); locked for guests in the page sections (hidden elsewhere); each suggestion shows a "luogo" line and how far it is from the centre. It always answers with points (models/sensor-picks): every source with a time limit (sensors 8 s, the map servers OVERPASS_URLS in turn 12 s each, the AI 40 s, asked a second time when its answer is empty or unreadable), the model's JSON read even in code fences or with trailing commas (readModelJson), its points kept only inside the area looked at and at least 150 m (MIN_GAP_M) from a sensor already there and from each other, with a place line filled from the nearest place (cleanPicks), and filled up to three — or replaced entirely when the AI is missing or fails — by the app's own picks from the same data (fallbackPicks: the place itself, then the sensitive places farthest from the sensors by how vulnerable the people are — kindergarten, school, hospital, clinic, nursing home first — one per direction, and for a vineyard or too few places the slopes and edges around); the panel tags such a result "Scelti dall'app con i dati della zona" or "AI e app insieme", and a dropped connection is retried once by itself before saying so (offline said apart).
- MVC: models hold data/formulas/store, controllers are server functions, views are features, routes are pages, ui/lib are shared primitives.
- Shared logo header stays in normal flow on every page and size; the pinned menu's --nav-h counts only its own height.
- Page files are warmed: once the app is idle the root loads the main pages' code (router.loadRouteChunk for /benvenuto, /urbano, /vigneto, /dashboard, /cronologia, /profilo, /scegli, /); links do not preload on touch (defaultPreload "intent" was tried and dropped: the work it started on touch-down delayed the button tap sound, which plays on the lift), so on a first open — before the offline worker has cached anything — moving between pages never waits for the network and never appears in jumps.
- Deploys: Vercel publishes main only (vercel.json git.deploymentEnabled test false: the test branch is still pushed to stay in step but builds nothing, as the free plan's deployment limit was reached with two builds per change).
- Offline PWA: generateSW at dist/client/sw.js, registered only via src/lib/register-sw.ts (refuses dev/iframe/?sw=off). Pages are NetworkFirst with no timeout (cache "pages-v2", used only offline): a 4 s timeout served a page of an older version with the new files on a slow server start, which broke the app with "reading 'component'". A page of one version running with files of another (a missing chunk, "reading 'component'", routeId — isChunkError in router.tsx) reloads clean once (reloadFresh: every cache deleted and the service worker unregistered, then reload; the app registers a fresh one), from the root ErrorComponent, register-sw's stale handler and the error listeners alike; "Riprova" on the error page does the same. Updates reach the installed app quickly: register-sw asks for a new worker on every return to the app (visibilitychange → registration.update()) and every 15 minutes, and once a new one takes over (controllerchange, only when a worker already controlled the page) a lasting toast says "Nuova versione dell'app pronta · Tocca Aggiorna per usarla subito." with Aggiorna — never a reload by itself, so a capture or an upload is never cut off. Every build serves /version.json ({build: the same time as __BUILD_TIME__}, vite plugin mdt-version-json on the client build): the toast also shows when the version online differs from the one running (checked 10 s after start and after each return), Aggiorna is lib/app-version applyUpdate (tells a waiting worker to take over, remembers the expected version in sessionStorage mdt-update-target and reloads; checkUpdateLanded, run by register-sw at start, reloads clean once — every cache and the worker dropped — when the page came back still on another version), and Cerca aggiornamenti compares with /version.json too, so a version already installed but not loaded is found and loaded instead of "Hai già l'ultima versione" (the plain reload used before could leave the app on the old version). Each update installs light: the precache leaves out the heavy files used only now and then (globIgnores: pdf.worker, pdf, maplibre-gl, ar-scene, jspdf, html2canvas, comuni-it, index.es, purify — kept by the runtime "assets" cache the first time they are used), about 2.7 MB instead of 5.8 MB. The account panel shows which version runs, above Twin (AppVersion: "Versione dell'app · 31/10/2026 · 22:40" from __BUILD_TIME__, lib/app-version buildLabel) with "Cerca aggiornamenti" (while a new version installs — any update, found on return, every 15 minutes or by this button — lib/update-progress followInstall, started by register-sw on updatefound when a worker already controls the page, reads the new /sw.js manifest (manifestKeys: {url, revision} entries, revision added as __WB_REVISION__) and polls the workbox-precache cache every 300 ms for the share already there, never going back and 100 only once the worker is installed; the root's UpdatePill, a small glass pill at the top, shows "Installo la nuova versione · n %" with a turquoise bar, then "Nuova versione installata" for 2.5 s, and the button reads "Installo… n %" over the same bar; its arrows turn while it looks — "Cerco…", at least 1 s, with a pulsing sliver of bar (phase checking, markChecking) — and while it updates; the bar is shown on every update, also when the new version was already downloaded in the background: before the reload applyUpdate fills it from where it is to 100 % in about 0,7 s (showApplying, phase applying: the button reads "Carico la nuova versione… n %" and the UpdatePill "Carico la nuova versione"), so the toast's Aggiorna shows it too; the answer line keeps its height even empty so Twin never moves, and the bar (3 px thin) is drawn in that same 16 px line instead of the answer, so updating never grows the panel nor squeezes Twin; checkForUpdate: registration.update(), waits for the new worker to take over, then reloads; else "Hai già l'ultima versione." or, without a connection, "Senza rete: riprova quando sei connesso."). The manifest's theme_color and background_color are the dark canvas (#1d2129, THEME_DARK), since the app opens dark: Android paints the installed app's splash and system navigation bar with them (white left a light strip under the bottom bar). The manifest link carries crossorigin="use-credentials" so protected Vercel previews serve it with the visitor's cookie and stay installable.
- Index preloader (lib/preloader.ts): on "/" only, an inline script in the shell's <body> draws an animated preloader before any bundle loads (on <html>, outside what React hydrates: "Reale / Digitale" — on the dark canvas with a soft turquoise glow, the same tree twice: on the left a white line drawing that draws itself ("REALE"), on the right a cloud of turquoise points flying into its shape ("DIGITALE"), while a glowing scan line passes from one to the other, a 3 s loop; then "MICRO DIGITAL TWIN" spaced with TWIN turquoise and "Creo il gemello digitale del luogo…"; still and complete with reduced motion); the root calls hidePreloader once mounted and the fonts are in (at most 1.5 s), it stays at least 1.8 s (so the tree forms) and fades in 0.5 s, and an 8 s timeout removes it whatever happens.
- v10 opening and map (built on v8, without any v9 simulation): the entrance is two pages that stand alone and are always dark, whatever the app theme (their roots carry the .dark tokens and color-scheme dark; useDarkChrome sets the dark theme-color while open and restores it on leaving) (views/v10/intro.tsx; the root renders no logo header, desktop menu, footer nor bottom bar on / and /scegli), over an aurora (Backdrop: Twin turquoise, cool blue and a warm heat glow, kept light: soft radial gradients with no blur filter nor blending, moved only by transform (drifts of 14–23 s, a turn every 60 s), and every now and then drifting to another tint — violet-blue, then greener — resting there and coming back (a 48 s hue-rotate cycle); a thin curved horizon of light low on the screen (a huge ellipse's top edge, the night below) floating up and down with a soft halo breathing over it and a white glint running along the line — all light: transform/opacity for the float and the halo, and the glint is a small streak riding an offset path drawn on the same ellipse (HorizonGlint, recomputed on resize); a sky of twinkling data points; sparks rising from the bottom; a vignette for the text; all still with reduced motion; a planet and a grid floor were tried and dropped). The tutorial (/, where a fresh open lands only at the first access on the device — previews, where __DEMO_LOGIN__ is set, show it at every opening —: lib/intro-seen marks it seen when the tutorial ends or the choice opens, then a fresh open lands on the Mappa tab of the profile's simulation (the root's landing: /vigneto for an identified agronomist, else /urbano, with openSimTab(…, "mappa"); lib/use-pro-id getProId reads the profile outside React) and / draws nothing while the root moves on; a shared ?scenario= link skips it; Preferenze privacy can show it again) is five screens swiped sideways (touch events, mouse pointer events on desktop, on a touch-pan-y track: the screens follow the finger, a 50 px swipe turns the page, a mostly vertical move is ignored, the ends give only a little; dots to jump, arrow keys, a haptic tick per screen; off-screen screens are aria-hidden): a hello (Twin big and centred with "MICRO DIGITAL TWIN" spaced under it, TWIN in turquoise, then "Prova gli interventi prima di farli.", "Ecco come funziona, in quattro passi.") then four steps (STEPS: Trova il luogo — "Nella scheda Mappa cerchi il luogo o accendi il GPS, che resta attivo e ti segue. Lo guardi in 2D o 3D, ne disegni l'area con i puntini e la dividi in zone e lotti.", drawn as a map with a five-pin area split by a dashed line and a pin with GPS signal arcs — · Capisci quando hai bisogno del mio intervento (the arc drawing, the same line) · Inquadra una pianta · Raccogli i risultati; the identification comes after the tutorial, on entering a section), each a white line drawing that draws itself when its screen shows (Reveal: IntersectionObserver fade-up with data-shown; .intro-draw strokes with pathLength 1 and a dash-offset transition; drawn at once with reduced motion), "0n — 04", a thin title and one line; no Avanti button: only the swipe (or the dots) moves; the white "Inizia" button appears on the last screen only, and "Salta" leads to the choice too. The choice (/scegli) is its own page: a huge thin "Da dove vuoi iniziare?", one line ("Dicci chi sei: ti identifichi qui e si apre la tua simulazione. Un profilo alla volta."), three numbered choices (01 "Sei un funzionario urbano?", 02 "Sei un agronomo?", each saying how one identifies and what opens, 03 Fotocamera AI; the identification happens here: the white button of a profile ("Identificati come funzionario / agronomo", "Entra nella piazza / nel vigneto" when already that profile) shows its IdentifyForm in place on the dark page, with "Indietro", and once identified its simulation opens; the chosen one white with a line on its left edge and its description unfolding; tap selects, tap again or the white "Inizia con …" opens; arrow keys move) and "Salta, vai al benvenuto". The logo header of the other pages has no logo nor name: the account button on the left, then on the right an "i" ("Come funziona", /?rivedi=1) to the left of the theme switch: it shows the tutorial only, which then reads "Chiudi" / "Fine" and goes back to the page it came from. Home is no longer in the menus: its place (first column of the bottom bar with a history (clock) icon, labelled "Cronologia" in the bar only — the page stays "Benvenuto" — and the first desktop pill "Benvenuto") leads to Benvenuto (/benvenuto, views/v10/Welcome): a teal ambient, PageHero "Benvenuto", two entries (Piazza, Vigneto) and the history of the interventions made, filtered Tutti | Piazza | Vigneto, grouped by day (groupByDay: one card per calendar day headed "29/10/2026" with "n interventi", each row with its time hh:mm only), with a line saying where it is kept. Each entry recorded by the simulations keeps its mix (models/intervention-history HistoryMix: the piazza's trees/lawn/shade, every vineyard zone's irrigation/shade/windbreak plus the scope; history-summary urbanMix/vineyardMix) and shows "Riapri", which puts it back (lib/use-history reopenHistory; the place stays the current one) and opens that simulation on Simula; entries without a mix (older ones, the dashboard log) have none. The history block is shared (views/v10/HistoryBlock): on Benvenuto compact — the latest 5 days as closed day tabs (DayTab: calendar icon, gg/mm/aaaa, "n interventi · Piazza n · Vigneto n · first–last time", a chevron; a tap opens that day's rows under it, aria-expanded) and the latest 12 photos and videos, then a white "Apri la cronologia · N interventi" — every photo and video thumbnail shows a turning turquoise ring while it loads and fades in once there (ShotMedia; still with reduced motion) — kept light: ShotMedia never puts the whole photo or a video element on the page but a small JPEG made once per file (lib/media-thumbs mediaThumb: THUMB_PX 360 on the long side, scaled while decoding by createImageBitmap, a video's frame at 0.1 s drawn from a hidden element, four at a time, kept on the phone too (Cache Storage mdt-thumbs-v1, keyed by the file's id, so each file is made small once per device — Svuota cache drops them and they are made again —), each given at most 12 s (a slow one gives way to the original photo or the video's dark tile so the queue never stalls; a video too short to seek draws its first frame at once), kept in memory for the session, readyThumb shows a made one at once; a photo that cannot be made falls back to its original, a video to the dark tile with its play mark), started only when the thumbnail comes within 300 px of the screen (IntersectionObserver); the viewer's blurred backdrop uses that thumbnail too, and an opened pile deals out only its first 6 thumbnails of the first 3 sections; photos and videos are grouped every 24 hours by calendar day (headed gg/mm/aaaa) and can be archived by day (models/media-archive: keys "piazza-<id>" with the time archived, on this device in localStorage mdt-media-archive-v1 and, signed in (Premium), in the account's media storage as <user>/meta/media-archive.json (lib/account-marks readMarks/writeMarks, written 1.5 s after the last change, remove then upload as the bucket has no update policy) — each change uploaded, and on sign-in the root's useSyncMediaArchive merges the account's archive with the device's (never replaces, so nothing archived is lost) and uploads the union —, nothing deleted; the Archivio card says "nell'account" or "su questo dispositivo"): an open pile offers "Archivia" for the whole day and, while selecting, "Archivia n" beside "Elimina n"; archived ones leave the piles (and Benvenuto) for an "Archivio" card under them on the Cronologia (ArchiveBlock: folded, "n giorni · n foto"; open, a "Vai al giorno" date field and folders named gg/mm/aaaa under month headings, newest first; a folder opens like a pile with "Ripristina" instead of "Archivia", and "Ripristina tutto · n foto" puts the whole archive back; on Benvenuto, where the archive is not shown, an "Archivio · n foto · tocca per recuperarle" row leads to its own page /archivio (routes/archivio.tsx, HistoryPage's ArchivePage with HistoryBlock's ArchiveOnly: back to Benvenuto, PageHero "Archivio", the ArchiveBlock already open and nothing else — no interventions, no piles —, only the profile's project when identified, "L'archivio è vuoto" when nothing is archived; the bottom bar keeps Benvenuto lit there); both count every archived key of the tab, even for files not loaded on this device yet, so Ripristina tutto can always bring the archive back); photos and videos are shown everywhere as piles, one per day — on the Cronologia page a "Per gruppo | Per giorno" switch under the kind pills (PileViewSwitch, radiogroup "Raggruppa foto e video", Per gruppo by default, the choice kept in localStorage mdt-pile-view-v1; Benvenuto stays by day) piles them instead one per simulation group (groupPilesOf: the same Gruppo n as the simulation, whole even when loaded over several days, newest first, "Piazza · / Vigneto ·" before it with both projects, the days under it — daysText "31/10/2026" or "29/10 – 31/10/2026" —; opened, a group pile splits into its days — a header per day, calendar icon, gg/mm/aaaa, the kinds counted and a fold chevron, newest first, each day its own row — just as a day pile splits into its groups) (PhotoPiles: stacked like prints, the newest on top and the next two peeking out turned a little, a count badge, the date and "n foto · n video" under it, two per row; the open pile wears a ring of light turning around it (.pile-ring: 10 px out, a turquoise arc chasing round a faint frame, conic gradient on the registered --pile-angle, masked to a 2.5 px border, 2,6 s a turn, plus .pile-glow, a soft turquoise shadow breathing on the same beat; both scale in; a plain turquoise ring with reduced motion — the original look, restored after trying smaller, slower and blurred ones); a tap spreads that pile right under its own row of piles (useGridColumns: 2, 3 from 640 px, 4 from 768 px; the panel a col-span-full item after the row's last pile, brought into view with scrollIntoView nearest — under every pile it fell out of sight with many groups) and set apart from the page (.pile-panel: a lighter slate card — the sheets' oklch 0.25 in dark, the card colour in light — with a turquoise edge, a faint turquoise halo, a light top edge and a deep shadow, plus a notch on top pointing at the pile it came from, --notch-x from its column), a day pile split by the simulation's groups (useGroupNumbers: uploadGroups of each project's whole list, so a photo reads under the same "Gruppo n" as in the simulation; dayGroups: newest group first, "Piazza · / Vigneto ·" before it when the day has both; each header "Gruppo n" with the kinds counted and a chevron that folds its row, aria-expanded), each group one row of thumbnails that slides sideways (snap, touch-pan-x, [data-map-gestures] so the page's tabs stay put; the card keeps its height however many there are), with Chiudi; opening it is animated (.pile-open: the panel unrolls from the pile with a clip and a short rise; .pile-group: each group fades up, 70 ms apart; .pile-deal: the thumbnails are dealt out like prints — a slide from the pile with a little turn, 45 ms apart; .pile-fold: a folded group closes on a grid-rows transition; all still with reduced motion); a tapped thumbnail opens inside the app, never in a browser tab (ShotViewer, "Foto e mappa": the shared dialog as a full-screen page — on top, 54dvh, the photo or video fitted whole over a blurred copy of itself, on a snapping sideways row of its own ([data-map-gestures]), videos with the viewer's own controls (views/v10/VideoPlayer, also used by the simulation's photo-and-video dialog in LabMedia, autoplaying there; the player fills its box absolutely as a flex centre (absolute inset-0 flex, the video max-h/max-w full; a grid sized its auto row to the video, so a 1080×1920 phone video grew past the box and clipped the bottom controls — the viewer slides are flex too), overlay on z-10 over the video, shown with a tap on the video and hidden by another tap or 3 s after it starts playing: a big glass play / pause in the middle, then a glass panel with a strip of 8 frames drawn from the video to drag along — touch-none slider, arrows ±5 s —, time gone and length, Audio on/off, speed 1× → 1,5× → 2× → 0,5× and Ripeti) and paused when slid away, an "n / N" pill; below, on a panel with rounded top, the project's place (name big and thin, the rest and the shot's date and hour under it), a still MiniMap (satellite tiles at a fractional zoom fitting the drawn area, the area in white, its zones dashed in ZONE_COLORS — the photo's own zone, read from its "Zona - " file-name prefix by ExportSheet's zoneOfName, stronger and the others faded — and the place's turquoise pin; a dark ground when the tiles do not load), chips (Zona: …, the simulation's season and hour from the history entry of that project nearest the shot within three hours, Piazza / Vigneto · foto / video) and Condividi — the phone's own share window with that file, fetched as soon as it shows, checked with ExportSheet's forShare, else saved in Download — and Elimina — "Eliminare questa foto / questo video? Non si può recuperare." asked in place, then removeMedia and the viewer moves on, closing after the last —, messages written in the panel since toasts sit under the dialog; ✕ or the phone's Back closes it); there "Seleziona" turns the thumbnails into checkboxes with Tutte / Nessuna and Annulla (each checkbox thumbnail is w-full: a button sizes itself to its content, and with the picture laid absolutely inside it the square shrank to nothing, so in a pile or an archive folder the photos to pick did not show), and "Elimina n file" asks once in place — "Eliminare n file? Non si possono recuperare.", Annulla | Elimina — then deletes them for good through lab-media-store removeMedia: from the account's storage when uploaded, from the device's IndexedDB otherwise — every deletion for good goes through it — only the Cronologia's (and the cloud bar's Svuota) delete for good: deleting in the simulation (row swipe, Elimina tutti, group bin) only takes the files out of the simulation (models/sim-hidden hideFromSim: marks "piazza-<id>" kept on the device, localStorage mdt-sim-hidden-v1, and in the account's media storage as <user>/meta/sim-hidden.json, merged on sign-in by the root's useSyncSimHidden, moved with the file by syncDeviceMedia's moveHidden; useSimMedia/simOnly give the simulation's own list, used by LabMedia, its groups and the dashboard report), toasts "Tolto dalla simulazione · Resta nella Cronologia", and the Cronologia keeps them in their own groups — groups are numbered on the project's whole list both in the Cronologia and in the simulation (LabMedia filters each group to what is still in the simulation and hides a group with nothing left, so numbers never shift: "Gruppo n · k elementi" when part of a full group was taken out), each thumbnail out of the simulation captioned "Fuori dalla simulazione", and an opened pile holding any offers "Rimetti nella simulazione · n" (unhide); removeMedia: removeMedia keeps only what the storage lists back as deleted (it answers without an error even when it let nothing go, and such files used to come back at the next relist), drops their archive and simulation marks (restoreMedia, unhide), and throws not-deleted:n for the rest, which stay listed with an error toast; a toast says how many, a failure says to check the connection) — and the whole of it on its own page /cronologia (views/v10/HistoryPage: a back link to the page it was opened from — lib/back-to: the "Apri la cronologia" and Archivio links remember their page per target in sessionStorage mdt-back-to:<target>, so opened from the dashboard it reads "Dashboard" and goes back there, with the bottom bar keeping Dashboard lit (historyHome), the Archivio opened from the Cronologia goes back to the Cronologia, and with nothing remembered it is Benvenuto —, PageHero "Cronologia", pills Tutti | Piazza | Vigneto, every entry and photo; the bottom bar keeps Benvenuto lit there). The history follows the professional profile: identified, Benvenuto and /cronologia list only that profile's project (the municipal officer the piazza, the agronomist the vineyard) with no Tutti | Piazza | Vigneto pills, headed "Cronologia della piazza / del vigneto", and Cancella empties only that project's; with no profile all three filters stay. Guests' history is recorded on this device like the account's is in the cloud: useAutoHistory records 4 s after the levers stop, and a change still waiting is recorded at once when the simulation page is left, hidden or closed (unmount, visibilitychange, pagehide); an empty mix (every lever at zero) is not an intervention and is not recorded. On the Cronologia page "Cancella cronologia" shows only on a filter tab that lists interventions and empties just that tab's (all on Tutti; "Cancella cronologia della piazza / del vigneto" otherwise), asking once in place (no browser dialog), on this device and, signed in, in the account (clearHistory(flow)). Every row carries where it is kept (useHistory marks each entry's source): "Con accesso · account" (turquoise, cloud icon) or "Senza accesso · dispositivo" (grey, phone icon), the detail page a "Salvato" row, and signed in with device entries too the section hint reads "Account e questo dispositivo". Entry ids are UUIDs, as the account table's id column expects; the account table's optional mix column comes from supabase/migrations/20261029000000_intervention_history_mix.sql — without it entries still save, only without Riapri. Each entry also keeps the season and hour the simulation was set to (useSeasonClock publishes them to models/sim-when; addHistory stores season/hour, columns from supabase/migrations/20261029010000_intervention_history_when.sql, saving without them when missing); rows show the clock time (hh:mm, the date heads the day's card) and "Stagione estate · ore 15:42" (every simulated hour is written hh:mm — clockText in models/sim-when: the "when" chip and its stepper follow the device clock's minutes while live, an hour chosen by hand starts at :00; the minute is recorded with the entry (minute on the device, a copy in mix.when for the account) and older entries read hh:00) ("Stagione e ora non registrate" when missing; a copy rides in the mix as mix.when so an account table without the season/hour columns keeps them, read back by whenOf; the dashboard's Registro and Quaderno record the simulations' season and hour too), and the row's "Apri" (an arrow, not a reload sign) no longer jumps into the simulation: it opens the entry on its own read-only page /cronologia/$id (routes/cronologia_.$id.tsx, views/v10/HistoryDetail: back to Cronologia, "Quando" — registered on (date), registration time, simulated season and hour, "Non registrata" for older entries — and "Interventi fatti" from mixRows: the piazza's trees/lawn/shade, or each vineyard zone's irrigation/shade cloth/windbreak, only the chosen zone off Tutte; the text alone without a mix); only its secondary "Riprendi nella simulazione" puts the mix, season and hour back (reopenHistory + requestSimWhen, read once by useSeasonClock, no longer live) and opens that simulation; the bottom bar keeps Benvenuto lit on /cronologia/*. The history (models/intervention-history, lib/use-history, table intervention_history from supabase/migrations/20261028000000, applied by hand in the SQL Editor) is fed by the dashboard Registro (piazza) and Quaderno (vigneto) through addHistory: guests keep it on the device (localStorage mdt-history-v1), signed-in users in their account (if the account save fails it stays on the device with a toast). /mappa only redirects to /benvenuto. The map is a tab of each simulation (views/v10/SimMap, kind piazza|vigneto): the 2D | 3D switch (3D for signed-in users only: guests see a lock and "Accedi"), the place search (PlaceSearch: LocationSearch without its own preview — field, search, my position; it changes that flow's place; the my-position button is a GPS that stays on: once tapped it turns turquoise ("GPS attivo", aria-pressed), is kept on this device across openings (localStorage mdt-gps-v1, read after mounting) and follows the phone with watchPosition — high accuracy — moving the place, through the reverse lookup or "La mia posizione (lat, lng)", whenever it has gone more than 100 m (GPS_STEP_M) from the current one; a refused permission turns it off with the message, a missing fix keeps it on and retrying; a tap turns it off; a place chosen by hand while it is on — a suggestion or a search — pauses the following: the GPS keeps running but no longer moves the place (mdt-gps-paused-v1, kept across openings), the button turns to a turquoise outline "GPS in pausa" and a line under the search says "Hai scelto tu la zona: il GPS resta acceso ma non la sposta." with "Seguimi di nuovo", which — like a tap on the paused button — goes back at once to the last position known and follows again; the Autorizzazioni switch clears the pause), the place name and a big interactive map in a 52vh box (centred, with its pin, on the middle of the place's mapped shape — getPlaceArea now returns centre, the area-weighted ringCentroid of the square, park or vineyard plot containing the point — or, when the address's point falls just outside every shape, of the nearest one within 120 m (placeCentre; then no surface is measured, only the pin moves) —, through the same place-area / vine-area query and cache as the surface — the shape is asked to the app's server, which tries the map servers in turn (OVERPASS_URLS: the main one and two mirrors, 12 s each, placeAreaResult) and throws when none answered; then the phone asks them itself (lib/place-area-client loadPlaceArea, used by every place-area / vine-area query), since a busy main server turning the app's server away left the pin on the address beside the square — a pin put right by hand wins over all of that (models/pin-fix: on the 2D map the place's pin is draggable outside drawing and arranging, its spot kept per place on this device in localStorage mdt-pin-fix-v1 and used in 3D too, a toast "Pin spostato"; under the map "Il pin non è al centro del luogo? Tienilo premuto e trascinalo." — "Spostalo sulla mappa 2D." in 3D — or "Pin messo da te." with "Rimetti automatico"), then the middle of the area drawn on the map (ringCentroid of its ring), then the mapped shape's centre; SatelliteMap2D moves the pin and recentres without rebuilding the map —, and on the address's point only without a shape, since the geocoded point can fall a few metres aside, e.g. off the fountain of Piazza Vittorio Emanuele II; 2D: views/v10/SatelliteMap2D, maplibre-gl loaded lazily; with the browser key its base is Google's own map — the Maps JavaScript API (lib/google-maps-js loadGoogleMaps, one loader shared with the 3D view, gm_authFailure read through onKeyRejected) drawing a satellite map with names (mapTypeId hybrid, no controls, no gestures) in a layer under a see-through maplibre that keeps the area, zones, simulation and gestures and leads the camera (every move: Google's moveCamera at the same centre, zoom + 1 for its 256 px tiles; rotation off, as Google's satellite map does not turn), so 2D and 3D show the very same imagery; the Map Tiles 2D session tried first was refused by the key and dropped — else — no key, or the key refused — open satellite imagery and place names (raster tiles, no key, so a key that does not allow the address never shows an error), a pin and a recenter button, one finger moves it and two zoom and turn it with no "use two fingers" lock (the embedded map it replaced forced that on phones), tilt off, the place's pin our own (placePinElement in views/v10/map-area: a red teardrop (#ea4335, darker edge and eye) with a red dot ringed white on the exact point — red like Google 3D's own pin —, its tip the element's bottom edge, anchor "bottom" — the library's default pin, placed by an offset, read a little off the spot; the open 3D uses it too), no credit box on the map (attributionControl false: the imagery credit lives in Licenze), the whole box [data-map-gestures] so a drag on it never turns the tabs; a missing tile leaves a gap, not an error; 3D: with the browser key the lazy photorealistic LocationMap3D of the Lab — if the key is refused for this address the API's gm_authFailure flips a module flag and LocationMap3D renders its fallback, OpenMap3D, instead — without it views/v10/OpenMap3D — maplibre-gl loaded only on 3D, an open vector style tilted 60° with buildings raised from their heights, a pin and a recenter button, no credit box on the map (credits in Licenze); 2D stays default and signing out goes back to it). Over the map, in 2D and 3D alike, day and night follow the simulation's season and hour (models/sim-when useSimWhen, live; models/sun-shadow sunHeightDeg and dayLight: day above 10° of sun, a golden veil at dawn or dusk down to the horizon, a blue twilight to −6°, a dark blue veil at night — rgba(10,18,50,0.5) —; SimMap DayNight, a pointer-events-none veil fading in 700 ms, and a Sun / Sunrise / Sunset / Moon icon at the start of the reading pill). Over the map, in 2D and 3D alike, a reading follows the levers live (SimMap MapReading, top centre, clear of the recenter button and of the 3D view's controls, shown while drawing and arranging too, a short pulse on every change (.map-reading, keyed by the reading; none with reduced motion), and "· a quest'ora non serve" beside the piazza's reading when something is in place but takes nothing off at that season and hour; one small see-through line — a pill of black 20 %, a 2 px blur, a faint rim and shadowed 13 px text so the map shows through; the season and hour said to screen readers only —, pointer-events none, role status): for the piazza the very reading of its simulation's arc (UrbanThermometer publishes before/after and the season and hour to models/sim-reading setPiazzaReading, usePiazzaReading) — "23,1° → 22,7° −0,4°" (screen readers also hear "Calore percepito · Autunno, ore 22:22"), the before struck through, the after coloured on the heat scale (heatTone: red ≥39, orange ≥36, amber ≥30, turquoise ≥18, blue below), just the reading and "· senza interventi" when nothing is in place —; for the vineyard the temperature on the leaves, the zones' average before and after applyZoneMix (shade cloth up to −3 °C), "Temperatura sulle foglie · media delle zone". The simulators have tabs Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI, and their place name and the ⋮ "Cambia luogo" open the Mappa tab. Drawn area (models/drawn-area, views/v10/map-area): under the map an AreaBar offers "Disegna l'area"; drawing (always on the 2D map, the view switches there) a tap on the map adds a numbered pin (up to MAX_PINS 40), pins drag to correct, the fill (a light turquoise veil: 12 % on the 2D and open 3D maps, 14 % in Google 3D) and white outline follow live, and the bar shows the pins and the surface so far (shoelace pinsM2, surfaceText "5.399 m²" / "2,4 ha") with Ultimo pin, Esci and Fine (needs 3 pins); once saved it reads "Area disegnata · …" with Modifica and a bin. The area is kept in the lab state (urban.area / vineyard.area = { lat, lng, ring } of the place it was drawn for, so it counts only while that place is chosen — drawnAreaFor —; Ricomincia keeps it); changing place never loses a simulation (models/place-memory movePlace, called by lab-store setUrban/setVineyard on every location change farther than NEAR_PLACE, about 30 m): the place left keeps its interventions (piazza trees/lawn/shade; vineyard zones with their mixes, scope, older vineyard-level mix) and its area with zones and hand arrangements in urban.places / vineyard.places (the 30 most recent places), and the place reached gets its own back, the area re-anchored to the new spot; a place never seen keeps the current levers without the area drawn elsewhere; under the place search a "Torna all'ultima posizione · <name>" row (PlaceSearch → LocationSearch previous, place-memory lastPlace: the most recent other place kept, with its label) brings the last place left back in one tap, with its interventions and drawn area, pausing the GPS following like a place chosen by hand, and a toast "Sei tornato all'ultima posizione" and synced like the rest of the simulation; its surface replaces the measured one (drawn → measured → reference) in UrbanThermometer and VineThirst ("Superficie disegnata"), in piazzaSummary/vineSummary (drawn flag) and so in the dashboard ("… m² disegnati") and the report rows ("disegnati sulla mappa"), and the GeoJSON export adds it as a real Polygon feature (area_m2, pins). The 3D views show it too: OpenMap3D as a ground layer, LocationMap3D as a Polygon3DElement clamped to the ground and drawn when occluded. Inside the saved area the simulation is drawn (models/area-layout, pure geometry on a local metre grid of whole cells inside the outline) with the very m² the simulation counts: lawn and pergolas cover the lever's share of the usable surface (area less free zones; Prato / Pergolato zones with their own surface, the rest in whole cells of c², so within one cell; PiazzaLayout lawnM2/shadeM2 drawn and lawnTarget/shadeTarget counted, the SimBar legend reading "Prato … m² · Pergolati … m² · Alberi n · … m² di chioma" and saying when the area has no room for all of it), and each tree's crown is the 30 m² of shade the simulation counts per tree (TREE_SHADE_M2, TREE_RADIUS_M = √(30/π) ≈ 3,1 m, used by project-summary and UrbanThermometer too; the 3D crown's widest tier that radius ±5 %); the shadows are cast by the sun of the simulation's season and hour (models/sun-shadow sunAt: the season's middle day — SEASON_DAY —, the clock turned to solar time from the longitude and an hour less with ora legale; area-layout piazzaShadows: each pergola roof moved away from the sun by 3,2 m over the sun's tangent, each tree's crown stretched from its 2,5 m to its 9 m shadow plus a trunk band, capped at 5× the height when the sun is low, none with the sun down; SimMap computes them from the piazza reading's season and hour into SimLayout.shadows, drawn in 2D and the open 3D as the sim-shadow layer over the lawn and under pergolas and trees, and in Google 3D flat at 0,25 m; the SimBar says the shadows follow the simulation's sun, or that at that hour there are none): for the piazza as many trees as the lever, spread evenly 2 m inside the edge, lawn (drawn with LAWN_IMAGE: mowing stripes in two greens with scattered blades, no antialias hairlines between its strips; a lighter green in Google 3D) and shade covering their lever's share of the cells, gathered around their centres (lawn west, shade east by default), merged into row strips, the trees kept out from under the shade; the shade is drawn as wooden pergolas — in 2D a slatted pattern (SLATS_IMAGE, light wood strips with darker gaps) with a wooden frame, in 3D pergolaParts over each strip: posts about every 4 m on both long sides (0–2.9 m), a beam on each (2.8–3.1 m) and roof slats across every 0.7 m (3.1–3.25 m), over a soft shadow on the ground (LocationMap3D: posts raised, beams and slats as floating slabs); for the vineyard three zones side by side west → east, drawn as rows of vines on soil tinted by their thirst (VINE_IMAGES: green < 35, amber < 65, reddish) under a see-through veil of the thirst colour (kind "tint", sim-tint without antialias seams, 30 %; Google 3D a translucent tint) and outlined by white seams where one zone meets the next (Line kind "seam": vertical pieces on each row plus the horizontal steps between rows), drip lines along the rows of each zone (every row at +20 %, sparser below) drawn as a blue pipe with light round drippers, shade cloth on that share of each zone from the north as a dark square-mesh net (NET_IMAGE; in 3D netParts: threads every 1.2 m both ways 2.2 m up on corner posts, see-through), a windbreak along the edge nearest its handle (west by default) as a hedge of round bushes (HEDGE_IMAGE line pattern, as wide as its strength; in 3D hedgeParts: a 1.6 m wall 1.8–4 m tall by strength, lighter on top), and "Zona n" tags. It follows the levers live (views/v10/map-area simFeatures/addSimLayers: 2D fills, dashed blue drip lines and trees as circles sized in metres; trees in 3D are treeParts: a brown 3 m trunk and a pointed crown like a fir — three tiers of cones from 2.5 m to 11 m, the upper ones narrower, ending in a tip — of thin slabs, darker below and lighter on top, each tree its own green from its place — OpenMap3D extrudes them (base/height/colour per slab, 0.5 m), LocationMap3D, kept light so the camera moves smoothly, now lays every simulation shape and the area on Google's own ground model following its curves (models/drape: a polygon bigger than one cell is cut into tiles of at least 8 m, at most 32 — 51 for long thin shapes —, each following the ground at its own corners, its outline redrawn as a polyline with a point every 5 m; every line gets a point every 5 m; walls only get their outline densified; small shapes stay whole — on a hill a single flat polygon cut into the slope and vanished, its edges ran under it; the area lifted 35 cm with a 4 px white edge, ground shapes 40 cm) (build3DOverlay: RELATIVE_TO_GROUND — 15–30 cm up for the area, lawn, shadows, zones, stripes and lines, their height for crowns, roofs, beams and nets, trunks and posts as vertical lines from the ground, the hedge extruded —, drawn through the mesh): laid on the photographed mesh vertex by vertex, a corner on a real tree's crown or a roof was lifted metres and the lawn turned into slanted green wedges, and laid ABSOLUTE at the outside elevation used for the camera everything floated above the piazza; the older mesh-relative drawing stays in the code unused, it laid the ground shapes (area, zones, lawn, shadows, vine zones, stripes, drip lines) on the photographed mesh 20 cm up like the place's pin (RELATIVE_TO_MESH, CLAMP_TO_GROUND only as a fallback: clamped to the terrain model, metres under the mesh in a town, they looked shifted from the pin when tilted), measures heights from the photographed mesh (RELATIVE_TO_MESH: the terrain model can lie metres under it in a town and buried the objects) and draws every shape through the mesh (drawsOccludedSegments), each trunk and post a vertical line from the mesh up, and stacks just three crown discs (treeParts step 2.85), draws each pergola with posts about every 8 m, its beams and one see-through roof (pergolaParts lite) and each shade net as one see-through sheet (netParts lite), beside its Polygon3DElements; looks come from outlines, which cost no extra shape: lawn a fresh green with a darker edge and light mowing stripes across it (mowStripes: east–west lines every 3 m cut to the lawn's outline, at most 24 per lawn, as 5 px polylines), the pergola's beams edged and its roof framed in dark wood over darker posts, each tree five shapes — a soft shadow cast 1.4 m north on the ground, a dark trunk and three crown discs each ringed a deeper green —, the net and the hedge edged too; the Google map is built once per place and the area and simulation shapes are laid over it 250 ms after the levers stop (build3DOverlay), removed and redrawn without reloading the tiles or moving the camera — lawn and zones on the ground, the pergolas over their shadow — and Polyline3DElements). Smaller zones inside the area (DrawnArea.zones: SubArea { id, kind, ring, name }, kept when the outline is redrawn): under the SimBar a ZonesBar lists them (colour, name, surface, lots with their % of the cost, a pencil and a bin each — the pencil reopens the zone in the ZoneDrawBar with its pins draggable, a tap adding one more, kind radios to change it (a lot keeps its name, a zone turned into a lot takes the next free one) and "Salva"; "Superficie per gli interventi: … (… lasciati liberi)") and "Aggiungi zona" offers the kinds — piazza: Da lasciare libera, Alberi, Prato, Pergolato, Lotto; vineyard: Da lasciare libera, Lotto (ZONE_KINDS) —, then a ZoneDrawBar draws it with pins on the 2D map like the area, the big outline shown still (setOutlineData) and taps outside it refused with a toast; while the area or a zone is drawn or edited the rest of the simulation (trees, lawn, pergolas, lines and the other zones — the edited one left out) stays on the map faded to about a third (setSimGhost), the zone tags and handles hidden; a free zone (libera) is greyed, takes nothing (no trees, lawn, pergola or vineyard cells) and its surface comes off the one used for costs (usableM2 = area − free, drawnM2 returns it); a prato / ombra zone is lawn / pergola first and the rest of the lever's share goes around its centre — the zone itself drawn as its very outline (Patch exact: the lawn or the pergola exactly as big as the zone drawn, not grid strips; in 3D pergolaRingParts: posts at every corner and along every edge, about every 4 m — 8 m in Google 3D —, a beam along each edge and a see-through roof of the zone's own shape), only the rest of the share made of strips; a zone given an intervention holds it: when an Alberi, Prato or Pergolato zone appears (drawn, or found on opening the map) and its lever is too low to fill it, SimMap raises the lever (leversForZones in models/drawn-area: lawn/shade at least the zone's share of the usable surface in 10 % steps, about one tree every 60 m², only raising) with a toast "Interventi messi nelle zone", so its trees, lawn or pergola show in 2D and 3D and count in temperature and costs; each zone id and kind is seeded once per device (localStorage mdt-zone-seeded-v1), so a lever turned down later stays down; everything the simulation draws stays inside the drawn area: every tree with its whole crown (treeSpots keeps the trunk CROWN_REACH_M, 4,2 m, from every edge of the area and of its trees zone; with more trees than room the spots get closer and crowns overlap instead of leaving the area, the margin shrinking only where not even one whole crown fits; a trees zone too small for that many whole crowns still gives each trunk its own spot inside the zone, the crown allowed past the zone's edge but never past the area's, instead of piling all the trees on one spot where only the top one could be dragged; a tree moved by hand stays where it was put — even out of its trees zone — as long as its whole crown is inside the area and its trunk not in a free zone, else it goes back to the nearest spot that keeps its crown inside; the 3D tree shadow is cast only 0,4 m north), the windbreak hedge moved in from its edge by half its width plus 0,4 m and kept off the corners (hedgeWidthM 1,5–3 m by strength, the 2D hedge drawn that wide in metres at every zoom, the 3D hedge as wide), lawn, pergolas, cloth and drip lines on whole cells inside (src/test/layout-inside.test.ts); with alberi zones every tree goes inside them and the automatic lawn and pergolas keep off them while there is room; a lot ("Lotto A, B…", zoneName) changes no layout but gets its surface and share of the cost (lotsOf); every zone is outlined dashed in its colour (ZONE_COLORS) under a see-through veil of the same colour (22 %, a free zone greyed; also in OpenMap3D, and in Google 3D as a ground Polygon3DElement with a 40 % veil and a 4 px outline of its colour, drawn through the photographed mesh — drawsOccludedSegments, as every shape of the simulation there is —, since a faint veil alone was lost on the 3D ground) with a tag (zoneTag), listed in the dashboard ("Zone dell'area": zone libere, zone alberi/prato/pergolato, each lot with its share of the indicative cost, zoneSummaryRows) and the report rows, and exported to GeoJSON as polygons (drawn_zone, zone_kind; the area also carries usable_m2). Under the AreaBar a SimBar shows the legend and "Sposta elementi": while arranging, each tree and the Prato / Ombra (or Frangivento) labels become draggable handles (MovedPart → area.edits: trees[i], lawnAt, shadeAt, windAt), "Disposizione automatica" clears the edits, and redrawing the outline starts afresh; arranging switches the map to 2D. In the piazza "Posizione migliore" (a sun icon, turquoise and "· attiva" while on; edits.best, hand moves cleared) lays the elements out where the summer heat hits most (models/sun-exposure: sunPath — the sun every hour 10–18 solar time on a midsummer day at the area's latitude —, sunShare — the share of those hours a spot is lit, the buildings around taken as a 12 m wall (BUILDING_M) 8 m beyond the outline (STREET_M), ray-cast towards the sun): the pergolas on the sunniest cells, the lawn on the sunniest of the rest, the trees on the sunniest free spots that keep their crown inside, off the pergolas and spaced a crown and a half apart while there is room; the SimBar line then says so with "Al sole per gran parte del giorno: n % dell'area" (layout.sunny, cells lit ≥ 60 % of the hours) and a toast explains the assumptions; Sposta elementi still works on top of it and Disposizione automatica goes back to the plain layout (src/test/sun-layout.test.ts). Beside it "Solo quello disegnato" (a pen icon, turquoise and "Solo disegnato · attivo" while on; edits.onlyDrawn; disabled, with a line saying to add one, until there is an Alberi, Prato or Pergolato zone) shows and builds only what was drawn by hand: piazzaLayout draws the Prato and Pergolato zones exactly and nothing automatic around them, the trees only inside the Alberi zones (none without one), and the levers are set from the zones (drawn-area leversOfZones: lawn and shade their whole-% share of the usable surface, about one tree every 60 m² of each trees zone, at least one per zone, at most 30), again whenever a zone changes (the zone seeding is skipped), so temperature, costs, dashboard and report count only the zones; Posizione migliore is hidden while it is on, Sposta elementi still moves trees; moving a lever in the simulation (UrbanThermometer change) turns it off with a toast "Torna la disposizione automatica", as do Disposizione automatica and a second tap (src/test/only-drawn.test.ts).  The two simulations are separate pages, /urbano and /vigneto (views/LabFlows.tsx: UrbanFlow/VineyardFlow keep every Lab tool: real data, Copernicus, sensors, media and AI camera, location search and maps, Compare; no flow switch). Menu: Benvenuto, Simulazione urbana, Simulazione vigneto, Dashboard (Casi studio and Fonti removed). Tabs replace history; Back returns to Home. Tabs slide like the tutorial's screens (lib/use-tab-swipe on the simulations' Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI and the dashboard's Piazza | Vigneto): a sideways swipe anywhere on the page drags the open panel with the finger with the next tab's panel coming in beside it, 16 px apart, its top just under the tabs even when the page is scrolled (useTabSwipe returns ref/style/peek/peekSide/peekTop; only the Cronologia shows it, rendering the other filter's content; the simulations (urbano, vigneto) and the dashboard show no peek: the open tab only follows the finger and the other slides in once it turns; Panels still accepts peek/peekSide/peekTop for FoldTabs), the drag moves the track element straight (translate3d, once per animation frame, no React state while dragging, will-change only during the gesture) so the page is not redrawn under the finger; past the first and last tab it gives a little, softening (at most 80 px); past 50 px — or with a flick faster than 0.45 px/ms over at least 16 px, measured on the last 90 ms — it turns (the page scrolls so the new panel's top stays where its peek showed) with a haptic tick and the new panel, laid out before paint (useLayoutEffect), comes in from that side settling on the tutorial's curve cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) in 180–380 ms by the distance left; shorter it springs back the same way; a mostly vertical move only scrolls; sliders, maps and sideways rows keep their own moves; no transform at rest; Panels skips its own entrance after a swipe (justSwiped); reduced motion moves without animating. Sub-tabs slide the same way (useTabSwipe with a scope element: the Cronologia filter Tutti · Piazza · Vigneto, FoldTabs): only swipes starting inside them count, and while they can turn in that direction they claim the gesture so the page's tabs stay put; at their first or last sub-tab they let go and the page's tabs move.
- Alerts (toasts, components/ui/sonner): pills in the app's look, the same in light and dark — a dark see-through PS slate pill (oklch 0.25, 94 %, backdrop blur, a faint white rim, a deep shadow, radius 28 px so one line reads as a pill), white 14 px title, description at 70 %, the icon coloured by kind (turquoise done, red error, amber warning, light blue info; .app-toast in styles.css), the action a small white pill; at the top centre under the safe area (position top-center, mobile 1 rem from the sides).
- Piazza levers (2026-11): the lawn lever reads "Aiuole e ciottoli" everywhere on screen (lever, legend "Aiuole e ciottoli … m²", zone kind "Aiuole", handles, toasts, history rows and summary text, dashboard and report rows, FAQ, intro, sector page) — the same permeable-ground model and internal keys (permeable, lawn, prato) —, drawn in 2D as beds of short grass with small flowers beside paths of round pebbles (LAWN_IMAGE "lawn-beds-pebbles", a 48 px tile) and in Google 3D with light pebble-coloured paths over the green. A fourth lever "Nebulizzatori" (urban.misters 0–20 by 1, CloudDrizzle, sky blue): each misting pole cools the piazza's average by MISTER_COOLING 0,15 °C (piazzaEstimate's fourth argument, the 5,2 °C cap shared; sliding the arc keeps the misters and moves trees, aiuole and shade for the rest), costs 1.200–2.500 € (UNIT_COST.mister, interventionCost, dashboard/report row "Nebulizzatori"), is kept with the place (place memory), the history (HistoryMix misters, mixRows, summary, reopen, auto-history levers) and the CSV/GeoJSON export (misters_count); on the map piazzaLayout places them (misterSpots: spread evenly by farthest point — under the pergolas when there are at least twice as many pergola cells as poles, else the pergolas and the paving within 8 m of them, with no pergola the paving away from the area's empty corners —, never within 2 m of a trunk while there is room); the lawn and pergola blocks gather round their centres as compact rectangles in the area's proportions (byDist: the larger of the x and y distances scaled to the area's half-sizes, then the plain distance), not round blobs cut into steps and they are drawn as a light blue mist veil 3,5 m round (MIST_RADIUS_M) with the pole seen from above in 2D, a pole in the open 3D, and in Google 3D the ground veil, a pole and a see-through mist disc at 2,6 m; the SimBar legend adds "Nebulizzatori n".
- Plain words: no jargon on screen. The three intervention levels read "Subito, gratis" (zero), "Pochi sensori" (mvp), "Progetto completo" (roadmap), Confronta tags gratis/pochi sensori/completo, the matrix "Quanto costa e cosa si impara"; the dashboard says "calore percepito" (not WBGT) and "Sete della vite" 0–1 (not CWSI), also in the report. The sync indicator is a brief floating toast ("Salvataggio…" → "Salvato") outside the sticky bars, screen-reader only when idle.
- No visible technology: the UI never names data or service providers (Open-Meteo, NASA POWER, Copernicus/Sentinel, Google Maps, OpenStreetMap, Sensor.Community, Gemini…). The RealClimatePanel and CopernicusPanel cards stay in the code but are not rendered; their data keeps flowing (imported weather, AI camera thermal summary, captions read "Satellite"). Exceptions: the Google sign-in button, licence credits of reference photos (author/licence/Kew/GBIF) and admin-only setup errors.
- v6 look (PlayStation-app style, simple and serious): the phone bottom bar is a dark slab with a gently curved top edge (.ps-nav, same in light and dark themes) and five equal icons — Home, Urbano, camera (centre, AI logo once AI is ready), Vigneto, Dashboard; the active page icon is white with a glowing line under it and its name is written once, centred, below the row. Home is a grid of big tiles (Simulazione urbana, Simulazione vigneto, Dashboard): a dark drawn "image" area on top, then title, one line and a meta row. This replaces the v3 arch with the raised round button below.
- v7 "strumento" (serious tech tool, readable without reading): the bottom bar copies the PlayStation app: a frosted slate arch (.ps-nav-glass blur masked to the same path) with white icons and a darker crescent laid over its foot (the bar is a fixed 108 px plus a safe-area filler) that names the open page once, centred; one straight white light with a blue glow (.ps-nav-light) slides along the crescent edge to the open page, its position and tangent tilt per column in PS_LIGHT; the four side icons follow the arch's curved top edge (ARC: the outer columns 6 px lower, each tilted with the edge's tangent, −6° / −3° / 3° / 6° like the light; the camera stays as it is); the centre AI camera rises from the arch (.ps-nav-camera, slate with a faint rim — 1,5 px of white at 20 %, the bright white one stood out too much —, a soft blue glow (14 px at 30 %) and a sober breathing halo — a 1 px ring at 30 % growing only to 1,07× and fading every 5 s, with a pause —, none with reduced motion). Both simulations open with an instrument on a borderless surface that fades from the card colour (white in light) into the page below (.fuse-canvas; views/v7/studio-ui.tsx; only the heat field, its markers and the zone labels keep fixed dark tones): one big mono reading coloured on the five-step heat scale. Urban v9 "C · Termometro" (views/v9c/UrbanThermometer, on the same .fuse-canvas): no grid; the simulation is the focus: the half-ring arc is the one control, order hint → arc → "when" chip → levers → cost details, and once the gauge scrolls away (IntersectionObserver; only when it has gone up past the tabs — a hidden gauge, one sliding in sideways on a page change or one below the screen never shows the pill, which used to flash at the top on a page change) a compact pill (°C, verdict, gain; tap scrolls back) stays pinned under the sticky tabs; under the gauge one "when" chip (live dot, "Stagione, ore H · senza interventi T°") opens the season pills (four in a row), an hour stepper with "Adesso", the data note and the signed-in weather import, synced with the world time read in the place's own time zone (every 30 s and on visibilitychange) until changed by hand — lib/world-time corrects the device clock once per session against the server's (controllers/world-time getWorldTime, half the round trip off, kept on the device clock when slow or offline), the zone is the place's, from its position (see below); models/world-clock placeTime/utcOffsetText/zoneName; the open picker says whose hour it is ("Fuso del luogo, dalla sua posizione · Ora dell'Europa centrale (UTC+1)", "Ora del tuo dispositivo" while the place's zone is unknown) and, when the phone is in another zone, "da te sono le hh:mm", then ora solare or ora legale (summerTime: legale when the offset is above the year's lower one; "Nessuna ora legale qui" for zones without it) with the next change ("arriva l'ora legale domenica 28 marzo: alle 02:00 le lancette vanno alle 03:00"); the zone is automatic from the place's position, never chosen by hand (lib/place-zone placeZoneOf: @photostructure/tz-lookup, CC0, loaded lazily, worked out on the device with no network; then the weather year's zone, then the device's; the old hand choice mdt-zone-v1 is dropped), the line reading "Fuso del luogo, dalla sua posizione · …", and the place's year read at its own zone's hour (shiftHour where the two differ); then, in a zone with ora legale, three radios "Come il calendario | Ora solare | Ora legale" (DstChoice, per simulation in localStorage mdt-dst-v1: the clock reads dstShift minutes on, the line says "Ora legale, scelta da te (il calendario dice ora solare)", the year is read back at the calendar's hour), and two reminders of the next change (ChangeReminders): "Promemoria in calendario" downloads an .ics (changeCalendar: the change with an alarm 6 hours before, the evening before) and "Avvisami sul telefono" asks the notification permission (lib/dst-notice, mdt-dst-notify-v1); the root's useDstNotice tells each change once in the app (a toast, plus a phone notification through the service worker when allowed) from 36 hours before ("Domani notte / Stanotte torna l'ora solare: alle 03:00 le lancette vanno alle 02:00.") and once in the 12 hours after ("Stanotte è tornata l'ora solare…"), changeNotice in the device's zone, seen keys in mdt-dst-seen-v1; history entries record the ora too (SimWhen/HistoryWhen dst, entry dst on the device and in mix.when) and rows read "ore 15:42 · ora legale"; the "before" °C is the place's real perceived temperature averaged over its last year for that season and hour (controllers/climate getSeasonalClimate, hourly apparent temperature from the weather archive → models/season-climate seasonalProfile), else estimatedTemp labelled "Stima indicativa"; every cooling is scaled by coolingShare(before) (1 at 40 °C, 0 at 20 °C: no heat, nothing to take away; the slider is then disabled with a note), and the arc spans only what can be reached now (today + 0,6 … today − 5,2·share − 1,4 °C); under the arc, when the place's weather answers, "Temperatura reale adesso nel luogo n°" (a thermometer icon; views/air-temp-client loadAirTemperature through a query keyed by the place, refreshed every 15 minutes, nothing shown without an answer); above the arc a hint ("Trascina il punto bianco o tocca un livello"; v10: no "Costi e tempi" here, it is the FAQ page); the urban page has no Confronta button nor comparison dialog (SimShell onCompare is optional; Ricomincia in the ⋮ menu resets); then the half-ring gauge of perceived heat (a white dot where the piazza is now, a faint ring where today starts, and the three levels as labelled stops written just outside the arc where their ready piazza would bring the heat: one touch moves the dot there and applies it, the exact one white, the nearest one ringed, hidden when share ≤ 0,15; no separate level cards) that can be slid (an HTML layer over the drawing, touch-action none, is the role=slider: only a touch on the white dot grabs it (the dot itself, drawn a little larger — r 11, 13 while sliding — with a round handle of GRAB_RADIUS 28 px that is exactly the grab zone; the handle is touch-pan-y a tap or click alone never moves the dot, nothing below the arc's ends (min/max numbers, the reading) is grabbed even next to it, and a pointer moves nothing until it has gone 8 px (3 px for a mouse): mostly vertical is a page scroll (capture released, the dot stays put), mostly sideways starts the slide, and a sliding finger's horizontal position is projected onto the arc (tempAtX) so the whole arc is reachable sideways; mouse and pen still follow the pointer's angle; elsewhere the page scrolls), pointer-captured; while sliding the dot and arc follow the finger exactly, clamped to the reachable range, and grow, while the piazza snaps to whole trees; the dot clicks into a stop within 3,5 % of the arc with a haptic tick; arrow keys ±0,1 °C, Page Up/Down jump between stops; sliding walks the path empty → Subito, gratis → Pochi sensori → Progetto completo, mixFor blending the two neighbouring mixes so every stop is exactly its level) with, inside the arc, a plain-words verdict chip (Troppo caldo ≥39 / Molto caldo ≥36 / Caldo ≥30 / Si sta bene / Fresco / Freddo), the °C after and the degrees gained ("−1,2° rispetto a oggi", "A quest'ora non serve" when something is in place but takes nothing off, "Nessun intervento" when empty), and right under the arc two small labels under its end numbers — a blue "Il più fresco" on the left and a grey "Media stagionale dell'area" on the right (with an area drawn for the place the seasonal average and the real temperature now — "Temperatura reale adesso nell'area" — are read at the area's middle, ringCentroid rounded to about 10 m, else at the place's point, "… del luogo"; one line, whitespace-nowrap; "Stima stagionale …" when the place's year did not answer), under the arc's right end, which now reads the exact seasonal reading of the place at that hour without interventions (Gauge rightEnd, fmt(before), e.g. 28,4°) instead of the rounded arc bound — the arc keeps its small margins unnumbered on that side; no sentence explaining them; the label the white dot is near lights up and the other dims (pos = (after − lowest) / (before − lowest): ≥ 0,66 the right one turns warm amber, ≤ 0,34 the left one bright blue, the other at 35 % opacity, 300 ms transitions); the vineyard's arc (VineThirst) has the same two labels — a blue "Sete più bassa" and "Senza interventi" under its right end, which reads the exact thirst without interventions (rightEnd) — lit the same way, and with an area drawn its season's air is read at the area's middle too; then, always open, three one-line levers (Alberi 0–30 by 1, Prato and Ombra 0–100 % by 10) with − / + and ten marks that fill; changing a total clears the placed squares so they follow the default layout; then, with no money on screen in the urban Simula (the costs stay in the dashboard and the report), an always-open "Cosa ottieni" card: one line on the degrees taken off ("Il calore percepito scende di 1,2°: da 28,4° a 27,2°, si sta bene.", or why nothing comes off at this hour), four tiles — CO₂ l'anno (22 kg a tree), Pioggia tenuta (700 mm, 60 % kept by the lawn), All'ombra (lawn-free shade m² plus 30 m² a tree) and Persone all'ombra (≈ one every 2 m²) —, the surface it is computed on (drawn → the place's real m² measured on its mapped shape, controllers/osm-geo getPlaceArea → models/place-area, null when none, never invented → the 4.000 m² reference, each said) and a note with those assumptions. Vineyard v10 (views/v10/VineThirst, replaces VineStudio and its Confronta; built like the urban C): first the scope, a row of four radios (Tutte · Zona 1 · Zona 2 · Zona 3, each with its thirst after the interventions; allZones/selected in the store): levels, levers and rain act on that scope, each zone keeps its own mix (irrigation, shade, windbreak on the zone; zoneMixOf falls back to the older vineyard-level mix), on Tutte the levers show the average mix and setting one writes it to every zone, the reading is the real average of the zones and the cost is the scope's (each zone a third of the surface); then the half-ring arc (the shared Gauge of views/v9c/UrbanThermometer with unit/spoken/label/step props) of the vines' thirst 0–100, the average of the three zones, from no interventions down to the "Progetto completo" vineyard (− 6 … + 4 of margin), with the three levels as labelled stops (VINE_LEVELS in models/vine-interventions: zero +4 % irrigation / 0 % shade / 20 % windbreak, mvp 8/30/40, roadmap 16/80/70); dragging walks the path no mix → the three levels (vineMixAt, binary search on the thirst), keys ±1; inside the arc the verdict (Sete forte ≥65 / Da tenere d'occhio ≥35 / Le viti stanno bene), the thirst /100 and, under it, what that big number is — "Sete delle viti · −31 rispetto a oggi", "· a quest'ora non cambia" or "· nessun intervento" (the cost stays in the details card below); under it "senza interventi n/100", the three zones after the interventions, the four phase pills with a Pioggia toggle for all zones, a Temperatura lever (air temperature of the scope, 15–45 °C by 1, on Tutte the average and moving it shifts every zone by the same degrees), and the signed-in weather import; then three always-open Levers (Irrigazione +0–20 % by 2, Teli d'ombra and Frangivento 0–100 % by 10); then the folded "Dettagli costo e benefici" (cost per hectare in the header; inside the surface — the mapped vineyard plot within 150 m, getPlaceArea kind "vigneto" → vineyardAreaQuery, else 1 ha "superficie di riferimento" — zones safe, −°C on the leaves, −% wind, and the unit costs: drip 3.000–6.000 €/ha, shade cloth 4.000–8.000 €/ha, windbreak 6.000–12.000 €/ha). The mix is stored per zone (older saves: the vineyard-level one, else none) and vineMetrics applies it (applyZoneMix: + moisture, − up to 3 °C, − up to 60 % wind) before reading the thirst, so the dashboard follows. The sticky pill shows the thirst and verdict once the arc scrolls away. v7 canvas (dark theme): the .dark tokens are the PS palette (near-black blue page, slate cards, slate pill for the selected Pills option with plain white text for the others, PS blue primary); the logo header is a slate slab whose lower edge rises in the middle with a handle under it (light theme keeps the plain header); v8 "Console" (views/v8/console): every page has a fixed ambient glow tinted by what it shows — drawn once by the root (AmbientLayer, not on / and /scegli) in --ambient-tone, which each page's Ambient only sets (plus the status-bar tint); --ambient-tone is a registered colour (@property) with a 600 ms transition, so on a page change the light glides from one tint to the next and never goes out (a leaving page clears it only if no other page took it over within 600 ms) —, animated: over the still light, in the top band only (AmbientPools: the upper 26vh, masked to fade downwards), two soft pools of the page's own tint (background from --ambient-tone) drift sideways and breathe slowly (.ambient-pool: 16 s and 20 s alternate drifts, 9 s and 11 s opacity breaths, transform/opacity only, still with reduced motion), seen through the transparent header, a PageHero (small kicker, big thin title, round slate actions), PS pill tabs (TabRow: the open one on a slate pill, scrolled into view inside the row only) and one panel at a time;
