the logo header (account button, "i", theme switch) is transparent: the page's ambient light and its drifting pools show through it (.app-header-tint is no longer used); so are the sticky tab bars (simulations, locked section, dashboard) and the tablet/desktop menu, which keep only a backdrop blur (the menu only while pinned) so they stay legible over scrolled content; Ambient also sets the page theme-color (THEME_META, never the camera's own) to that glow over the page colour, restoring the plain one on unmount (smaller logo and name; on the right the theme switch beside the account button, its only place: not in the account dialog nor in the desktop menu); selection is a white PS5 focus frame with a soft glow (no cyan) and big readings are thin display numerals.
- v3 camera dock: the phone bottom bar is an arch (SVG .nav-arc) with a big central camera button between Urbano and Vigneto (phones only; tablet/desktop menus have none). It opens one shared sheet (views/CameraDock.tsx), a bottom sheet on phones (grab handle, slides up; a centred dialog from sm up) that follows the professional profile: identified, it never asks where — it goes straight to that profile's project ("Foto e video della tua piazza / del tuo vigneto"): a big Fotocamera AI card (turquoise ring and glow, "Inquadra una pianta: la riconosce e calcola il fresco", the project's place as a turquoise chip; locked with "Accedi per usarla" for guests, which opens the account dialog; hidden for a signed-in browser tab) and three round actions Foto, Video, Galleria (no separate Multiplo any more: Foto opens the app's live camera on Singolo — a single photo takes ImageCapture.takePhoto's full-resolution photo when given within 4 s, else the frame, then closes —, and its switch or a held shutter makes it the multiple shot: the app's live camera, SystemCamera mode "burst", on a light live stream (ideal 1920×1440, at most 30 fps: the 12 MP stream asked before kept phones busy and warm; a single photo is still takePhoto's full-resolution picture, the multiple shot keeps the stream's frames), stays open while the page behind it stops drawing (html[data-sys-camera]: every body child but the camera's own, [data-sys-camera-root], is visibility hidden with its animations paused, restored on close) — each shutter tap answers at once (white flash, shutter sound and haptic tick first, then a 160 px preview in the strip) while the full frame encodes off the main thread; no full-resolution photo per shot (ImageCapture.takePhoto paused the preview on phones and made the shots lag); a count line, a sideways strip of every shot numbered with a bin each to drop one, a white "Fine · n foto" (shots still encoding are waited for, then handed over together) —; Back or a swipe keeps them all, nothing shot is lost, while ✕ asks in place "Hai scattato n foto": Aggiungi n · Continua a scattare · Scarta tutte; it opens after the sheet's Back has landed); the in-app camera (SystemCamera, photo or burst) has a "Singolo | Multiplo" switch above the shutter, so it turns into the multiple shot without closing (Singolo is available until the first shot of the multiple shot: once it has photos the Singolo radio is disabled — dimmed, "Prima tocca Fine per tenere gli scatti" — so a series is never switched by mistake, and Fine hands them over; before any shot, going back to it keeps the camera open, backToSingle, in the AI camera too), and holding the shutter (lib/hold-burst useHoldBurst: HOLD_MS 250, then a shot every BURST_EVERY_MS 200 until the finger lifts, frames only, no full-resolution swap while held; CameraCaptureControl onHold, the click after a hold stays silent, label "Scatta · tieni premuto per il multiplo"; no "FOTO" status above the shutter in photo mode — CameraCaptureControl shows its status line only for video, where it reads VIDEO or the REC timer) switches to Multiplo and shoots in a row; the AI camera's shutter does the same; the in-app camera zooms like the AI camera (pinch through CameraCloseGestures onPinch, the shared ZoomDial above the switch, lens-dial haptics; the camera's hardware zoom first, the rest a centred digital crop scaled on the preview and cropped into the photos, where a single photo then keeps the cropped frame instead of takePhoto; videos zoom in hardware only, and show no zoom without it); and a flash button in its top bar (the camera's own torch through lib/camera-torch, shown only where the phone offers it, never simulated; the AI camera's own button — camera-tool size-11 with Zap / ZapOff, "Attiva / Disattiva flash", lit like a pressed tool while on, beside a matching size-11 close —, kept on in photo, multiple shot and video, put back on after a zoom change since some phones drop it, off with the camera; a refusal is written on the camera); the phone's own camera behind Foto cannot, being the system's; off the project's page the AI camera opens there by itself (navigate, then requestCapture retried for up to 4 s), and so does Foto, the app's own camera needing no tap of its own: from any page or tab one tap opens it at once (afterClose → requestCapture retried every 120 ms for up to 4 s), while the move to the project's page and, 400 ms after the camera is up, to its Simula tab (showSimMediaNow) happen behind it; Video/Galleria move to that page with the sheet still on screen (carryTo: the sheet's history step is taken back under a pushBackGuard, the page replaces it and the step is pushed again, so the sheet never closes and reappears while the bar's light slides) — but Video (where the phone's own camera is used) and Galleria never wait for the move: from another page the phone's camera or gallery opens inside that very tap from the sheet's own hidden inputs (CameraDock videoInput / galleryInput, or showOpenFilePicker; galleryMultiple now in lib/system-camera), while the app moves to the project's page and its Simula tab behind it, and the picked files go to that page's media panel through lib/camera-dock deliverFiles / registerFilesHandler (kept waiting until the panel registers; camera videos straight in, gallery picks through GalleryReview) — a capture started after the move (relying on the tap still counting) did not start on phones; elsewhere (no system capture) carryTo still moves first and starts the capture there while navigator.userActivation.isActive, else the sheet stays for one more tap; on the project's page but another tab, the capture first switches to Simula at once (showSimMediaNow: flushSync, no panel entrance, no sliding pill, an instant scroll to the photos) so nothing moves while the camera or picker covers the page; every follow-up waits for the sheet's history Back (afterClose). With no profile it reads "Per scattare, dicci chi sei" and shows Piazza ("Per i funzionari del Comune") and Vigneto ("Per gli agronomi") with a lock, each leading to its identification page. AI in development (lib/ai-standby.ts AI_STANDBY, true for now): signed in, the AI camera reads as being built — the dock button shows the AI logo dimmed with a bright yellow dot on its upper right edge, ringed in the bar's slate and softly glowing (data-building, .camera-dev-dot, still with reduced motion; it replaced a half-filled turquoise ring; aria "fotocamera AI in sviluppo, non ancora disponibile"), the sheet's AI card the same half ring (.camera-dev-ring-card), an amber "In sviluppo" tag, the amber wrench badge and "Non è ancora disponibile: la stiamo sviluppando e arriverà con un prossimo aggiornamento. Per ora puoi usare Foto, Video e Galleria." (plain about the state, no spoilers: nothing says what it will do), dimmed and disabled; LabMedia offers no AI button; the rest of the app says it too without spoilers — the tutorial's step 3 becomes "Scatta foto e video" (a drawn camera, "Fotografa il luogo: foto e video restano nella tua simulazione."), /scegli's 03 reads "Fotocamera AI · in sviluppo" ("Non è ancora disponibile: arriverà con un prossimo aggiornamento. Intanto puoi scattare foto e video.", button "Apri la fotocamera"), Piani lists "Fotocamera AI (in sviluppo, arriverà con un prossimo aggiornamento)" and Chi siamo speaks of photos and videos; the legal drafts keep their data-processing lines; guests see the lock with the same amber "In sviluppo" tag and text, ending "Quando sarà pronta, accedi per usarla."; false turns it back on. The button shows the AI logo once AI is ready (signed in and installed app). Actions reach that page's LabMedia through lib/camera-dock.ts inside the tap; the AI start waits for the sheet's history entry to pop, else the camera reads it as Back. No repeats on phones: below 640 px the media panel's file rows delete with a leftward swipe (SwipeRow: axis locked after 8 px, data-map-gestures so the swipe never turns the Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI tabs, which still turn from anywhere else); the media panel hides its own AI/Carica capture buttons (Invia/Elimina stay) and the page title is screen-reader only, since the bar already offers the camera and marks the page. Preview-only simulated sign-in (lib/demo-login.ts) is turned off for now (DEMO_LOGIN_AVAILABLE false: no "Simula l'accesso" anywhere, a stale flag reads as signed out); __DEMO_LOGIN__ (vite dev and VERCEL_ENV=preview, never production) still marks a preview build (PREVIEW_BUILD) for the tutorial shown at every opening.
- Urban and vineyard pages v8 (views/v8/SimShell): ambient light, the place (tap opens Mappa) above a big thin title, Confronta and the Chrome-style vertical ⋮ context menu (drawn above the sticky tabs; Fotocamera, Foto e video salvati, Dove mettere i sensori, Cambia luogo, Invia all'agronomo on the vineyard, Ricomincia) on the right, then sticky PS pill tabs Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI (v10: no Luogo, the place is in the Mappa tab) with one panel at a time (views/v8/console: openSimTab/useSimTab module store; a simulation always starts on Mappa — the store's default, and SimShell's startOnMap on entering the page (bar, Benvenuto tiles, menu, fresh open, a new identification) — unless a tab was asked for by name in the last 1,5 s on the way in (the dashboard's Modifica and Riprendi nella simulazione open Simula, the camera's photos too); Panels mount a panel on first open and then keep it, hidden). Simula holds the v7 studio (UrbanStudio / VineStudio) and, below it, the photos and videos with no visible title (LabMedia's list is folded by upload in groups of 20 — models/media-groups MEDIA_GROUP, uploadGroups: oldest first by the time in the file's name, so the first 20 uploaded are Gruppo 1, a full group never changes and every 20th upload starts a new one —, shown newest group first with its files newest first; each header "Gruppo n · 20 elementi" or "Gruppo n · 7 di 20" while it fills, "n foto · n video" and a chevron, aria-expanded, shown from the first file; only the newest group open until one is toggled; a bin beside each header asks in place "Eliminare il Gruppo n? n file (… foto, … video): non si possono recuperare." Annulla | Elimina n, now "Togliere il Gruppo n dalla simulazione? … restano nella Cronologia, dove puoi rivederli o eliminarli per sempre." Annulla | Togli n, which takes the group out of the simulation only (hideFromSim) with a toast; the other groups keep their numbers) (a section labelled "Foto e video" for screen readers; LabMedia embedded with no card, on the same .fuse-canvas fade; its AR/system cameras and file inputs portal to document.body); Simula is always mounted so the camera dock reaches LabMedia from any tab, and a dock photo/video/gallery or the ⋮ "Foto e video salvati" opens Simula and scrolls to it (showSimMedia). Luogo is LocationCard; Sensori AI is SensorAdvisor embedded (guests see a locked card with Accedi that opens the account dialog; the simulated preview sign-in shows a labelled example result built around the place, models/sensor-example.ts).
- Dashboard v10 (views/v10/Dashboard.tsx, rebuilt from scratch as the summary of the user's two projects; its numbers come from models/project-summary.ts — piazzaSummary/vineSummary, piazzaMix/vineMixRows, heat/thirst words and steps — shared with the PDF report, and the piazza's season is a module store, getDashSeason, so the report prints the season on screen): a big thin "Dashboard" title with the white "Esporta report" (ExportReport: PDF, copy, print, GeoJSON, CSV of the open tab) on the right, then two sticky PS pill tabs filling the full width in equal halves (TabRow fill) on a bar that bleeds to the screen edges (mx calc(50%−50vw), body overflow-x clip; on phones the pills themselves reach the edges, no side padding), Piazza | Vigneto (?tab=Piazza|Vigneto, anything else falls back to Piazza). Each tab, top to bottom: the result card (.fuse-canvas: place, a white "Modifica" back to the simulation, the same half ring as the simulation but read-only — faint ring where it starts, white dot where the interventions bring it, verdict chip, big thin reading, level ("Su misura" off the stops) and cost); the piazza reads exactly what its simulation shows (UrbanThermometer publishes to models/sim-reading the before/after, season, the hour the year is read at and the arc's ends dialMin/dialMax; piazzaDash in Dashboard, shared with the PDF report, uses them: "Come nella simulazione · autunno, ore 04:20", the same arc, numbers and verdict colours — Fresco on water blue, Freddo on urban blue); the four season pills then light only the simulation's season and switch the others off (disabled, dimmed, "La stagione e l'ora si cambiano nella simulazione" under them; piazzaSummary takes atHour), and only before the simulation has been opened in the session are all four free, reading the hottest hour of the season picked (real year of the place via getSeasonalClimate, else estimatedTemp said "stima indicativa"), the vineyard the average thirst of the three zones as left in the simulation; then "La giornata" (piazza: 24 h area chart, without vs with interventions, coolingShare applied) or "Le tre zone" (vineyard: thirst after/without, temperature and water per zone); "Cosa ottieni" (three facts: CO₂, rain kept, shade · zones safe, °C on the leaves, wind); "Cosa hai messo" (each lever with its unit cost and the indicative total on the measured or reference surface); no photos or videos (they live in the simulations and the report). Then the records: free for everyone the Registro interventi (piazza, saved per signed-in user, in memory for guests) or the Quaderno di campagna (vineyard), then, free for everyone too, "Invia il progetto" (views/v10/Dashboard SendProject, a "gratis" tag, one line on what goes and a white "Invia il progetto della piazza / del vigneto" that opens the Esporta report sheet through lib/send-project openProjectSend — only the open project, so an identified municipal officer sends the piazza and never sees the vineyard, the agronomist the reverse; Piani lists it among the Free features), then "Premium": signed in (or simulated on previews) it shows, for the piazza, Approvazioni degli enti — the approval cycle bar and the three public bodies (Comune · Giunta, Soprintendenza, Ufficio tecnico) each with referent, date, state and "Invia richiesta via email" (a real account sends the secret answer link; the simulated sign-in prepares the email without it and says so; "Simula arrivo parere via email" without a real account) — and, for both, Piano suggerito dall'AI (FieldAI, shown on the simulated sign-in too); guests see each Premium tool as a row with a lock (tapping opens the account dialog) and one "Accedi per sbloccare" plus "Simula l'accesso" on previews. No AI scans, satellite or sensors on the dashboard. The demo-only KPI rings, heat alerts, probes, ripening curves, rescue irrigation and water balance are gone (also from the report). The PDF report (ExportReport buildPdfImpl) comes in two looks of the same layout, cached apart: the dark one for Salva PDF / Salva in…, and a light one for Stampa (white page, pale slate cards, dark ink, deeper turquoise; buildPdfImpl(light)). It wears the app's v10 look: dark pages (the PS near-black canvas) headed on every page by the robot logo and a spaced MICRO DIGITAL TWIN (TWIN turquoise), "Report · Piazza/Vigneto" on the right and a hairline under them, a cover (turquoise REPORT · PIAZZA/VIGNETO kicker, the place name big and thin, place and date), the result card drawn like the dashboard (half ring with the five-step gradient, faint ring where it starts, white dot, verdict pill, big reading, level · cost, "Da … a …"), then La giornata (24 h lines, without orange / with green) or Le tre zone, Cosa ottieni (three big numbers), Cosa hai messo (slate rows card with the total), Quaderno di campagna, AI scans and photos; short cards move whole to the next page with their title; footer note and n / N; the copied text uses the same rows.
- Esporta report (views/v10/ExportSheet, opened by the dashboard's white "Esporta report"): the camera's bottom sheet in steps with a bar of dashes, moved by a sideways swipe (the step follows the finger, past 50 px or a flick it turns, a little give where there is no step; mouse drag too; rows that scroll sideways keep their move), the ← button or the white button: 1 "Quali file vuoi?" — switches with their weight: Report (PDF, its weight once drawn), Tabella (CSV), Dati per la mappa (GeoJSON), Foto e video (off without media; "Scegli quali · n di N"), Testo nel messaggio; Opzioni "Comprimi le foto" (1600 px JPEG, shows before → after; videos stay) and "Pagine chiare" (the light PDF); "Avanti · … · size" —; 2 "Quali allego?" (only with photos on: a grid of the simulation's photos and videos, all chosen, a tap takes one out, pills Tutte · each gallery zone · Nessuna); 3 "A chi lo mandi?" (chips of what goes; Agli enti — "Per email" in the vineyard —, which opens the email app, Condividi, Salva sul telefono — a zip when more than one file —, Stampa when the report is on: the existing print preview); then the files are prepared (ring %, rows ok/spinner/in coda, "Continua in sottofondo" leaves a pill above the bottom bar that reopens it) and "Pronto" lists them with their real weight; the white button sends them, never as a link: Condividi opens the phone's own share window (every app: WhatsApp, Telegram, Drive, Gmail…) with the files attached, each checked against Chrome's own lists of the kinds it takes (forShare: SHARE_EXT / SHARE_MIME; canShare() says yes to everything, then one file of another kind makes the phone refuse the whole share with "NotAllowedError: Permission denied"), typed by its extension (a fetched photo can come typed octet-stream), a file without extension named from its type, the map data sent as plain text ("….geojson.txt"), in turns of at most 10 files and 45 MB ("Condividi · parte 1 di 2", one tap each, as the phone takes no more at once); files the window does not take at all (.mov, .heic…) are saved in Download and said so; a refusal is written in the sheet with its reason, to tap again or Salva; Email ("Apri l'email") opens the default email app (Gmail…) through mailto with subject and message, and since a web page cannot attach files there, the same tap saves them in Download (one file, or the zip made while preparing) to attach with the paper clip; Salva saves the file or the zip). The sheet sits on a lighter slate than the page (dark: oklch 0.25, a light top edge and a deep shadow) so it stands out; the camera sheet (CameraDock) and the gallery review sheet wear the same slate, top edge and shadow, "Mandalo anche" offers the other ways, plus "Apri il report" (the in-app preview) and "Ricomincia". File names follow the place (report-piazza-vittorio-emanuele-ii.pdf).
- Timed email-demo progress stays in local presentation state, not saved decisions; stages derive from entity decisions, Realizzato needs explicit completion; external approvals render below chart/summary.
- Shared scenarios use a base64 ?scenario= param on / (sections redirect there on fresh open); Home applies it and opens /urbano or /vigneto.
- Vineyard stress index: shared component; in the controls panel on phone/desktop, a full-width strip under grid+controls on tablet.
- Compare: stable baselines, snapshot resets; vineyard highlight = card closest to sliders, shared bounds.
- Mount dialogs closed, then open via state; StrictMode's double mount makes useBackClose close initially open dialogs.
- The AI camera button shows only in the installed app (display-mode standalone/fullscreen/minimal-ui or iOS standalone), never in a browser tab.
- Gallery review (views/GalleryReview, from LabMedia's gallery input or file picker, so also the camera sheet's Galleria): photos and videos picked from the gallery first open a sheet rising from the bottom like the camera's — "N dalla galleria · Per la tua piazza / Per il tuo vigneto", a sideways strip of previews (videos show their first frame and length, each with a ✕, a dashed "+" to pick more into the same batch), "Dove l'hai scattata?" (optional, the zones drawn on the map for the current place, free zones left out; the chosen zone rides in the file name "Lotto A - foto.jpg" so the list and the report show it) and the white "Aggiungi N alla piazza / al vigneto"; the same sheet then follows the upload ("Carico 2 di 4", a progress bar, a spinner/check/retry mark on each preview, "Continua in sottofondo"), and closed while it runs a pill above the bottom bar ("Carico 2 di 4 · Piazza", a ring) reopens it; the queue's own toast reports the end; a gallery pick no longer opens the share sheet by itself (Invia does). On Android the gallery input allows picking several files at once from Chrome 120 (galleryMultiple: the Android photo picker, a Google Photos–like grid, handles multiple), older Chrome keeps single picks (multiple opened the file manager there). Camera shots still go straight in.
- Ordinary capture uses synchronous system-camera.ts clicks on Android/iOS and live SystemCamera elsewhere because Windows ignores capture; gallery stays separate and touch AI stays visible.
- AR uses validated photo planes, no synthetic plants or placement screen; ar-placement bounds size/cooling. ar-frame excludes grids from photos; ar-frame-loop caps 30 fps and pauses hidden pages.
- AR recognition uses a shared validated model and a server-only streaming parser; durable service pauses prevent repeating denied requests across reloads.
- Camera close gestures (views/CameraCloseGestures, lib/ar-close-swipe, the AI camera and the in-app camera alike): sideways only like Android's back gesture — a swipe starting in the 32 px strip (BACK_EDGE_PX) along the left or right edge of the screen and moving inwards, clearly sideways (isEdgeBackSwipe, screen coordinates); a sideways swipe across the middle of the preview never closes; upward from the preview's lower quarter still does.
- Preview swipes exclude controls. Shared CameraCaptureControl owns the capture dock/local timer; camera-scoped tokens keep footage legible. Touch policy gates center capture.
- AI rotation uses plane-gravity dead bands, shortest-turn snapshots and shared preview/scan counter-rotation; native resize wins (landscape-natural tablets reporting 90/270 with a portrait orientation type stay upright with their system bar), flat/pitched holding stays stable. Reduced motion skips animation; close restores portrait. While open, lib/camera-chrome.ts hides the white system bars: browser fullscreen when the host allows it (from the opening tap; iOS has none) plus a camera-owned dark theme-color meta kept first in head as fallback, both undone on close. One rotation at a time: once fullscreen is granted the camera asks the shared syncAppOrientation for "any" and, if held, follows Android's native rotation with no CSS turn (cameraRotatesNatively), re-laying out once after the reported size settles (NATIVE_SETTLE_MS, sensors ignored) with a short fade of the camera surface when portrait/landscape flips; syncAppOrientation never re-requests a lock it already holds and treats a superseded request (AbortError, fired by the shell's resize/rotation/tap re-locks) as no refusal, so it never unlocks mid-rotation (that flashed landscape → portrait → landscape); outside fullscreen (refused, iOS, or after leaving it) the app stays portrait and the CSS camera surface turns alone. A fullscreen exit is read as Android Back and closes the camera at once (one Back), unless a screen-orientation change happened within 1 s (fullscreenExitIsBack), which keeps it open — and then, if the camera is still open half a second later, it goes back to fullscreen by itself (regainFullscreen: at once when the browser allows it, else on the next tap anywhere, which carries the needed activation; cleared on close), so turning the phone never leaves it out of fullscreen; the camera dialog is sized by its four edges only (fixed, inset 0, width/height auto; overlay h-auto), never 100vw / 100dvh, which lag behind an Android rotation in fullscreen and left the camera square; Chrome's own fullscreen notice is the only one.
- AI HUD groups compact selectors with Info/flash/close; separate hints/results prevent overlap. Landscape tools sit beside thermal results; on smartphones (short side < 600 px) the specimen balance and the Esito/Analisi/AR tools panel also sit in that right-hand column and the expanded summary stops above the capture row, and the balance folds like an accordion to a one-line summary (Bilancia termica · esemplari · °C) whose header sits at the bottom and never moves: the body clips away and unfolds bottom to top; in portrait the expanded summary stops above the bottom HUD (measured --ar-summary-room) and scrolls, with the balance aligned to its left edge; in phone landscape the tall bottom HUD passes taps through its empty part so the minimized summary reopens; tablets keep their layout with no toggle. Keep one expanded panel, honest scan phases and camera-local tutorial swipes.
- AI identification shows leaf evidence/uncertainty; bounded specimen counts drive cooling without stage plants. The specimen dock shows perceived temperature before → after (coolingReadout: Lab realTemp or Open-Meteo air temperature via views/air-temp-client.ts, minus the cooling), flashes each specimen step (cooling kept to thousandths; coolingLabel/coolingReadout switch to hundredths or "< 0,01 °C" for small plants so steps never read −0,0), and keeps the Copernicus surface temperature as a reference line. Photo captions freeze botanical/thermal results for existing media storage and PDF inclusion.
- AI camera zoom (lib/camera-zoom.ts): pinch on the preview (CameraCloseGestures, two fingers cancel the close swipe) or three Xperia-style chips (views/ZoomDial, shared with the in-app camera: neighbouring lens stops 1·2·3·5 on the sides, live value in the larger centre chip, tap = 1×; a sideways swipe on them — 6 px, mostly sideways, pointer-captured, the click after it swallowed — opens a light ruler above them (no panel: thin ticks and small numbers straight on the picture with a soft shadow, faded at the ends) sliding under a fixed thin amber centre mark, logarithmic like the Xperia dial (lib/zoom-dial dialZoom: DIAL_PX 110 per doubling, left zooms in, right out, 0.1× steps; rulerTicks: a mark every 0.1×, longer on the halves, numbers on whole steps), which also shows for a moment after a pinch or a tapped chip and folds back 0.9 s after the finger lifts; still with reduced motion), 1–5×, with lens-dial haptics (zoomHaptic: 0.5× ticks, lens-stop detents, limit bump) via hapticTick; hardware track zoom first, the rest a centred digital crop shared by preview, tracker sample, recognition frame and photos.
- Flash shortcut uses capability-checked track torch, never simulated light; recognition uses full frame and same-frame leaf crop.
- GIS/CSV uses a pure model and selected-flow snapshot; place coordinates are reference points, not invented geometry.
- Home videos: tablet/desktop autoplay the tutorial in the hero slide only on the first app access (TUTORIAL_AUTOPLAY_KEY), with sound when allowed, else muted until the first tap/click/key (never fullscreen unless the expand button is used); phones (short side < 600 px) never autoplay; a tapped video opens in browser fullscreen locked landscape (setHomeVideoLandscape), falling back to the CSS-rotated edge-to-edge surface where fullscreen or the lock is refused; leaving fullscreen closes it and restores portrait. Hide before close.
- ar-focus holds per-tab AI camera targets (prompt appended server-side); ar-subject-tracker owns pure vegetation tracking.
- AI camera sections keep their own scan: switching section sets the current result (species, soil, survey, specimens, cut-out) aside and restores the other's, for both flows. Every completed scan is logged per flow in models/ar-scan-log.ts (module store mirrored to sessionStorage, session-only, specimen count kept in sync) and listed in that flow's dashboard report (PDF and text), photo or not. The AR tools "Elimina tutti" (shown while specimens or records exist) asks to confirm in an in-camera alertdialog (a short blurred fade in place) over a blurred veil (inside the rotated surface, never window.confirm), then clears the specimens, every section's result and that flow's scan records. The confirm shows a big bin beside Annulla that empties (lid up, files out) for the ~1.1 s the deletion takes, skipped with reduced motion. The expanded summary pages through the flow's recorded scans (swipe via tutorialSwipeStep, which wraps last → first and first → last for the tutorial too, plus ‹ › buttons and "Scansione n di N"); past records render read-only, and the badge on the section pills follows the page ("2/5", the bare total with one or no record; absolute, no row width). Esito shows the scan's own photo as the main picture (cropRealPhoto; each record keeps a light 400 px copy under its own sessionStorage key, scanPhoto, removed with the record) and, in a corner frame it fills edge to edge (ar-esito-reference-inset, object-fit cover), the species' reference photo from a botanical database (models/ar-reference-photo.ts: Royal Botanic Gardens, Kew via Plants of the World Online first, then an openly licensed GBIF field-observation photo; it must show the whole plant: wholePlantScore ranks habit captions first and drops leaf/flower/fruit close-ups and herbarium sheets, order Kew habit → GBIF → uncaptioned Kew; never fetched inside the scan, so the scan and its sound keep their length: after a recognized species the camera calls getReferencePhoto (server, Kew + GBIF in parallel, 8 s timeouts, logs "AR reference photo: none"), retries GBIF from the phone when that finds none (gbifPhotoSearchUrl), then fills Esito and the record via setScanSpeciesPhoto), with its credit as a plain caption and no link; either photo alone fills the frame when the other is missing; no video; not the cut-out; the cut-out still drives placement. Esito and the specimen balance follow the summary page (on a past record the balance shows and edits that record's specimens and cooling via updateScan, −/+, drag and keys alike, "scansione n di N" in its header; the live AR copies are untouched; hidden for a record without a cooling estimate): on a past record it shows that scan ("Scansione n di N" · section · time, its photo, species or soil, specimens), and the Esito chip shows while records exist.
- AI scans upload a lighter frame (longest side 1280 px, JPEG 0.85; leaf close-up 768 px): an HTTP error with an empty body (oversized or slow host) is retried once at 960 px without the close-up, and if it fails again the message says "risposta vuota dal server" instead of empty parentheses.
- Each ar-focus target seeks vegetation or ground: vegetation scans stop before the AI without a tracker lock, and any scan stops when the result lacks its target. The scan loop plays optional public/sounds/scan-loop.mp3 with fades, else a synthesized hum.
- Admin: /admin (ssr:false) uses server fns in controllers/admin.functions.ts guarded by requireAdmin (user_roles via service client); every change writes admin_audit_log.
- Copernicus (Premium, signed-in, on demand): controllers/copernicus.functions.ts calls the CDSE Sentinel Hub Statistical API with CDSE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET; models/copernicus.ts builds Sentinel-2 L2A NDVI (SCL cloud mask) and Sentinel-3 SLSTR S8 surface brightness-temperature requests and parses the latest valid interval; labelled as real but informative, never fed into the simulation; views/copernicus-client.ts shares one reading per place between the Lab panel and the AI camera, which shows it in the thermal summary, adds it to the detect prompt as area-only context (models/copernicus satellitePrompt) and to photo captions.
- External services are called directly: Gemini (GEMINI_API_KEY, re-emitted as Responses SSE) or OpenAI Responses via lib/ai-responses.ts, Google Maps/Places, parallel.ai. Supabase URL + publishable key are pinned in integrations/supabase/config.ts; only SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY comes from the environment.
- AI camera "nessuna pianta": the colour lock (lib/ar-subject-tracker.ts: gray-world white balance, chromaticity green so dark/shaded foliage counts) only steers the reticle and the focus crop. scanPrecheck stops a vegetation scan before the AI only when nothing is locked AND the frame is unusable (frameUsable: near-black or flat); an unlocked usable frame goes to the AI with a prompt to search the whole frame (small, off-centre, shade, backlight, dark/silver/autumn leaves, trunk only). The prompt maps shrubs, hedges, herbaceous and potted plants to type tree, and the server treats any result with plant data as a plant, so a plant labelled "object" no longer reads as missing.
- AI camera species: the model returns plant.alternatives (up to 2 look-alikes, nullable; withPlantAlternatives fills it for replies that omit it) and the server keeps the first of [its pick, alternatives…] that Catalogue of Life or GBIF confirms. A plant without a confirmed species is not a scan: no record (the scan counter does not move) and no scan photo; soil-only scans of ground targets are still recorded.
- v10 account panel and info pages: the account panel keeps the avatar above "Account" and ends with Twin still at the bottom (TwinStill: the mascot drawn without animation nor name, smiling eyes); before it, after the sign-in or account section, grouped rows like a phone settings page (rounded cards, bold heading, rows with a chevron): Piani · Free e Premium (/piani, simplified: two plan cards, Free "Senza registrazione" and Premium "Gratis con la registrazione" with "Tutto di Free, più:", a few plain grouped lines each (FREE_FEATURES / PREMIUM_FEATURES, no service names); the user's own plan is highlighted — white ring, soft glow, green checks and an "Il tuo piano" badge — the other muted; guests get "Accedi per passare a Premium", which in the panel goes back to the sign-in list), Progetto · Chi siamo (/chi-siamo: why the app exists, what it does, where it started — a piazza in Campobasso and a Molise vineyard — and the team, partners and school left as bracketed placeholders, with a link to Contatti), Aiuto · Domande frequenti (/faq) and Contatti (/contatti: email, phone and seat rows plus "Segnala un problema", whose mailto adds page and device; the addresses are left to fill in like the legal drafts — an empty one shows its bracketed placeholder and no link, the report button appears once the email is set), Privacy · Informativa sui cookie (/privacy/cookie), Informativa sulla privacy (/privacy/informativa), Preferenze privacy (/privacy/preferenze), Legale · Licenze (/legale/licenze), Termini di servizio (/legale/termini); the header shows the plan in use ("Piano Free" / "Piano Premium", Premium = signed in or simulated) and the Free e Premium row says "Il tuo: …"; a row opens its page inside the panel, which stays open (InfoNav context: back and links between pages stay in the panel; the page slides in, follows a finger dragged to the right and goes back past 70 px; the phone's Back returns to the list through a pushBackGuard on use-back-close; closing the panel drops the page's history step too); the routes still open the same pages on their own. The pages (views/v10/legal-pages.tsx on views/v10/InfoPage: back arrow — it goes back and opens the account panel again, to pick another row — + thin title, text cards, Faq rows as native details that open with a +) are: the FAQ — the old "Costi e tempi" table opened up into plain questions (what the app simulates, the three levels, cost, time, precision, what each level teaches for the piazza and the vineyard, where to start, where data come from, whether data are saved), so neither simulation has a "Costi e tempi" button any more; cookie and privacy notices and terms marked as drafts with bracketed parts to fill in; Preferenze privacy with real controls (show the tutorial at the next opening; wipe this device's saved data with a confirm, account data untouched); Licenze with the data licence credits (including the satellite navigation systems behind the phone's position for the map's GPS button: GPS, Galileo, GLONASS, BeiDou, QZSS, NavIC, shown under that line as six tiles — SatelliteLogos: each system's logo on a white tile with its name and country under it, the name alone in the tile while there is no logo; models/gnss GNSS/gnssLogoQuery/gnssLogosFrom: the logo is the Wikidata logo image P154 of the system's English Wikipedia article, one SPARQL query for all, so no item id is hard-coded; lib/gnss-logos asks once, 8 s at most, keeps the answer in localStorage mdt-gnss-logo-v1 and never keeps a failed lookup; credited with the crests as Wikimedia Commons files, the logos belonging to their owners), a "Servizi usati dall'app" card first as logo tiles (the Software card stays a plain list of names and licences, LIBRARIES, no logos) (views/v10/LogoTiles on models/credit-logos SERVICES / LIBRARIES — only the services that gather data and the technologies the app is made with, plus the satellites; nothing about publishing the site or signing in (no Supabase, Supabase JS, Vercel nor Google sign-in), by choice —: each tile the logo on a white tile — a dark one for a light mark, needsDarkTile —, the name and one line, what it is for or the licence; the mark is a bundled Simple Icons path in its brand colour (src/data/brand-icons.ts, CC0, only the ones used) or else the Wikidata P154 logo of its English Wikipedia article through the same lookup as the satellites (lib/gnss-logos wikiLogos, one query per list, kept per list in localStorage mdt-service-logo-v1 / mdt-gnss-logo-v1), else the name alone, shrunk and wrapped to fit; a line says the marks belong to their owners), a "Permessi del telefono" card before Software (what position, camera and microphone, and notifications are used for, only with permission and while in use, and that position and camera can be revoked inside the app at any time in Autorizzazioni, which also says where to remove them in the phone's settings) and the open-source libraries.
- v10 seasons in the vineyard: the same "when" chip as the piazza (views/v10/when: useSeasonClock + WhenPicker, shared with UrbanThermometer) sits under the arc: season and hour follow the device clock until chosen by hand and set the zones' air temperature from the place's real year averaged by season and hour (else estimatedTemp with a 36 °C summer peak, said so), keeping each zone's difference from the others, and the vine's phase (spring Germogli, summer Acini, autumn Vendemmia; winter keeps it); applied once per season, hour and place (vineyard.climateKey), so the Temperatura lever can still tune it by hand.
- Professional profiles (models/professional-id, lib/use-pro-id): the vineyard simulation is for agronomists and the piazza for municipal officers, one profile at a time, so identifying as one locks the other; with no profile both are locked. Separate identification pages: /identificazione/agronomo (views/v10/Identify IdentifyPage kind agronomo: name, licence type Consulente | Utilizzatore, patentino fitosanitario number, issuing region — whose coat of arms works like the town's for the officer (REGION_QID maps each region to its Wikidata item, regionCrestQuery asks P94; shown at the end of the agronomist's form under "Regione …", beside the agronomist's ProfileCard title and in the agronomist's round Profilo button) — a combobox (RegionField on the same SuggestField as the town: all 20 regions as soon as it is touched, narrowed as one types, names starting with it first, searchRegions; the region must be one of them, findRegion takes its own spelling) —, expiry still valid, a declaration that the data are true) and /identificazione/funzionario (name, Comune — a combobox (ComuneField) that, from two letters, lists up to 8 Italian towns with their province code (models/comuni searchComuni: name starts, then a word starts, then contains, each by population; accents, case and apostrophes ignored) from src/data/comuni-it.json (7.904 towns, ISTAT 2020 via comuni-json, MIT, credited in Licenze; lib/comuni loads it lazily, ~75 kB), arrows/Enter/Escape, and the town must be one of them (its spelling and ISTAT code are taken from the list — comuni-it.json rows are [name, sigla, population, istat] —; the email example follows the picked town); at the end of the form, once a town is picked, a card shows "Comune di …" with its coat of arms (views/v10/TownCrest: lib/town-crest asks Wikidata's SPARQL endpoint for P94 by ISTAT code P635 — models/comuni crestQuery/crestUrl, a 160 px Commons picture —, 8 s timeout, the answer kept per town in localStorage mdt-crest-v1, a failed lookup not kept; nothing drawn while loading, without a crest or offline; the crests credited in Licenze only, no caption on screen), and the funzionario's ProfileCard wears the same crest beside its title with "Comune di …" under it (TownCrest label; "Regione …" for the agronomist) (profile.istat, else found by name), and so does the round Profilo button of Benvenuto (useProfileCrest: the crest fills its circle, else the profile's badge); the account picture stays the chosen image —, Ufficio di appartenenza — a combobox (OfficeField on SuggestField: every listed office as soon as it is touched, narrowed as one types, searchOffices; OFFICES now also lists Edilizia privata, Patrimonio, Protezione civile, Sviluppo economico, Cultura e turismo, Servizi sociali; an office not listed is written by name and saved as such, officeOf mapping it to "Altro" + its name, so there is no separate "Altro" field) —, Ruolo, the town's email …@comune.<nome>.it, optional matricola, the same declaration); checkAgronomo/checkFunzionario check the form only (no register is queried, and the pages say so), a field's message clears when it changes; once identified the page shows who you are, "Vai …" and "Esci dal profilo". The profile is kept on the device (localStorage mdt-pro-id-v1) and, signed in, in the account's user data (pro_id; useSyncProId in the root adopts it on sign-in or uploads the device's); signing out of the personal account signs out of the professional one too (account-control signOut first saves it in the account with keepProIdInAccount, then useSyncProId's SIGNED_OUT drops it from the device with a toast "Sei uscito anche dal profilo professionale"), and the next sign-in to that account brings it back; a guest who identified without an account keeps it on the device. /urbano and /vigneto render SectionGate (views/v10/ProfileGate): nothing until the profile is read, then the simulation or the locked section ("Sezione … · bloccata") showing only its "Profilo" tab with the identification form of the profile that opens it right there (IdentifyForm: the agronomist's in the vineyard, the municipal officer's in the piazza; the current other profile and that switching locks it); once a profile is identified the locked section gives its place to that profile's badge in the bottom bar (same column, itemsFor: the agronomist's where Urbano was, the municipal officer's where Vigneto was; with no profile both sections stay, locked) and in the desktop pills (navFor, "Profilo agronomo / funzionario"), drawn in views/ProfileIcon in the bar's 24 px line style (a badge on its clip: AgronomoIcon with a leaf, FunzionarioIcon with the town hall); tapping it opens that profile's page /profilo (lit there and on /identificazione/*); the account panel's first card is "Profilo professionale" (identified: only that profile — the badge, name, summary, "Esci dal profilo"; with no profile: the two identification pages); /profilo (views/v10/ProfilePage) shows ProfileCard (who is identified, their data as rows, "Vai …", "Esci dal profilo", which locks the section again; no "Cambia profilo"), or with no profile the two ways to identify; SimShell keeps Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI; the dashboard follows the profile: identified, it shows only that profile's project (the piazza for the municipal officer, the vineyard for the agronomist; ?tab is ignored and the swipe is off) and, in the place of the Piazza | Vigneto tabs, a profile strip (badge, "La tua piazza / Il tuo vigneto · name", proSummary, to /profilo); under "Cosa hai messo" the project's history (HistoryBlock compact flow=piazza|vigneto: "Cronologia della piazza / del vigneto", closed day tabs, "Apri la cronologia · N interventi" counting that project) before the Registro or Quaderno; the report's cover and copied text add "Redatto da <name> · <proSummary>"; Esporta report is hidden on a locked project; with no profile the dashboard keeps both tabs and a locked tab shows SectionLockCard (who it is for, how to identify, the white "Identificati come …" to the identification page, "Torna …"); with no profile the bottom bar and the desktop pills wear a small lock on both sections, the Benvenuto tiles a lock and "Per i funzionari del Comune" / "Per gli agronomi"; once identified, the Benvenuto tile of the locked section gives its place to a round profile button (ProfileButton: an 80 px circle with the profile's badge and "Profilo" under it, centred in the locked tile's half of the row so the open section's tile keeps its size) leading to /profilo; the account panel has a "Profilo professionale" card (current profile, the two identification pages, "Attivo").
- Sector gate (views/v10/SectorGate, mounted by the root, portalled to body at z 999999999 — over menus and bottom bar, under the account dialog): with no professional profile (never chosen, or after signing out of the account or of the profile) and the tutorial already seen, every page but /, /scegli, /identificazione/*, the info pages, /reset-password and /admin is covered by a dark full-screen page like a sign-in one (the entrance's Backdrop and useDarkChrome, the page behind not scrolling): "Micro Digital TWIN", a huge thin "Da dove vuoi usarmi?", "Scegli il tuo settore e identificati…", two big choices — Settore urbano (municipal officers, the piazza) and Settore agricolo (agronomists, the vineyard) — and, for guests, "Hai già un account? Accedi" (openAccount; the account's saved profile comes back). A choice shows that profile's IdentifyForm with Indietro and the same Accedi row; once a profile is in (identified or restored by the sign-in) the page goes away and that simulation opens on Mappa. No way past it without choosing. It opens straight on one sector's form, over any page, when asked by name (lib/use-pro-id openSectorGate / useSectorGateAsk, cleared once a profile is in): leaving the profile (the account panel's or ProfileCard's "Esci dal profilo", which also closes the panel), signing out of the account (SIGNED_OUT) — both on the kind just left — and the account panel's rows "Agronomo · apre il vigneto" / "Funzionario comunale · apre la piazza" (now buttons, no longer links to /identificazione/*), so the profile page is skipped. The form recognises a profile registered before: the last profile of each kind is kept (localStorage mdt-pro-id-last-v1, and pro_last in the account's user data, merged on sign-in; profiles saved before count too) and shown as "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" with its name and summary and a white "Continua come <name>" that puts it back (toast "Bentornato…"), its project's history coming back with it; "Oppure registra un altro profilo" keeps the form under it.
- App permissions (lib/app-permissions, views/v10/Permissions): position (the map's GPS), camera with microphone (photos, multiple shot, videos with sound) and notifications (the change of ora). At the first start, once the tutorial is over (never on / nor /scegli), PermissionsSheet — the camera sheet's slate bottom sheet — shows once per device (mdt-perms-asked-v1; skipped when nothing is left to ask): a row each (icon, what it is for, a state chip Da chiedere / Consentito / Bloccato / Non disponibile, its own white "Consenti"), a white "Consenti tutto" asking them one after the other (geolocation getCurrentPosition, getUserMedia video+audio then video alone, Notification through askChangeNotifications so the change-of-ora notices switch on too), "Più tardi", then "Fatto"; every result is written in its row (changeMessage, plain words agreeing in gender and number). Account → Privacy → Autorizzazioni (/privacy/autorizzazioni, PermissionsPage, also inside the account panel) shows the same rows at any time, "Ho cambiato le impostazioni: aggiorna", and the app's own switches "GPS sempre attivo sulla mappa" (mdt-gps-v1, asking the position if still to ask) and "Avvisi del cambio dell'ora sul telefono" (mdt-dst-notify-v1), each change said in place. A web app cannot take a permission back: a given or blocked one shows where to change it (howToChange: installed Android app → long-press the icon → Informazioni app → Autorizzazioni, or Chrome's site settings; Chrome tab; iPhone; computer) and a blocked one says the app cannot ask again by itself. States are read through navigator.permissions (camera and microphone together, the worse of the two), again on return to the app and on each PermissionStatus change; the root's usePermissions(true) says a change made in the phone's settings once, as a toast. A web page cannot take back a permission the phone gave it (permissions.revoke is gone), so position and camera are revoked inside the app: on the Autorizzazioni page their rows carry a switch ("L'app può usarla" / "L'app non la usa"; a "Revocato nell'app" chip, a message in place, and "Il telefono conserva comunque il suo permesso" with where to remove it there too); the state is kept on the device (mdt-app-perm-off-v1, appAllowed/setAppAllowed/useAppAllowed) and enforced twice — the root's installAppPermissionGuards wraps navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia (video refused with NotAllowedError, open camera streams stopped on revoke) and geolocation.getCurrentPosition/watchPosition (error code 1) for whoever asks, and the entry points say it with guardUse (a toast with "Riattiva"): LabMedia's camera, burst and AI camera, the camera sheet (closes first, as toasts sit under it; the gallery stays allowed), the map's GPS button (the GPS goes off at once when the position is revoked and does not restart on opening while revoked); the "GPS sempre attivo" switch is off and disabled while the position is revoked; asking a permission again ("Consenti") switches it back on in the app.
- Shot kinds (models/shot-kind: shotKind reads the file name, so files already saved count too — "foto-ar…"/"video-ar…" the AI camera's, "multiplo-…" the multiple shot's (SystemCamera burst files are named multiplo-<time>.jpg), any other video "video", anything else a single photo, camera or gallery): the history counts and shows all of them — kindCountText ("1 foto · 3 scatti multipli · 1 con l'AI · 2 video", only the kinds there are) on the piles, the "Foto e video" heading, the folders and the Archivio card; a corner label on every pile and thumbnail (KindMark: an icon and shotLabel — Foto with a camera, Multiplo with Layers, AI or "AI · Multiplo" with Sparkles, Video —; the simulation's file rows in LabMedia read the same label); the viewer's chip names the kind. The Cronologia page and the archive (ArchiveBlock, so also /archivio) split them into sub-categories with one swipeable row of pills (KindPills, role radiogroup "Tipo di foto e video": Tutti, then always all four kinds — Foto, Scatto multiplo, Con l'AI, Video — each with its count, an empty one dimmed and disabled; shown as soon as there is one file); in the archive a kind shows only its folders and "Ripristina le foto / gli scatti multipli / quelle con l'AI / i video · n" restores just those, while Tutti keeps "Ripristina tutto · n file". The archive can also be deleted for good: under the restore button a red "Elimina tutto l'archivio · n" (or "Elimina le foto / gli scatti multipli / quelle con l'AI / i video · n" for the chosen kind; DeleteArchive, hidden while a day is picked) asks once in place ("Eliminare tutto l'archivio? … archiviati vengono eliminati per sempre. Non si possono recuperare.", Annulla | Elimina n) and deletes them through removeMedia (account and device, marks dropped); an opened archive folder has a red "Elimina" for the whole day (the same in-place confirm, all its files chosen) beside Ripristina and Seleziona, whose selection also offers Elimina n. The archive can be saved on the phone as well (SaveArchive on saveShots: each file fetched four at a time from the account or the device, one file saved as it is, several as one zip in Download with a folder per day gg-mm-aaaa, compression STORE, via jszip): "Salva sul telefono tutto l'archivio · n" (or the chosen kind) above the red Elimina, with a turquoise fill and "Preparo n di N…" while it gathers, and a "Salva" in an opened archive folder's header for that day; a toast says "Cartella zip salvata in Download · n file" and how many could not be reached. Both archives carry the date they were archived (archivedOn: "Archiviata il 01/11/2026", or "dal 30/10 al 01/11/2026" over several days) on every day folder, and the interventions' card and opened day too. The interventions have their own archive with the same features (models/history-archive: entry ids → time archived, localStorage mdt-history-archive-v1 and, signed in, <user>/meta/history-archive.json through lib/account-marks, merged on sign-in by the root's useSyncHistoryArchive; archiving deletes nothing): on the Cronologia each day card has "Archivia" and above them "Archivia tutti gli interventi · n" (one confirm in place); archived entries leave the day cards (and Benvenuto, the dashboard's history and "Apri la cronologia"'s count) for an "Archivio interventi" card (HistoryArchiveBlock: folded "n giorni · n interventi · nell'account / su questo dispositivo" with the archive date; open, "Vai al giorno", "Ripristina tutto · n", "Salva sul telefono · n" — a CSV table, ; separated with BOM: data, ora, progetto, luogo, intervento, stagione e ora simulate, salvato, archiviato il —, the red "Elimina tutto l'archivio · n" asked in place, then folders by day under month headings; a folder opens as ArchivedDay with Ripristina, Salva, Elimina, Seleziona — checkboxes, Ripristina n / Elimina n — and Chiudi, its rows EntryRow); deleting goes through lib/use-history removeHistory (device and account by id, archive marks dropped, every history reloads); "Cancella cronologia" spares the archive (only the shown entries go when the tab has archived ones). Benvenuto shows an "Archivio interventi · n interventi · tocca per recuperarli" row to /archivio, which now holds both archives ("L'archivio è vuoto" only when both are). The two archives are told apart by their icons (ArchiveIcon): photos and videos a stack of pictures (Images) in the app's turquoise, interventions a clipboard list (ClipboardList) in a bright violet (violet-300 on dark, violet-600 on light; the PS blue was too close to the turquoise), each round with a small archive box on its corner, on their cards and on Benvenuto's rows; the photo card reads "Archivio foto e video"; the day folders take their archive's colour (turquoise for photos, violet for interventions). Under the pills the Cronologia offers "Archivia tutto · n" (or "Archivia gli scatti multipli / le foto / quelle con l'AI / i video · n" for the chosen kind; ArchiveAll, shown with two or more): one confirm in place ("Archiviare tutto? … di n giorni vanno nell'Archivio. Non si cancella niente", Annulla | Archivia n), then every shown photo and video of every day goes to the archive at once with a toast. The AI camera (ArCamera) has its own multiple shot: in photo mode a "Singolo | Multiplo" switch sits above the capture row (.ar-burst-bar); in Multiplo each shutter tap keeps the captioned photo (named foto-ar<tag>-multiplo-<time>.jpg, so it stays an AI shot labelled "AI · Multiplo") with a white flash and a haptic tick and the camera stays open, a strip of the shots with an ✕ each and a white "Fine · n" adds them all; closing the camera any way (Back, swipe, ✕, fullscreen exit) adds what was shot too (onClose wraps the camera's close and hands the shots over first).
- Vineyard thirst explained: under the arc's labels a row of chips says where the number comes from (models/data vineThirstParts, lab-store vineThirstPartsOf — the same latitude offset and zone mixes as vineMetrics — averaged over the scope after the interventions): "Terreno asciutto +n" ((35 − moisture) × 1,6), "Caldo +n" ((temp − 24) × 3), "Vento +n" (× 0,55), "Fase · <phase> ×w" and "Pioggia −15" when it rains; zero parts dimmed (src/test/vine-thirst-parts.test.ts).
- The user's own sensors (thermal and others; supabase/migrations/20261105000000_sensors.sql, applied by hand: tables sensors — flow, name, kind temperatura | superficie | umidita_aria | umidita_suolo | vento | pioggia, lat/lng, vineyard zone, token_hash, last_seen_at — and sensor_readings, owner-only RLS): models/sensor-devices (SENSOR_KINDS with units, ranges and aliases, parseReadings accepting {value}, {kind,value,at}, {temperature:…, humidity:…} or {readings:[…]} — out-of-range, unknown and future/30-day-old readings dropped, at most 50 a call —, latestByKind, freshAverage over readings of the last 2 hours (FRESH_MS), newSensorToken "mdt_" + 40 hex, tokenHash sha-256; src/test/sensor-devices.test.ts); controllers/sensors.functions (auth: listMySensors with the latest reading per kind over 7 days, saying ready false while the tables are missing, addMySensor returning the key once, renewSensorKey, removeMySensor, addManualReading); devices post to /api/public/sensor-readings (routes/api/public/sensor-readings.ts: key in Authorization Bearer, X-Sensor-Key or ?key=, only its hash stored; GET with the key says which sensor it is; CORS open; JSON answers {ok, saved, dropped}). UI: the Sensori AI tab adds "I tuoi sensori" under the suggestions (views/v10/MySensors, signed in for real only; lib/use-my-sensors useMySensors re-read every minute): each sensor with a green dot when it read in the last 2 hours, its latest value(s) and age, "Lettura a mano", "Nuova chiave" and "Elimina" asked in place; "Aggiungi" (name, what it measures, the vineyard's zone, at the drawn area's middle or the place) shows the key once with a ready curl example to copy; each AI suggestion has "Ho messo un sensore qui", which opens the form at that spot. Fresh readings feed the simulations: the piazza's line under the arc reads "Dal tuo sensore / Dai tuoi n sensori adesso n°" (with the surfaces' thermal reading beside it) before the weather's; the vineyard shows "I tuoi sensori adesso: … " with "Usa nella simulazione", which writes temperature, soil moisture, wind and rain into each zone (a zone's own sensors first, else the whole vineyard's).
- Vineyard levers (2026-11-05, supersedes the shade cloth and windbreak written above): the vineyard's interventions are Irrigazione, "Parassiti animali" and "Parassiti vegetali" (models/vine-interventions VineMix { irrigation, pestAnimal, pestPlant }, VINE_LEVELS zero 4/0/20, mvp 8/40/50, roadmap 16/80/80; costs per hectare and season: drip 3.000–6.000 €, animal-pest defence — traps, mating disruption, targeted treatments — 400–1.200 €, plant-pest defence — rounds against downy and powdery mildew, mowing — 500–1.500 €). Each zone also has how many pests it has (VineZoneState animalPests / plantPests 0–100, PEST_DEFAULT 30 / 40), set in the Simula tab under "Quanti parassiti ci sono" (Animali, Vegetali) like the temperature; a full defence takes 85 % of them off (DEFENCE_MAX, pestsLeft in applyZoneMix — irrigation still adds water; no more cooling of the leaves nor calming of the wind). The pests left add to the index (models/data vineThirstParts: animals × 0,2, plants × 0,25, then the phase weight), so the vineyard's 0–100 reading is now called "Stress delle viti" everywhere on screen ("Stress forte" / "Da tenere d'occhio" / "Le viti stanno bene", "Stress più basso", "stress su 100"), and the chips under the arc add "Parassiti animali +n" and "Parassiti vegetali +n". "Cosa ottieni" (simulation, dashboard and report) shows "Parassiti animali −n %" and "Parassiti vegetali −n %" instead of the leaves' °C and the wind. On the map (area-layout vineLayout) each irrigated zone has a main pipe (Line kind "main", along its western end, dark blue with a light core; 5 px in Google 3D) feeding a drip line on its rows (every row from +16 %), drawn over a faint wet band with drippers; the animal-pest defence lays traps on a regular grid (VineLayout.traps, one every ~250 m² at full defence: yellow dots in 2D, trapParts — a 1,4 m post with a yellow box — in 3D); the plant-pest defence covers that share of the zone's rows from the south as treated rows (Patch kind "treat": a light droplet pattern, SPRAY_IMAGE, in 2D; a see-through veil in Google 3D); the hedge, the shade net, their images, hedgeParts / netParts and the vineyard's "Sposta elementi" are gone. The drawn area's white outline has a dark casing under it (drawn-area-casing) so it reads on bright fields too. The profile "agronomo" (internal key unchanged) reads "Conduttore del vigneto" / "Conduttore" on screen (KIND_LABEL, the bar and menu badge, the choice "Sei il conduttore di un vigneto?", "Per i conduttori del vigneto", "Identificati come conduttore", "Profilo conduttore"); its form is unchanged (licence and region). "Invia all'agronomo" and the advice to ask an agronomist keep the word, as they mean an outside agronomist.
- Aiuole e ciottoli in 3D, drawn like the 2D pattern (supersedes every 3D look tried before: stripes, curbed beds, plain plane, rounded beds, squares): beds of grass run north–south, 1,2 m wide, with 0,4 m pebble paths between them (and on the patches' edges) made of stones only — no ground sheet under them, the grey one looked like a slab — round pebbles in white, sand, grey and brown in rows along each path (one row on a 0,4 m path, a new row every 0,25 m on wider ones, every 0,22 m along it on a small lawn, at most 140 in all), every stone and flower whole inside its piece; at most 50 beds and 36 flowers, so the 3D stays light; with the drawn area (LocationMap3D passes it) every bed and path piece is kept inside it: a piece reaching past the outline is cut into 0,5 m slices and only those wholly inside stay, so nothing spills out of the area; and piazzaLayout lays the rest of a lever's share around an Aiuole / Pergolato zone only when it is more than one lever step (10 % of the usable surface), so a lever raised in 10 % steps to fill a zone no longer spills strips past the zone's edge (two greens alternating), small flowers scattered on the grass (at most 60; pink, yellow, white, violet, orange) (models/area-layout bedParts, src/test/bed-parts.test.ts): the beds are the patches cut into bands by drape's clipBox (exported), so they follow the patches' shape, the pieces of row strips stacked over the same band joined into one bed; the band pitch grows while there would be more than 70 pieces; a fixed pseudo-random sequence, the same on every device; every part a thin slab (BED_H: pebbles 0,55–0,58 m, grass 0,60–0,65 m, flowers and stones 0,65–0,70 m — over the area's turquoise veil at 0,35 m, the shadows and the zones; the open 3D lowers them by 0,55 m), the grass with a darker green edge in Google 3D (BEDS_3D); in Google 3D they lie on Google's ground model like every other shape (laid on the photographed mesh they wrapped over the real trees). The 2D keeps its beds-and-pebbles pattern.
- Misters zone: the piazza's zones inside the area add "Nebulizzatori" (ZoneKind nebulizzatori, ZONE_KINDS piazza, ZONE_LABEL, ZONE_COLORS sky #7dd3fc, ZONE_HINT "I nebulizzatori vanno qui dentro: circa uno ogni 40 m²."): piazzaLayout puts every misting pole inside the misters zones (misterSpots over their cells, a zone smaller than a cell its middle; src/test/zone-levers.test.ts); PiazzaLevers carries misters, leversForZones raises the Nebulizzatori lever to about one pole every 40 m² of the zones (M2_PER_MISTER, 1–20) when such a zone appears (toast "Interventi messi nelle zone · … Nebulizzatori n"), leversOfZones sets it from the zones in "Solo quello disegnato" (a misters zone alone enables it too).
- 2026-11-06 (the handwritten list): the conductor's patentino is optional (checkAgronomo: an empty number asks no type nor expiry and saves licence "" and expires ""; given, it must read right with its type and a valid expiry; the form asks name, then Regione — where the vineyard is or the licence was issued —, then "Numero del patentino fitosanitario (facoltativo)", the type and "Scadenza del patentino" shown only once a number is written; proSummary "Conduttore del vigneto · Molise" without one; the ProfileCard reads "Patentino · Non indicato"; ProfileGate and /scegli say the patentino is optional). The piazza's "Importa dal meteo reale" also brings humidity, wind, the ground's temperature, the sun (kWh/m² a day, the last week's average) and the last week's rain (lab-store urban.realWeather RealWeather { at, humidity, wind, groundTemp, sun, rainWeek }; LabFlows importRealWeather from getRealClimate's openMeteo + nasa; the toast counts them), shown in the open "when" picker as small tiles "Meteo reale importato · gg/mm hh:mm" (UrbanThermometer RealWeatherTiles; informative, the simulation's reading is unchanged). The vineyard's arc reads the need of intervention (supersedes "Stress delle viti" on screen, the same 0–100 index): verdicts "Nessun intervento necessario" < 35 · "Intervento consigliato" < 65 · "Intervento urgente", the label under the number "Bisogno di intervento · −n rispetto a oggi / · a quest'ora non cambia / · nessun intervento fatto", the left label "Il minimo possibile", and the shared Gauge takes bands (VineThirst NEED_BANDS: green 0–35, amber 35–65, red 65–100): the fill is coloured by zone (need-scale gradient, edges placed along x) and a thin arc outside the track shows every zone in its colour; under the labels "Temperatura stagionale / Stima stagionale dell'area / del luogo n°" (the season's air at the area's middle, seasonAir); the dashboard, the report and thirstWords use the same words ("Bisogno di intervento", "bisogno di intervento su 100").
- 2D zoom (2026-11-06): zooming the 2D map never blows the simulation up — trees are 7 px circles and misting poles 6 px sky-blue dots (kind "mister" points; the 3,5 m mist veil only in the open 3D) at every zoom, like the traps (4 px), the drip lines' wet band a fixed 4 px; the 2D patterns (aiuole e ciottoli, pergola slats, vines, treated rows) are added at pixelRatio 2, so they stay fine and never grainy; areas, zones, aiuole, pergolas, shadows and vine zones still keep their real size on the ground.
- Misting poles counted on the maps (2026-11-06): piazzaLayout gives the zones drawn for misters about one pole every 40 m² each (at least one a zone) and places the rest of the lever itself as before (pergolas, paving), off those zones — PiazzaLayout.misters lists the zone ones first and mistersInZones says how many (src/test/zone-levers.test.ts). The 2D map numbers every pole 1…n with a round badge of fixed size (map-area misterElement: solid sky for the zones' poles, white with a sky ring for the app's), Google 3D the same numbered badge over each pole (a Marker3DElement with an SVG template, 3,4 m up, drawn when occluded), and over both maps, under the temperature pill, a status pill reads "Nebulizzatori n · k nelle tue zone · m messi dall'app"; the SimBar legend adds "(k nelle tue zone)".
- Moving the zones drawn by hand (2026-11-06): in "Sposta elementi" (offered in the vineyard too once it has zones) each zone drawn inside the area gets a draggable "✥ <name>" handle at its centre and, with two or more, a "✥ Tutte le zone" handle at their common centre (the plain zone tags hide while arranging); dropping one moves that zone — or all of them together — by the drag (MovedPart part "zones" → drawn-area moveZones: every ring shifted, the trees placed by hand inside a moved zone moving along; the trees, aiuole, pergolas and misting poles laid from the zones follow by themselves), with a toast "Zona spostata / Spostate n zone con quello che contengono"; a move that would take a zone past the drawn area's outline is refused ("Le zone devono restare dentro l'area disegnata") and the handle goes back (src/test/move-zones.test.ts).
- Aiuole follow the outline traced (2026-11-06, supersedes the north–south beds above and the 2D beds-and-pebbles pattern): bedParts works in a local metre frame turned onto the longest side of the largest lawn patch — a zone drawn by hand: its own main side; the automatic strips: their rows — so the 1,2 m grass beds and 0,4 m pebble paths run parallel to it and are cut exactly to every edge of the shape (bands across it clipped to the patches, row pieces joined, kept inside the drawn area, stones and flowers whole inside their piece; src/test/bed-parts.test.ts "follows a zone traced at an angle"). The 2D draws the very same beds as shapes (simFeatures kind "bed" on its own sim-beds layer over a gravel ground #b9ab8f in sim-lawn), no longer the fixed north–south LAWN_IMAGE pattern, so 2D and 3D match and stay crisp at every zoom.
- 2D aiuole keep their size on screen (2026-11-06): the 2D beds are laid again on every zoomend (SatelliteMap2D → setSimData with map-area bedWidthAt: about 12 px of bed in metres at the map's zoom and latitude), bedParts taking opts.bedM and scaling every measure (paths, pebbles, flowers, slices) to it, with caps of 900 pebbles and 200 flowers in 2D; zooming in or out shows more or fewer stripes of the same size, still following the traced zone's direction and cut to its outline; the 3D keeps the real 1,2 m beds.
- Aiuole run up and down the map (2026-11-06, refines "Aiuole follow the outline traced"): bedParts still squares the beds to the largest lawn patch's longest side, but of that side and the one square to it it takes the direction nearer north–south, so the grass beds stand vertical on the map (2D and 3D) while staying parallel to an edge of the shape traced and cut to it (src/test/bed-parts.test.ts).
- Aiuole in proportion (2026-11-06): in 2D the pebbles and flowers are drawn smaller against the beds (bedParts opts.detail 0,55: stone and flower radius, stone spacing and stone rows scaled by it, caps 2400 pebbles and 260 flowers), so the 12 px beds read as grass with fine gravel paths and small flowers at every zoom; the 3D keeps detail 1.
- Aiuole as sods (2026-11-06, supersedes the bed sizes above): the grass beds are cut across into sods (zolle) about 1,5 m long (bedParts, models/area-layout SOD: paths and the gaps between sods 0,25 m, pebbles 15–21 cm packed in rows every 0,22 m across and 0,19 m along, flowers about one every 10 m²), the sods growing longer only while there would be more than maxSods (3D 120, 2D 1500); the gaps between sods are pebble paths too; 3D up to 520 pebbles. The 2D beds are 10 px wide on screen and are laid again during the zoom (SatelliteMap2D: on zoom at most every 180 ms, and on zoomend), so the grass never swells under the fingers (2D detail 1, caps 6000 pebbles, 300 flowers).
- 2D aiuole on their own source (2026-11-06, refines the two bullets above): the sods live on a light GeoJSON source of their own (map-area SIM_BEDS, bedFeatures) — the sods as shapes, the pebbles and flowers as circle-layer dots with a radius in px (sim-pebbles: pebbles ≥ 1 px, flowers ≥ 1,6 px) — and SatelliteMap2D lays only that source again once per animation frame during the zoom and at zoomend (setBedData), so the grass, pebbles and flowers keep the same size on screen before, during and after a pinch (fill-pattern images were measured growing up to 2× between whole zoom levels, so they are not used for the aiuole).
- 3D sods barely parted (2026-11-06): in 3D (Google and the open 3D) the gaps between sods and between rows are a 5 cm hairline (bedParts opts.gap, default SOD.gap3d 0,05 m — too thin for pebbles, so the 3D aiuole are sods of two greens with flowers, barely parted), while the 2D keeps 0,25 m pebble paths (bedFeatures passes gap SOD.path).
- Map preview above the arc (2026-11-06): the Simula tab of both simulations opens with a small still map (SimShell renders SimMap preview while Simula is open, so there is never a second map on another tab): 34vh (min 14 rem), 2D only, framed on the drawn area (SatelliteMap2D still: fitBounds with padding, no corner pins, no recenter button), the simulation drawn in it live with the reading pill and day/night, no search, drawing nor gestures (the map takes no pointer, the page scrolls over it); the whole preview is a button "Apri la mappa" (a glass chip bottom right) that opens the Mappa tab (openSimTab); the zone seeding effect does not run in the preview. Easy to drop: remove `{tab === "simula" && <SimMap kind={kind} preview />}` in SimShell.
- Every 2D texture a fixed size on screen (2026-11-06, supersedes the 2D pattern images): on the 2D map the pergola slats (dark 1 px gaps every 4 px, north–south, over a plain wood fill), the vine rows (a 2 px green row every 5 px, east–west, over each zone's soil colour VINE_SOIL by stress step) and the treated rows' droplets (a circle every 6 px, rows staggered) are shapes and dots on the aiuole's own source (map-area bedFeatures with strips / dotGrid, anchored on a world grid so neighbouring strips line up, thinned past a cap), laid again every frame of a zoom like the sods; trees, misters, traps, lines and badges were already fixed in px. Only the ground surfaces themselves (the area, the zones, the aiuole, pergola and vine footprints, the shadows) grow with the zoom, as they are the real m² on the map. The open 3D keeps its image patterns.
- Misting poles in Google 3D (2026-11-06): their number badges live in a pool (LocationMap3D misterBadges, one Marker3DElement per pole index and kind, kept for the map's life): a Marker3DElement removed from the map kept being drawn, so poles taken away stayed on the 3D view; unused badges are now sent 500 m under the ground with drawsWhenOccluded off, and the pool is dropped with the map. Each pole is drawn as a slim steel pole (3 m) with a white ring head edged sky, the mist falling from it as three see-through discs widening downwards (0,9 / 1,7 / 2,5 m round at 2,6 / 1,9 / 1,1 m) over a soft ground veil; the badge sits at 3,9 m. The open 3D adds the ring head too.
- Closer 2D zoom (2026-11-06): the 2D map zooms to 23 with the browser key (22 without, the open imagery overscaled from 19); Google's satellite base follows up to GOOGLE_MAX 21 (SatelliteMap2D) and past it is enlarged on screen around the centre (CSS scale 2^(z+1−21) on its layer), so one can zoom in further with the drawing still on the photo; the fixed-size textures stay fixed until their caps make them grow.
- Drawing position in 3D by hand (2026-11-06, replaces the tap-to-align): Google's ground model can lie under the photographed piazza (shapes laid RELATIVE_TO_GROUND slide when the view tilts) and its mesh can sit a little aside of the map. In Google 3D, with an area drawn, a top-left round button (Move icon only, a small turquoise dot once changed; aria "Regola posizione del disegno") opens a small glass panel: Altezza − / value / + (0,5 m steps, −5…+20 m), a cross of arrows moving the whole drawing north / west / east / south by 0,5 m (±30 m, "Nord … · Est …" under it), "Rimetti com'era" and ✕; build3DOverlay shifts every ring and line (rawPoly / absLine) and misterBadges their badges; kept per place on this device (localStorage mdt-3d-adjust-v1 {h, n, e}, key lat,lng to 4 decimals; an older mdt-3d-lift-v1 height is read as h).
- Parallax in the map preview (2026-11-06): the Simula preview's map lies on a ParallaxLayer (SimMap) 50 % taller than its box that slides by 55 % of the page's scroll the other way, at most 25 % each side (transform once per frame on scroll and resize, read from the box's position against the screen's middle), so the map seems deeper than the page; the slide goes on while the preview goes up under the sticky tabs (SimShell's [data-sim-tabs]) and stops once the start of its faded foot (FADE_AT 0,72 of its height, where the mask begins) reaches them, so from there only the fade is left and the map scrolls away with the page; the reading pill, day/night and "Apri la mappa" stay put; none with reduced motion.
- Light theme fixes (2026-11-06): the arc's end labels (piazza "Il più fresco" / "Media stagionale …", vineyard "Il minimo possibile" / "Senza interventi") read in light too — sky-700/800 on a sky tint, amber-900 on an amber tint, the plain one foreground/75, dimmed to 50 % (35 % in dark); the Simula map preview's foot fades out by a mask (the map and its night veil under a linear-gradient mask, opaque to 72 % then transparent; the button has no background of its own), so the page itself — its ambient light, light or dark — shows through; a gradient to bg-background left a band of another blue under the map.
- /scegli as cards (2026-11-06, supersedes the numbered 01/02/03 list and its white button): under "Da dove vuoi iniziare?" the choice shows the sector page's cards — Settore urbano ("Per i funzionari del Comune", "Si apre la piazza: …") and Settore agricolo ("Per i conduttori del vigneto", "Si apre il vigneto: …") — each a tap that shows its IdentifyForm in place (or enters at once when already that profile, tagged "Il tuo profilo" with "Entra nella piazza / nel vigneto: …"), then the Fotocamera AI card (no "for whom" line): while AI_STANDBY it is not a button (aria-disabled, dimmed, no arrow) with an amber "In sviluppo" tag and "Non è ancora disponibile: arriverà con un prossimo aggiornamento."; "Salta, vai al benvenuto" stays.
- 3D fallback said (2026-11-06): when the photographic 3D is refused (gm_authFailure: key, quota or billing) LocationMap3D still shows OpenMap3D, now with a glass line at the bottom "Vista 3D fotografica non disponibile ora: mostro la mappa semplificata" (role status), instead of switching map silently.
- Google account space in the 50 GB (2026-11-06; the drive.file scope is NOT asked at sign-in for now: with it Google blocked the sign-in, "Accesso bloccato … non ha completato la procedura di verifica", 403 access_denied, as the OAuth app is in Testing — to turn it back on add `scopes: GOOGLE_USAGE_SCOPE` to signInWithOAuth once the consent screen is published or the users are listed as testers, with the Drive API enabled): models/google-usage GOOGLE_USAGE_SCOPE, and lib/google-usage-sync (installed by the root) reads, whenever a Google session carries its provider_token (right after the sign-in), Drive's about.storageQuota.usage — the whole Google account's used space (mail, files, photos) —, kept per user on this device (localStorage mdt-google-usage-v1 {userId, bytes, at}) and dropped on sign-out.
