It counts in the cloud space: lab-media-store cloudUsed (so the 50 GB limit on uploads) and useCloudUsage (used = media + google), and the account panel's bar adds "Comprende … già usati nel tuo account Google (posta, file e foto)." Needs the Google Drive API enabled in the OAuth client's Cloud project and the scope on its consent screen; accounts signed in before must sign in again to be read; email/password accounts have none.
- Day and night in the photographic 3D (2026-11-07): Google's 3D draws itself above the DayNight veil, so in 3D SimMap's MapLight sets --map-light-filter from the sun's height (lightFilter: none by day ≥ 10°; towards dusk a warm sepia, up to 0,35, with a little more saturation and 12 % less light; twilight to −6° down to brightness 0,5, less saturated and a little bluer; night brightness 0,45, saturate 0,65, hue-rotate 15°) and LocationMap3D's map host applies it (CSS filter, 700 ms transition, none with reduced motion), so the 3D map darkens and warms with the simulation's season and hour like the 2D.
- 3D shadows over the aiuole (2026-11-07): in Google 3D the shadows of pergolas and trees are laid at 0,74 m (rgba 11,15,21 at 40 %), over the sods (0,60–0,70 m), which hid them at 0,25 m; with the sun under the horizon (night) there are no shadows at all, as the SimBar says.
- Glass on iPhones (2026-11-07): iOS Safari before 18 reads only -webkit-backdrop-filter, and the build emits only the unprefixed one, so every backdrop-filter in styles.css now carries its -webkit- twin and a closing rule gives the Tailwind backdrop utilities ([class*="backdrop-"]) the prefixed property from the same --tw-backdrop-* variables; the see-through blurred bars, pills, sheets, toasts and the camera's glass now show on iPhones as on Android. Write both when adding a new one.
- Interventions keep off what already stands there (2026-11-07): for the piazza, once an area is drawn, the phone reads from the open map data what stands in it (models/real-objects realObjectsQuery: existing trees and tree rows, fountains and water, buildings and kiosks, monuments and memorials, in the area's box; realObjectsFrom keeps those touching the area, points as circles — tree 3 m, fountain 3,5 m, monument 2 m, a tree row a crown every 5 m —; lib/real-objects-client loadRealObjects asks OVERPASS_URLS in turn, 12 s each; SimMap's query keyed by the ring, kept for the session) and piazzaLayout takes their rings as `real`: no new tree, bed, pergola nor misting pole goes on them (cells and tree spots there are skipped, a tree moved by hand onto one goes back), while the surface the costs count is unchanged (src/test/real-objects.test.ts). The 2D map outlines them with a thin white dashed line (kind "realline"), and the SimBar says "Già nell'area: 3 alberi · 1 fontana. Gli interventi non ci vanno sopra (contorni tratteggiati sulla mappa 2D)." The zones drawn by hand still go where they were drawn.
- Bottom bar out of the way (2026-11-07): on phones the bar still slides down while scrolling down and back on scrolling up, and now also a still tap (touch or pen, under 10 px and 500 ms) on an empty part of the page lowers it exactly like the scroll does (translate-y calc(100% − 3px), for both: the raised camera's dome peeks out with the screen's edge through the middle of its yellow "in development" dot), and another tap brings it back (__root BottomNav, emptyTap: never on links, buttons, fields, sliders, tabs, maps [data-map-gestures], dialogs, the bar itself, anything with a pointer cursor nor while the in-app camera is open; [data-no-nav-tap] opts an element out); a new page always starts with the bar up.
- "Un profilo alla volta" dropped (2026-11-07): no screen says it any more — the sector forms (Identify KINDS intro, also on the sector gate and /scegli), /scegli's line, the profile page without a profile and the locked section's note (ProfileGate: only "Ora sei identificato come …").
- Guest access (2026-11-07): one can enter without a professional profile. The sector page ("Da dove vuoi usarmi?") has "Entra come ospite · Prova la piazza e il vigneto senza identificarti." under "Hai già un account? Accedi", and /scegli an "Entra come ospite" link (to /benvenuto) above "Salta" when no profile is in; lib/use-pro-id enterAsGuest / useGuest / isGuest / leaveGuest (localStorage mdt-guest-v1; saving a profile ends it). A guest with no profile has both simulations open (useSectionOpen and SectionGate count it, so the Benvenuto tiles, the dashboard tabs, the bottom bar and the desktop pills show no locks) and the camera sheet shoots for the simulation in view (the piazza elsewhere, "Foto e video della piazza / del vigneto"); the sector page no longer covers the pages for a guest, but still opens when asked by name (the account panel's identification rows, leaving a profile or the account). "Un settore alla volta" is no longer said on the sector page.
- Guest limits (2026-11-07, refines "Guest access"): a guest uses the app as it is built but not all of it. Open: both simulations (levers, arc, map, place, drawn area and zones), photos and videos, the dashboard. Locked (views/v10/ProfileGate useGuestOnly — guest and no profile — and GuestLock: a lock, the title, "non per gli ospiti", one line and "Identificati come funzionario" / "Come conduttore", which open the sector page on that form): the history is not recorded (lib/use-history addHistory returns at once for a guest, so the auto-history, the Registro and the Quaderno save nothing), Sensori AI (LabFlows GuestSensors), Invia il progetto (Dashboard SendProject) and Esporta report (hidden from the dashboard's hero). The guest has a badge: in the bottom bar the first column (Benvenuto/Cronologia) becomes "Ospite" (UserRound) to /profilo, as does the first desktop pill; /profilo shows GuestCard ("Sei entrato come ospite · Né funzionario né conduttore: usi l'app, ma non tutta.", "Puoi usare" with green checks, "Solo dopo esserti identificato" with locks, "Identificati per avere tutto" with the two profiles opening the sector page's form, and "Esci da ospite", which ends guest mode and shows the sector page).
- Vineyard levers back (2026-11-07, supersedes "Vineyard levers (2026-11-05)" and the traps / treated rows / pests written after it): the vineyard's interventions are again Irrigazione, "Teli d'ombra" and "Frangivento" (VineMix { irrigation, shade, windbreak }, VINE_LEVELS 4/0/20, 8/30/40, 16/80/70, applyZoneMix + moisture, − up to 3 °C on the leaves, − up to 60 % wind; costs per hectare drip 3.000–6.000 €, shade cloth 4.000–8.000 €, windbreak 6.000–12.000 €); no pests anywhere — no "Quanti parassiti ci sono" levers, no pest chips under the arc, no pests in the index (vineThirstParts soil, heat, wind, phase, rain), "Cosa ottieni" (simulation, dashboard, report) back to "Sulle foglie −n °C" and "Vento −n %", mixRows / history / summaries / FAQ / Chi siamo / sector cards ("acqua, ombra e frangivento contro la sete delle viti") with shade cloth and windbreak. Kept from later work: the "Bisogno di intervento" words and green/amber/red bands of the arc, the main pipe feeding the drip lines (Line kind "main"), the cased area outline, the "Conduttore" profile name. On the map: the shade cloth from the north of each zone (Patch kind "cloth": in 2D a dark veil with its threads as 1 px lines every 4 px both ways on the fixed-size source, netline; NET_IMAGE in the open 3D; netParts in 3D), the windbreak hedge along the edge nearest its "Frangivento" handle (Line kind "wind", windAt; in 2D a fixed 9 px dark-green line with round lighter bushes, sim-hedge / sim-hedge-bush; hedgeParts in 3D), and the vineyard's "Sposta elementi" moves the Frangivento handle (plus the zones' ✥ handles). History entries saved with pest defences no longer reopen (their mix fails isHistoryMix).
- Simulation tabs order (2026-11-07): Mappa · Simula · Sensori AI (SimShell TABS; the swipe follows the same order), since a simulation opens on Mappa and the work goes place → levers → sensors; every other mention of "Simula · Mappa · Sensori AI" above now reads in this order.
- Profile back on sign-in (2026-11-07): signing in to an account whose profile in use is empty but that registered one before brings it back by itself (lib/use-pro-id useSyncProId adopt: the account's pro_id first; else the device's profile, uploaded; else, unless the account says pro_left — set only by leaving the profile on purpose, saveProId(null) —, the most recent of the account's registered profiles, pro_last, put in use and saved as pro_id with a toast "Bentornato, <name> · Sei entrato con il tuo profilo professionale."), so a conductor who signs in is a conductor again with no form; a guest mode on the device ends with it.
- Vines in relief in 3D (2026-11-07): the vineyard's zones carry rows of vines standing on the ground (models/area-layout vineRowParts, VINE_ROW: east–west rows 2,5 m apart — wider while there would be more than max —, 0,8 m wide, 1,6 m tall, 0,4 m off their strip's ends): in the open 3D 400 at most, each a dark foot to 0,7 m, the leafy wall and a lighter top (map-area treeCrowns); in Google 3D 160 at most, one extruded green wall each with a darker edge, following the ground (LocationMap3D, lite); src/test/vine-rows.test.ts.
- Agricultural sector wording at the entrance (2026-11-07): the Settore agricolo card on /scegli and on the sector page reads "Per chi lavora nel settore agricolo" (no "conduttori"), its button "Identificati per il settore agricolo"; the profile elsewhere is still "Conduttore".
- "Conduttore" dropped (2026-11-07): the vineyard profile (internal key "agronomo") reads "Agricoltore" everywhere on screen — KIND_LABEL, proSummary ("Agricoltore · Molise"), the bottom bar badge, the desktop pill "Profilo agricoltore", the identification form title and page title, "Per chi lavora nel settore agricolo" on Benvenuto, the camera sheet and the locked section, "Agricoltore · apre il vigneto" in the account panel, "Come agricoltore" on the guest locks and "Né funzionario né agricoltore" on the guest profile.
- three.js in the open 3D (2026-11-07): OpenMap3D (the 3D shown without the photographic one, also its fallback) draws the simulation's objects with three.js inside the maplibre map (views/v10/three-layer simThreeLayer: a custom "3d" layer sharing the map's WebGL context and depth, local frame in metres from the place, x east · y up · z south, camera = the map's mainMatrix × the model matrix): vines plant by plant along their rows (vineRowSegments, a plant every ~1,1 m, at most 24.000, instanced: a trunk, an icosahedron of leaves touching its neighbours so the row reads as a green wall, each its own green, a post every ~6 m and two wires), trees as a trunk and three cones of their own green, misting poles with see-through mist discs, and pergolas, beds, hedge and shade net from the same parts as the other 3D views merged per colour (MeshStandardMaterial); lit by the simulation's sun (useSimWhen → sunAt: direction, warmer and weaker when low, soft PCF shadows on a ShadowMaterial ground; at night a cool faint light, no shadows). The map is built once per place; levers and clock update the three.js scene and the ground layers without rebuilding it (the old extruded slabs, SIM_TREES_3D, are emptied while three.js runs and come back if it fails to load).
- Account profiles kept apart from the device (2026-11-07, refines "Profile back on sign-in"): on sign-in the account's pro_id wins; else, unless pro_left, the most recent of the account's own pro_last (never the profiles this device knew before — a guest's try or someone else's — which made a vineyard account come back as a municipal officer); only an account with no profile of its own takes the device's. saveProId / keepProIdInAccount write pro_last as the account's own plus the profile in use (accountLast), no longer the device's whole list.
- Wrong profile repaired (2026-11-07): once per account (user data pro_checked), when the profile in use is of the kind the account never worked with — its intervention_history all vineyard and it is a municipal officer, or the reverse — lib/use-pro-id checkKind puts back the right kind (the account's own registered one, else this device's) with a toast "Ho rimesso il tuo profilo: <name> · Il tuo account ha sempre lavorato sul vigneto / sulla piazza."; with none known it drops the wrong profile from the account (pro_id and pro_last) and opens the sector page on the right form. Mixed or empty history: nothing changes.
- Vineyard scope on the map preview (2026-11-07): the "Tutte · Zona 1 · Zona 2 · Zona 3" row left the card above the arc (it pushed the arc down, so the preview had scrolled away by the time the arc showed) and now rides over the Simula map preview as one line of see-through pills under the reading pill (black 20 %, a 2 px blur, a faint rim, shadowed text, the chosen one white 80 %) (VineThirst VineZonePicker: each with its need of intervention and its colour dot, the chosen one white; a tap chooses the scope without opening the map). The preview is a clickable box (cursor pointer, a tap anywhere opens Mappa) with "Apri la mappa" its own button, so the pills are not buttons inside a button.
- Vineyard main pipe inside the area (2026-11-07): the main pipe no longer runs straight along the zone's westernmost x (which, with a slanted or curved outline, left the area): it is a polyline through the west end of every row of its zone, so it follows the zone's edge and bends with a curved outline, always inside (area-layout vineLayout; src/test/vine-main-pipe.test.ts).
- 2D map opens on the area (2026-11-07): with an area drawn, the Mappa tab's 2D map opens framed on it with room around (SatelliteMap2D frameArea: fitBounds with padding 26 % of the box's width on each side, 24 % on top and 20 % below, so the area takes about half the width and what lies beside it shows too), once per opening and not while drawing; the recenter button comes back to that framing (the place's point and zoom without an area).
- No cost in the vineyard simulation (2026-11-07): VineThirst lost its folded "Dettagli costo e benefici" card (euro per hectare); the dashboard and the report keep their indicative costs. In its place, under the levers: the three facts (Zone al sicuro, Sulle foglie, Vento) always shown; "Cosa fare adesso" (models/vine-advice vineAdvice: how the vineyard is and where to start — "Comincia dalla Zona n …" or "Il vigneto sta bene adesso (n/100) …" —, then each action once for all the zones that need it, most urgent first — dry soil under 25 % with little irrigation, heat from 32 °C with little shade cloth, wind from 15 km/h with little windbreak, rain: stop watering —, then one line for the vine's phase; a red / amber / green dot each); "Acqua necessaria" (vineWater: what the vines drink a day — 0,13 mm per °C over 6 °C, at most 7 mm, times the phase's coefficient 0,35/0,55/0,7/0,6, none on a rainy day —, a week, what brings the soil back to 30 % over the roots' first 50 cm — 5 l/m² a point —, per zone on a third of the surface, and what the irrigation set gives; waterText in l or m³); "Prossimi 7 giorni" (loadForecast: the place's daily highest temperature, strongest wind and rain from the open weather forecast, kept an hour; forecastNeed: each day's need of intervention with the interventions set, every zone keeping its soil and its difference in air; a sideways row of day cards with °, rain or wind and the need in its colour; "Previsioni non disponibili ora." without an answer). src/test/vine-advice.test.ts.
- Vineyard advice as an accordion (2026-11-07, refines "No cost in the vineyard simulation"): "Cosa fare adesso", "Acqua necessaria" and "Prossimi 7 giorni" are the three folds of one card (VineThirst VineAccordion / AccItem: a header with icon, title, a one-line gist — "n cose da fare · <the first one>", "… al giorno · … per il terreno", "Il giorno più critico: ven, 36° · 61/100" — and a chevron, aria-expanded; one open at a time, the advice open first, a tap on the open one closes it).
- Only the account's own profiles (2026-11-07, supersedes "Profile back on sign-in", "Account profiles kept apart from the device" and "Wrong profile repaired"): a profile is the account's own only when it was identified or picked again ("Continua come …") while signed in to it — saveProId / keepProIdInAccount add its kind to the user data pro_own. On sign-in (lib/use-pro-id useSyncProId adopt, once per user per load) only those kinds count: the account's pro_id if its kind is owned, else (unless pro_left) the most recent owned pro_last with "Bentornato"; never the device's profile. Accounts saved before pro_own get their owned kinds once from their history (legacyOwn: vineyard entries → agronomo, piazza → funzionario; none → none). An account owning nothing ends with no profile on the device, pro_id null and unowned pro_last dropped from its data, a toast "Questo account non ha ancora un profilo professionale · Scegli il settore e identificati, oppure entra come ospite." when one was removed, and the sector page opens on the two sectors with "Entra come ospite" (not for a guest, nor after leaving the profile on purpose). checkKind / pro_checked are gone.
- Account picture on "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" (2026-11-07): the sector page's recognised-profile card shows the personal account's picture (the one in the header: chosen, uploaded or Google's) in a 56 px round frame beside the name; account-control publishes it to lib/account-avatar (setAccountAvatar / useAccountAvatar, null signed out), with a person icon when there is none.
- GPS on from the start (2026-11-07, refines the map's GPS button): mdt-gps-v1 is on unless it reads "off" (turned off by the button or the Autorizzazioni switch, or a refused permission / no geolocation), so the Mappa tab's GPS starts by itself on every opening (asking the position the first time) and moves the simulation's place to where the phone is; each opening also clears the pause left by a place chosen by hand (mdt-gps-paused-v1), which now lasts only until the map is opened again. Revoked in the app, it stays off.
- No default place, GPS icon (2026-11-07): the simulations no longer start on a default place (the Campobasso piazza, the Molise hills): models/lab-store's defaults carry location { label "Nessun luogo scelto", pending: true } (the coordinates kept only for the numbers), and a device whose saved place is still one of those old defaults with no area drawn there is moved to it on load (dropDefault). While pending the Mappa tab shows the place search over a card "Nessun luogo ancora · Tocca il GPS per usare la tua posizione, oppure cerca la piazza / il vigneto qui sopra" instead of a map, and the Simula preview a card "Apri la mappa: il GPS trova dove sei, oppure cerca un luogo." that opens it; the GPS's first fix sets the place however near (no 100 m step while pending), and place-memory keeps nothing for a pending place. The GPS button's icon is a navigation arrow (lucide Navigation, filled while following).
- GPS icon and alert (2026-11-07, supersedes the navigation arrow of "No default place, GPS icon"): the GPS button shows the crosshair (LocateFixed) while off, a satellite (lucide Satellite) while on — following or paused —, the spinner while locating; the first position found after each switch-on (also the automatic one when the map opens) shows a toast "GPS attivo · La mappa segue la tua posizione." (or, paused, "Hai scelto tu la zona: il GPS resta acceso ma non la sposta.").
- GPS keeps a place being designed (2026-11-07, refines "GPS on from the start"): opening the map switches the GPS on but no longer moves a place that has an area drawn for it (PlaceSearch keepPlace = drawnAreaFor(area, location)): the GPS starts paused there ("Hai scelto tu la zona…", "Seguimi di nuovo" to follow), so the simulation is never pulled away from the piazza or vineyard being designed (it had moved it to the phone's position, another place, whose own interventions showed instead — e.g. aiuole and pergolati 0 m²); with no place yet, or a place without an area, it follows the phone as before.
- Sticky tabs out of the way (2026-11-07): the simulations' sticky Mappa · Simula · Sensori AI bar hides while scrolling down past 160 px (SimShell useHideOnScroll: slides up and fades, pointer-events none, data-hidden) and comes back on scrolling up, like the bottom bar, giving the map preview and the simulation the whole top of the screen; the compact reading pill under it follows through --sim-tabs-gap on the document (3.75rem shown, 0.75rem hidden), and the preview's parallax reads the screen's top edge while the tabs are hidden.
- Map fixes (2026-11-07): the sticky tabs stay until half the map box (the Mappa tab's map or the Simula preview, [data-sim-map-box]) has gone up past them, then hide while scrolling down (refines "Sticky tabs out of the way"). The 2D Google base never goes past the closest zoom with photos at the place (SatelliteMap2D: google.maps.MaxZoomService asked once per map, GOOGLE_SAFE 19 until it answers, GOOGLE_MAX 21 at most; past it the base is enlarged on screen), since outside town centres Google's grey "no imagery" tiles left the map empty under the night veil. The GPS puts a coarse first fix right: a fix at least twice as precise as the one the place came from moves it even within the 100 m step (when it is more than its accuracy and 15 m away).
- Sticky tabs slide like the bottom bar (2026-11-07): hiding, the tabs bar only slides up out of the screen (transform, 300 ms, the same transition as the bottom bar sliding down; no fade) and slides back down when it returns.
- Sticky tabs slide smoothly (2026-11-07): the hide/show no longer goes through React state (a re-render of the whole simulation page at every switch made the slide stutter): SimShell's useHideOnScroll sets data-hidden on the bar and --sim-tabs-shift on the page straight on the DOM; the bar slides with a transform only (will-change transform, data-[hidden] classes), the compact reading pill follows with a transform too (translate-y var(--sim-tabs-shift), no more transition on top), and the preview's parallax reads where the tabs end when shown (their sticky top plus height), not their live position, so the map no longer jumps when they hide or return.
- Sticky tabs follow the scroll (2026-11-07, supersedes the slide of "Sticky tabs slide like the bottom bar" and "Sticky tabs slide smoothly"): once half the map box has gone up past it, the tabs bar moves up by exactly as much as the page scrolls down until it is out of the screen, and comes down by as much as it scrolls up (SimShell useHideOnScroll: an offset 0…its height applied as translate3d once per frame, no transition, no React state; data-hidden once fully out); the compact reading pill follows by 80 % of it (--sim-tabs-shift in px).
- Place as the simulation's title (2026-11-07): the simulation pages are titled with the place chosen instead of "Piazza" / "Vigneto" (SimShell: PageHero small — text-2xl, 3xl from sm, up to two lines), the small line above reading "Piazza · <rest of the address>" / "Vigneto · …" (tap opens Mappa); a label starting "La mia posizione" shows its street or town; the GPS's reverse lookup now names the place by its street (else town) instead of "La mia posizione" (controllers/geo reverseGoogleLocation).
- Sticky tabs direction inverted (2026-11-07, as asked; refines "Sticky tabs follow the scroll"): past half the map box, scrolling up (back towards the top) lifts the tabs bar out of the screen by as much as the page moved, and scrolling down lowers it back; at the very top of the page it is always shown.
- Simulation title back (2026-11-07, undoes "Place as the simulation's title"): the pages read "Piazza" / "Vigneto" big again with the place's name in the small line above (tap opens Mappa); kept: a "La mia posizione · …" label shows its street or town there, and the GPS's reverse lookup names the place by its street (else town).
- Sticky tabs only move once stuck (2026-11-07, fixes the lifted bar over the title): the scroll-linked shift applies only while the tabs bar is stuck at the top of the screen (its unshifted top at its sticky top); near the top of the page, where it is still in the flow under the title, the shift goes back to 0, so it never covers "Piazza" nor leaves a gap above the map preview. The preview's parallax reads the tabs' unshifted bottom (rect bottom minus their transform).
- Municipal officer's email (2026-11-07): the funzionario form takes any email, personal ones too ("La tua email", isEmail; the town-email check isTownEmail stays in the model but no longer blocks), its hint showing the office email the card will carry once Comune and office are picked; the profile card (ProfileCard profileRows) shows as "Email" the chosen office's email at its town — models/professional-id officeEmail(office, comune): the office's name before " e ", without accents or spaces, at comune.<town>.it ("ambiente@comune.campobasso.it", "ufficiotecnico@…"; built from the usual pattern, not looked up) — as a mailto link, and the person's own email under it as "Email personale" when different. The sector card, the locked section and the profile page say "la tua email" instead of "l'email del Comune".
- Emails as plain text (2026-11-07, supersedes the mailto link of the profile card's email): the profile card's "Email" and "Email personale" are plain text, no link (Identify ProfileCard).
- Office email from the official register (2026-11-07, refines "Municipal officer's email"): the profile card's "Email dell'ufficio" is the official address of the chosen office from the public administrations' register (IPA, AgID open data, CC BY 4.0, credited in Licenze): controllers/ipa.functions getOfficeEmail finds the register's CKAN datasets by name (package_search "enti" / "unita organizzative", their datastore resource), the town's entry ("Comune di <name>", by ISTAT code when known) and its code, then its offices and addresses (fields read loosely: descrizione, mail1…5, tipo_mail), and models/office-email pickOfficeMail keeps the office sharing at least half of the chosen office's words (5-letter stems count, "ufficio/settore/servizio…" ignored), its ordinary address before the PEC (src/test/office-email.test.ts); lib/office-email useOfficeEmail asks once per town and office and keeps the answer on the device (localStorage mdt-office-mail-v1, a register that could not be reached is not kept). Without an official match the card shows the usual pattern as "Email dell'ufficio (indicativa)". The register could not be reached from the development container, so the dataset lookup is untested against the live service.
- Piazza history by office (2026-11-07): every piazza intervention recorded by a municipal officer keeps who recorded it (models/intervention-history HistoryBy { comune, office, role }, entry.by, a copy in mix.by for an account table without its column; byOf reads either; supabase/migrations/20261107000000_intervention_history_by.sql adds the optional "by" jsonb column, applied by hand — saveRows and the account read fall back without it). lib/use-history useHistory shows a municipal officer only the piazza entries of the office in use (ofOffice: same town and office; entries recorded before the office was kept count as its own), unless the scope is "all" (useOfficeScope / setOfficeScope, localStorage mdt-history-office-v1); HistoryBlock shows "Il mio ufficio · <office> | Tutti gli uffici · +n" pills (OfficeScopePills) when other offices' entries exist or "all" is chosen, every row of an entry with an office reads "<office> · <role> · Comune di <town>", the detail page adds "Registrato da" (it reads every office: useHistory({ allOffices: true })), and "Cancella cronologia" removes only the shown entries while other offices' are hidden. src/test/history-office.test.ts.
- Office filter always shown (2026-11-07, refines "Piazza history by office"): for a municipal officer the "Il mio ufficio · <office> | Tutti gli uffici" pills sit at the top of every piazza history (Benvenuto, dashboard, Cronologia) even when no other office has entries ("+n" only when some do); the row's office · role · town line wraps instead of being cut.
- Choose any office in the history (2026-11-07, refines "Office filter always shown"): the office pills read "Il mio ufficio · <office> · n", "Tutti gli uffici · n", then every office of the list (OFFICES but Altro) and any other office that recorded something, each with its count — one swipeable row ([data-map-gestures], touch-pan-x) with the chosen pill scrolled into view; a chosen office shows only the piazza interventions it recorded (OfficeScope "office:<name>", kept in mdt-history-office-v1; useHistory returns offices, the count per office, entries recorded before the office was kept counting as the office in use).
- Archive folders open in place (2026-11-07): in both archives (interventions' HistoryArchiveBlock and photos' ArchiveBlock) an opened day folder unfolds right under its own folder row (ArchivedDay / OpenPile inside the list, after its button) instead of at the end of the whole list.
- Offices of urban planning only (2026-11-07, supersedes the longer OFFICES list): the municipal officer's form (and the history's office filter) lists only the offices dealing with the town's spaces — Urbanistica, Ufficio tecnico, Lavori pubblici, Edilizia privata, Ambiente e verde pubblico, Mobilità e traffico, Patrimonio; any other is still written by name (officeOf → "Altro" + its name), and profiles already saved with a removed office keep it.
- Offices list shorter, counts in badges (2026-11-07): OFFICES is Urbanistica, Ufficio tecnico, Lavori pubblici, Edilizia privata, Ambiente e verde pubblico (Mobilità e traffico and Patrimonio dropped; any other written by name). The history's office pills show their count in a round badge of its own (h-6, min-w-6): turquoise on the chosen pill, a turquoise tint with a ring when there are interventions, grey at 0; the pill's label for screen readers says "<office>: n interventi".
- Connected devices (2026-11-07): the account panel (signed in for real, under the cloud bar) has "Dispositivi collegati" (views/v10/AccountDevices): every phone and computer signed in to the account, seen only by its owner — table account_devices from supabase/migrations/20261107010000_account_devices.sql (applied by hand: device_id, label, first_seen, last_seen, unique per user, owner-only RLS) —, each with an icon, its plain label (models/device-label deviceLabel: the phone model when Chrome gives it, the system and the browser or "app installata"; src/test/device-label.test.ts), "Ultimo uso …" (adesso, n min fa, oggi/ieri hh:mm, a date) and the first sign-in, this one tagged "Questo dispositivo"; the others have an ✕ that takes them off the list (said: a device still signed in comes back at its next use); "Esci da tutti i dispositivi" asks in place, then keeps the profile in the account and signs out every session (supabase signOut scope global), this one too. lib/account-devices: this device's own id in localStorage mdt-device-id-v1, registerDevice upserts it on opening, on SIGNED_IN and on return to the app (at most every 10 minutes; the root's useRegisterDevice); without the table the card says "L'elenco non è ancora attivo." and nothing else breaks.
- Plans and info on their own page (2026-11-07, refines the v10 account panel's grouped rows): the panel's list no longer carries the groups Piani · Progetto · Aiuto · Privacy · Legale; one row "Piani e informazioni · Piano Free/Premium · Chi siamo · Aiuto · Privacy · Legale" opens them as a page inside the panel (account-control InfoMenu on InfoPage, page "menu"), and a page opened from it goes back to it (back arrow, swipe right, the phone's Back), then back to the panel's list. The app version, "Cerca aggiornamenti" and Twin stay at the foot of the panel, untouched.
- Guest locks per section (2026-11-07, refines "Guest limits"): GuestLock takes the section's profile (kind): in the piazza (its Sensori AI, the dashboard's Piazza "Invia il progetto") it offers only the white "Identificati come funzionario", in the vineyard only "Identificati come agricoltore"; without a kind both stay.
- 2D never empty (2026-11-07, refines "Map fixes"): with Google's base the open satellite imagery and names stay visible until Google's map fires its first "tilesloaded" (and the style is loaded), only then hidden; a Google map that draws nothing — a refused or blocked service, a place without photos — leaves the open imagery on instead of an empty dark map (seen on the vineyard's GPS place).
- Vineyard zones with an intervention (2026-11-07): "Aggiungi zona" in the vineyard offers, besides Da lasciare libera and Lotto, Irrigazione (sky #38bdf8), Teli d'ombra (slate #94a3b8) and Frangivento (green #22c55e) (drawn-area ZoneKind irrigazione | teli | frangivento, ZONE_KINDS vigneto, VINE_ZONE_KINDS, ZONE_HINT): area-layout vineLayout lays drip lines on every row of an Irrigazione zone with a main pipe through the rows' west ends, shade cloth over every cell of a Teli zone and a hedge (strength 70) inside a Frangivento zone along its longest side — whatever the levers, in 2D and both 3D views; when such a zone appears SimMap raises the levers once per zone and device (vineLeversForZones: irrigation ≥ +8 %, shade cloth ≥ the Teli zones' share of the usable surface in 10 % steps, windbreak ≥ 40 %, every vineyard zone, only raising) with a toast "Interventi messi nelle zone", so they count in the simulation. src/test/vine-main-pipe.test.ts.
- Vineyard view options like the piazza (2026-11-07): the vineyard's SimBar has "Solo quello disegnato" and "Posizione migliore" beside "Sposta elementi". Solo quello disegnato (edits.onlyDrawn; enabled once an Irrigazione, Teli d'ombra or Frangivento zone exists): vineLayout lays only the zones' drip lines, cloth and hedge (no lever-driven ones), and the levers of every vineyard zone follow the zones (drawn-area vineLeversOfZones: irrigation +16 % with an Irrigazione zone, shade cloth the Teli zones' share in 10 % steps, windbreak 70 % with a Frangivento zone, else 0), again whenever a zone changes; moving a lever in VineThirst turns it off with "Torna la disposizione automatica". Posizione migliore (edits.best): each zone's shade cloth goes on its cells most in the summer sun (sun-exposure sunPath/sunShare) instead of from the north. The SimBar texts say so for the vineyard. src/test/vine-main-pipe.test.ts.
- Smoother arc dot (2026-11-07, refines the Gauge's slide): the dot follows how much the finger turns round the arc's centre from where it was grabbed (angleAt/tempOfAngle, the finger–dot offset kept: no jump at the start, the same feel along the whole arc, ends included — the sideways projection tempAtX is gone), for touch, mouse and pen alike; a touch starts sliding when it moves along the arc (its tangent at the dot) and is left to the page when it moves across it (1,2× more), and the round handle (GRAB_RADIUS now 36 px) is touch-none near the arc's ends (|cos| > 0,6, where sliding is an up-and-down move) and touch-pan-y on its top; the simulation is updated once per animation frame while sliding (the last value flushed on release), the dot itself every move.
- GPS off said (2026-11-07): turning the map's GPS off with its button shows a toast "GPS spento · La mappa non segue più la tua posizione. Toccalo di nuovo per riaccenderlo."; revoking the position inside the app while the GPS was on says "GPS spento · Hai revocato la posizione nell'app…" (LocationSearch in views/SimulationGrid).
- Build number instead of date (2026-11-07, supersedes the "Versione dell'app · 31/10/2026 · 22:40" label): the account panel reads "Versione dell'app · Build 446408" — lib/app-version buildNumber, the minutes from 1 January 2026 UTC to __BUILD_TIME__, so each build has its own number, newer ones higher, no date nor time on screen (src/test/build-number.test.ts); /version.json and the update checks still compare the build time.
- Alerts swiped away (2026-11-07): every toast (the GPS ones included) can be swiped away sideways, left or right, or up (components/ui/sonner swipeDirections ["left", "right", "top"]; at the top centre sonner's default allowed only up). GPS alerts share the toast id "gps-state", so the two map searches (Mappa and the preview) show one alert, a new one replacing the last.
- Build code and no tab swipe (2026-11-07): the account panel reads the version as an alphanumeric code like a console model's, "Versione dell'app · MDTW-9KK8" (lib/app-version buildLabel: "MDTW-" + buildNumber in base 36, upper case; supersedes "Build 446408"). The sideways swipe between tabs is off (lib/use-tab-swipe TAB_SWIPE_ON false: the simulations' Mappa · Simula · Sensori AI, the dashboard's Piazza | Vigneto, the Cronologia's filters and FoldTabs change only with a tap); set it to true to bring the swipe back.
- Tab change animation (2026-11-07): opening a simulation no longer slides (useSimTab's server snapshot is the store's own tab, so the page no longer rendered Simula and then slid to Mappa on hydration; Panels gives no entrance in its first 600 ms). TabRow's slate pill is one element under the tabs that glides to the tapped tab (transform and width, 320 ms on cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1); placed without animation on the first layout, a resize, a quiet switch or reduced motion), and a panel enters with a short fade and a 1 rem slide (260 ms, same curve).
- No gliding pill (2026-11-07, undoes the TabRow pill of "Tab change animation"): the open tab wears its own slate pill again, switching at once with a colour transition; kept: no slide on opening a simulation and the panels' short fade-and-slide entrance.
- Pages open from the right (2026-11-07, supersedes the root's 40 px page slide by menu side): every page change slides the new page in from the right edge to its place (__root: #main-content animated from translate3d(100%) and 60 % opacity to rest, 380 ms on cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1), in a layout effect so it never shows at rest first); none on the app's first page nor with reduced motion; the body's overflow-x clip keeps the page from scrolling sideways meanwhile.
- Dashboard opens at its top (2026-11-07): the office pills (HistoryBlock OfficeScopePills) bring the chosen pill into view by scrolling their own row only (scrollLeft), no longer with scrollIntoView, which also scrolled the page down to the history on every opening of the dashboard (and Benvenuto, Cronologia) for a municipal officer.
- Tabs enter from the right side (2026-11-07, fixes "Tab change animation"): SimShell lists its panels in the tabs' order (Mappa, Simula, Sensori AI — Simula had been first, so tapping it slid in from the left), and Panels decides each entrance once when the tab changes and keeps it (it was recomputed on the next render and the class came off one frame in, so the panels barely moved); a later tab enters from the right, an earlier one from the left, with a fade and a 3 rem slide in 320 ms on cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1).
- Build word back (2026-11-07): the version reads "Versione dell'app · Build MDTW-9KO1" (buildLabel "Build MDTW-" + base 36).
- Same device once (2026-11-07): "Dispositivi collegati" lists each phone or computer once — rows with the same label are the same device registered again after its id was lost (reinstall, cleared data); models/device-label mergeSameDevices keeps this device's row (else the one used last) with the group's earliest first sign-in and latest use, and lib/account-devices removes the extra rows from the account (src/test/device-label.test.ts). Two identical phones on one account read as one.
- Map reading pill stays centred (2026-11-07): the pill over the map (and the update pill) is centred by Tailwind's translate property, so their entrance keyframes no longer add a transform translateX(-50%) on top (which pushed them half their width left until the animation ended, then they jumped to the centre): .map-reading pulses with scale only, update-pill-in moves with translateY only.
- /scegli sign-in (2026-11-07, supersedes its "Salta, vai al benvenuto"): under "Entra come ospite" the choice page reads "Hai già un account? Accedi" (shown to guests only), which opens the account panel (lib/camera-dock openAccount); the entrance pages have no header, so the root mounts AccountControl hidden on / and /scegli for the panel to open there.
- Swipe back on the identification forms (2026-11-07): on a sector's form (/scegli, the sector page) and on /identificazione/* a swipe to the right goes back like "Indietro" (lib/use-swipe-back useSwipeBack: the page follows the finger at 60 % once the move is clearly sideways, past 70 px or a flick it goes back with a haptic tick, shorter it springs back; vertical moves scroll; maps, sliders and sideways rows keep their own moves).
- Back from the forms never blank (2026-11-07): the sector forms on /scegli and the sector page take the phone's Back (and Android's edge back gesture, which fired with the swipe) through useBackClose, so Back closes the form and shows the choice again instead of leaving the page; and "/" reached again later with the tutorial already seen (Back from /scegli) moves on by itself after 450 ms to the profile's simulation on Mappa (Tutorial skip), since the root's landing runs only on a fresh open and the page stayed an empty dark screen.
- Choose among profiles registered before (2026-11-07, refines the sector page's "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto"): this device keeps every profile identified on it per kind, newest first (lib/use-pro-id known list, localStorage mdt-pro-id-known-v1, at most 8 each; the same person — name and licence/region or town/office — replaces their older entry; the account's own pro_last joins it on sign-in; the old single last profile seeds it). With one the card is as before; with more it reads "Su questo telefono si sono registrati n profili del settore urbano / agricolo: scegli chi sei…" and lists them (initials badge, name, proSummary, an arrow to continue as that one with "Bentornato", and an ✕ "Togli da questo telefono", forgetKnownProId). The sector page's form takes the phone's Back through its own history step (__sectorForm), not useBackClose, which on unmount stepped back after a profile was chosen and undid the move to its simulation.
- Profile chosen wins over the account read (2026-11-07): lib/use-pro-id saveProId stamps pickedAt, and the sign-in's adopt (which awaits the history check) leaves the profile alone when one was chosen on the device after it started, so the account's older profile no longer turns a farmer just picked back into a municipal officer. The "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" chooser is a shared component (SectorGate KnownProfiles) shown on the sector page's form and on a locked section's page (ProfileGate, above its form), so on the vineyard while identified as an officer one picks the farmer profile in one tap.
- Recognised profiles carry their personal account (2026-11-07): each profile in the device's known list keeps the personal account it was registered with (lib/use-pro-id KnownProId.acct { email, pic }: attachAccount, run whenever a profile is identified or picked while signed in, stores the account's email and a 96 px JPEG data-URL copy of its picture — a storage link that would expire is not kept, another site's picture keeps its link); "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" (SectorGate KnownProfiles) shows that picture (else the initials) and the email under each profile, the single card too. The lists stay per sector (urban profiles on the urban form, vineyard ones on the vineyard form), each tied to its own personal account.
- One personal account, one sector (2026-11-07, as asked): a personal account belongs to the sector of its first professional profile (user data pro_sector, written by saveProId and by the sign-in's adopt; lib/use-pro-id sectorOf: pro_sector, else the only kind owned, else — an older account owning both — the kind in use, else its most recent; src/test/account-sector.test.ts). Signed in to it, the other sector's profiles cannot be identified nor picked: saveProId refuses them (a toast "Questo account è del settore urbano / agricolo") and returns false, the forms (IdentifyForm, so the sector page, /scegli, the locked section and /identificazione/*) show AccountSectorLock instead — "Questo account è del settore …", "L'account <email> è legato alla piazza / al vigneto: ogni account personale vale per un solo settore. Per il settore … accedi con un altro account, o creane uno nuovo." and a white "Esci e usa un altro account" (keeps the profile in the account, signs out, opens the account panel) —, and "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" hides that sector's profiles. The adopt keeps only the account's own sector (pro_own and pro_last trimmed to it); the account's sector and email are published to useAccountSector (on the session read and every auth change). Signed out, both sectors stay open as before.
- Each profile to its own simulation (2026-11-07, supersedes the locked section shown to the other profile): identified as a farmer, /urbano goes straight to /vigneto on Mappa, and identified as a municipal officer /vigneto goes to /urbano (ProfileGate SectionGate redirect, replace); the locked section with its form now shows only with no profile and no guest mode.
- Switching an account's sector (2026-11-07, refines "One personal account, one sector"): the lock (Identify AccountSectorLock) offers first a white "Usa questo account per il settore urbano / agricolo", asked in place ("Usare questo account per il settore …? Il profilo … resta salvato nell'account, ma finché l'account è del settore … non apre la piazza / il vigneto.", Annulla | Cambia settore), then "Esci e usa un altro account". lib/use-pro-id switchAccountSector writes pro_sector and brings back the account's own profile of that kind (pro_last) or leaves the form to ask for it; the account keeps every kind it registered (pro_own and pro_last are no longer trimmed to the sector), so switching back finds the other profile again. useAccountSector also reads the session again on mount, so a form opened before the root published the sector (the sector page showed the other sector's "Continua come …", then refused it) shows the lock.
- Signing in from the other sector's form (2026-11-07): the lock (AccountSectorLock) first offers the account's own profile of its sector, read from the session's pro_last — a white "Entra come <name> · <proSummary> · apre la piazza / il vigneto" that puts it in use (the sector page then closes and opens that simulation) —, then "Usa questo account per il settore …" (dimmed beside it) and "Esci e usa un altro account"; signing in with Google from the vineyard's form to an urban account (or the reverse) no longer leaves one stuck on the lock.
- Sector lock as before (2026-11-07): "Blocco settore più snello" was reverted at the user's word; the lock keeps "Entra come …", "Usa questo account per il settore …" (with its confirm) and "Esci e usa un altro account".
- Recognised profiles as a 3D carousel (2026-11-07, supersedes the list of "Choose among profiles registered before"): with more than one profile of the sector on the phone, "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" shows them as cards in a sideways carousel (SectorGate ProfileCarousel: scroll snap, cards 64 % wide with the neighbours peeking, the ones aside turned up to 38° and pushed back in depth, faded, painted once per frame on scroll; dots under it to jump; flat with reduced motion; [data-map-gestures] so the swipe stays inside): each card the account picture (else the initials), name, profile, the account's email, a white "Continua" (on a side card it first brings that card to the middle) and a bin that asks in place "Eliminare questo profilo dal telefono? Resta nel suo account." Annulla | Elimina (forgetKnownProId, a toast).
- Pictures and emails for profiles registered before (2026-11-07): lib/use-pro-id syncKnownAccounts, run by the account control whenever the signed-in account's picture is known, ties the phone's profiles that are the account's own — already tied to its email, its registered pro_last, or the one in use with no account yet — to its email and a small copy of its picture, so the carousel's cards (swiped sideways) show them for profiles saved before the list kept accounts too.
- Nicer profile cards (2026-11-07, refines the 3D carousel): each card is a tall rounded card (28 px) 74 % wide on a gradient of its sector (green for the farmer, blue for the officer, into the night slate), a soft light from its top, a light rim and a deep shadow — the one in the middle with a turquoise rim and glow —; on top a chip with the sector's icon and "AGRICOLTORE / FUNZIONARIO" and a round glass bin; the account picture 84 px in a turquoise–blue–violet ring with a glow (else the initials); the name in the display face; one line of detail (detailOf: "Patentino … · Molise" / "Regione …", or "Comune di … · office"); the email in a glass pill with a mail icon (breaking before the @ when long); "Continua" at the foot.
- Profile cards like the single one (2026-11-07, supersedes "Nicer profile cards"): with more profiles of the sector, the carousel's slides are the very "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" card of a single profile — 86 % wide, the next one peeking — each with the kicker, "n / N" and a bin on its top line, the account picture (else the initials), name, proSummary, email, the line "Ti eri già registrato con questo profilo…" and the white "Continua come <name>"; one sentence above them ("Su questo telefono ci sono n profili del settore …: scorri e scegli chi sei."); swiped sideways, the ones aside turned only 24° and 70 px back; the delete confirm in place as before.
- Profile carousel always 3D (2026-11-07, refines "Profile cards like the single one"): the carousel is used for one profile too (no dots, no sentence above), its cards 78 % wide with both neighbours in view, turned 42° on the edge near the middle and 90 px back — always, also with reduced motion, as it is a pose that follows the finger, not an animation; the kicker "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" and a bin on top (no counter), the button "Continua come <first name>" so nothing is cut.
- A profile belongs to its personal account (2026-11-07): signed in, "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" shows only the profiles tied to this account's email (and those not tied to any account), never another account's; saveProId refuses a profile tied on this phone to another email ("Questo profilo è di un altro account"); attachAccount never re-ties a profile already tied to another email; the sign-in's adopt drops from the account a profile this phone knows as another account's (pro_own, pro_last and the sector it gave). Signed in, the identification forms start filled with the account's name (Google's full name, useAccountSector name) and, for the officer, its email; the profile card's personal email is the signed-in account's ("Email personale", "Email" for the farmer).
- Account sector read after dropping other accounts' profiles (2026-11-07, fixes "A profile belongs to its personal account"): the sign-in's adopt first drops from the account every profile this phone knows as another account's (by the known list's emails), then reads the sector from what is left (pro_sector only if still owned, pro_id and pro_last without the foreign ones) and publishes it at once; so an account given the other account's farmer profile by mistake (mabiondi82 with the farmer of marco.biondi@studenti…) goes back to its own urban sector by itself at the next opening, and its data are rewritten clean.
- Sign-in follows the sector being entered (2026-11-07): the sign-in's adopt moves the account to the sector the person was entering — the sector page's form (kept across Google's round trip in sessionStorage mdt-sector-wanted for 15 minutes, lib/use-pro-id rememberWantedSector, set by SectorGate when a sector's form opens), else the open /urbano or /vigneto page — when the account registered that sector's own profile (not another account's), over its saved pro_sector; its profile of that sector comes back ("Bentornato") and the account is rewritten with that sector. So signing in with the urban account from the urban form opens the piazza even if the account had been left on the vineyard.
- Every registered profile in the carousel (2026-11-07, supersedes the filter of "A profile belongs to its personal account" on "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto"): the carousel shows every profile of the sector kept on this phone, signed in or not, each with its own account's picture and email. A card tied to another personal account than the one signed in reads "Questo profilo è di un altro account: accedi con <email>…" and its white button "Accedi come <first name>" keeps the current profile in its account, signs out, remembers the sector being entered and opens the account panel (toast "Accedi con <email>"), so once in with that account its own profile comes back; it is never taken over by the current account (saveProId still refuses it). Signed in to an account of the other sector, only the other accounts' profiles of this sector show.
- Profile names chosen by the person (2026-11-07, supersedes the name prefill of "A profile belongs to its personal account"): the identification forms never fill the name from the personal account (accounts often carry the same Google name); the officer's email is still prefilled. Each card of "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" has a pencil beside the bin ("Cambia il nome di …"): in place "Che nome vuoi dare a questo profilo?", a field, Annulla | Salva; lib/use-pro-id renameKnownProId rewrites the name in the phone's known list and last profile and, when it is the profile in use, saves it again (in the account too); the name wraps on the card instead of being cut.
- The account's history decides its sector (2026-11-07, fixes an agricultural account read as urban): on every sign-in and opening the adopt reads the account's own intervention_history (legacyOwn, now always): a kind it has interventions of is owned whatever its saved data say; a profile of such a kind is never treated as another account's (the phone's wrong tie is put right, retieKnown); and an account that only ever worked on one project is of that sector (pro_sector rewritten) unless the person is entering the other sector with that account's own profile. Mixed or empty history: the saved sector as before, and the lock's "Usa questo account per il settore …" still switches it.
- A recognised profile enters its personal account too (2026-11-07): lib/account-sessions (installed by the root) keeps every personal account's session seen on this phone, per email, always its latest tokens (localStorage mdt-account-sessions-v1; an explicit "Esci" — signOut, which ends it — forgets it). Picking a profile in "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" tied to an email other than the signed-in one: the current profile is kept in its account, switchToAccount sets that account's kept session (supabase setSession, the account left stays kept for the way back), its sector is moved to the profile's if needed (switchAccountSector), the profile is put in use, a toast "Bentornato … Sei entrato anche con il tuo account <email>" and a clean load of that simulation. Not kept here: signed in to another account it is left without ending it (leaveAccount: the auth storage key removed, page reloaded with the account panel open), signed out the profile is put in use and the account panel opens with "Accedi con <email> · … da qui in poi entrerà da solo con il profilo". The sector lock's "Esci e usa un altro account" now leaves the account the same way (kept, not ended).
- Signing in enters the profile too (2026-11-07, the other way round of "A recognised profile enters its personal account too"; refines "Only the account's own profiles"): on sign-in the adopt counts as the account's own the profiles this phone tied to its email (the known list's acct.email, case ignored — recognised with it, or picked with "Continua come …" before signing in): each fills the account's pro_last for its kind when empty, its kind joins pro_own, and the usual choice follows (the sector being entered, else the account's sector), so signing in to the personal account puts its professional profile in use with "Bentornato" and writes it to the account. Profiles on the phone not tied to any account are still never given to an account.
- Biometric sign-in (2026-11-07): lib/biometric (WebAuthn platform passkey, userVerification required: Face ID / Touch ID on iPhone, fingerprint or face on Android, Windows Hello; biometricName says which in plain words) unlocks a personal account kept on this phone (lib/account-sessions). Signed in, the account panel has "Accesso con <name>" (views/v10/Biometric BiometricCard, under the cloud bar, hidden where the phone has none): a switch that asks the biometrics once (enableBiometric: a credential made for this site, only its id kept per email in localStorage mdt-biometric-v1; the biometric data never leave the phone) and off again. With it on, "Esci" keeps the account on the phone (leaveAccount without opening the panel; the professional profile leaves the device) instead of ending it, and signed out the panel offers above Accedi | Registrati one turquoise "Entra con <name> · <email>" per such account (BiometricSignIn): the phone's recognition, then switchToAccount — no password nor Google. switchToAccount asks the biometrics first for an account that has them on (so also the recognised profiles' carousel): "refused" stops there ("Non ti ho riconosciuto"), a session no longer valid says "Questo account va riconnesso". An account whose session ends (explicit sign-out with it off, or ended elsewhere) loses its biometric sign-in. It is a lock on this phone, not a server login. Tested with a virtual authenticator.
- Biometrics on the professional profiles (2026-11-07, supersedes the account panel's "Accesso con …" and "Entra con …" of "Biometric sign-in"): Face ID / Touch ID / fingerprint is set per professional profile (views/v10/Biometric, key "pro:<kind>:<at>", so a renamed profile keeps it): each card of "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" has a round fingerprint (ScanFace on Apple) button before the pencil and the bin (ProfileBioButton, turquoise while on, a toast says it), and the profile page's ProfileCard a row "Accesso con <name>" with a switch (ProfileBioRow). With it on, "Continua come …" shows the icon and first asks the phone's recognition (checkProfileBiometric: "Non ti ho riconosciuto" stops there), then enters the profile and its personal account as before. "Esci" from an account with such a profile keeps the account on the phone (leaveAccount, not ended), so the profile's recognition brings the account back too. The account panel no longer shows biometric controls.
- Biometrics in the account panel (2026-11-07, refines "Biometrics on the professional profiles"): the panel's "Profilo professionale" card has, under the profile in use, "Accesso con <name>" with a switch (views/v10/Biometric ProfileBioSwitch, same key as the cards' button), and signed out the panel shows above Accedi | Registrati one turquoise "Entra con <name> · <profile> · piazza/vigneto · <email>" per profile with the recognition on whose personal account is kept on the phone (BiometricSignIn): the recognition, then that account (switchToAccount, its sector moved to the profile's if needed), the profile in use and a clean load of its simulation; a session no longer valid says "Questo account va riconnesso". The sector page's "Hai già un account? Accedi" opens this panel, so the same buttons are there.
- Recognition enters at once, "Esci" only here (2026-11-07): turning Face ID / fingerprint on for a profile while signed out enters that profile and its personal account straight away when the account is kept on the phone (views/v10/Biometric enterWithProfile, recognised just now; else "Accedi una volta con il tuo account: poi … ti farà entrare da solo"), and opening the app signed out with such a profile asks the recognition by itself (useBiometricAutoEnter in the root, once per load, 0,9 s in: the profile in use first, else the newest; recognised, the account and the profile open on its simulation; where the browser wants a tap first, the account panel opens with "Entra con …"); not right after leaving an account on purpose (leaveAccount sets sessionStorage mdt-bio-skip). The account panel's "Esci" ends only this phone's session (supabase signOut scope "local"; it was the default "global", which signed the account out on every device); only "Esci da tutti i dispositivi" ends them all.
- Straight into a stored profile, "Aggiungi account" slide (2026-11-07, refines "A recognised profile enters its personal account too"): picking a profile in "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" never stops at the account panel: its account kept on the phone is entered (with its recognition when on), else the profile opens at once without its account — signed out, saveProId and the gate moves on; signed in to another account, setProIdOnDevice then leaveAccount({ openPanel: false, to: its simulation }) — with a toast "Bentornato … Per salvare nel cloud entra con <email> dal pannello account." The carousel ends with an "Aggiungi account" slide (dashed turquoise card, UserPlus, "Entra con un altro account Google o email: il suo profilo si aggiunge qui.", white "Aggiungi account"; a dot of its own): it remembers the sector and opens the sign-in (signed in, the current account is first kept in the phone and left, leaveAccount, the panel reopening after the reload).
- A stored profile signs in its personal account (2026-11-07, refines "Straight into a stored profile"): picking a profile whose account is not kept on the phone puts the profile in use and starts its account's sign-in by itself (lib/account-sessions signInAs): a Google account — the known entry's acct.provider, now saved by attachAccount / syncKnownAccounts from app_metadata.provider, else a Gmail address — goes straight to Google's sign-in for that address (signInWithOAuth with login_hint, back to the profile's simulation through mdt-auth-return), any other opens the account panel with its email written (the "mdt-open-account" event's detail.email) asking only the password; a toast "Accedo con <email> · Collego il tuo account personale a <name>". Signed in, the adopt takes the profile tied to that email, and the session is kept on the phone from then on, so the next pick enters at once (with its recognition when on).
- Recognised profiles sign in with Google (2026-11-07, refines "A stored profile signs in its personal account"): signInAs goes to Google's sign-in for the profile's address whenever its account is not known to use email and password (acct.provider "email"), so school and work addresses on Google Workspace (…@studenti.itsdemos.it) are identified with their Google account too, not only Gmail ones; only accounts known to use a password open the panel with the email written.
- Text changes from the user's table (2026-11-07, tabella_modifiche_testi.xlsx, applied as its last column; supersedes the older wording above where they differ): piazza Simula — comfort classes calculated from the perceived temperature (UrbanThermometer comfortClass: caldo estremo ≥46, caldo molto forte ≥38, caldo forte ≥32, caldo moderato ≥26, else "Nessun disagio da caldo"; the arc's chip and "Cosa ottieni": "Il calore percepito scende di n° (da a° a b°). Livello: <class>."), "−n° rispetto a senza interventi" (the number mono, the words small), "Temperatura percepita" with an ⓘ ("Combina temperatura dell'aria, sole, umidità e vento: è il caldo che senti davvero."), "Temperatura dell'aria ora", arc ends "Minimo raggiungibile" / "Media stagionale" (or "Stima stagionale"), "Calore percepito medio degli ultimi 12 mesi (fonte: Open-Meteo)" — the user asked for the source here, an exception to "No visible technology" —, the no-heat lines ("A quest'ora non c'è stress da caldo: l'effetto rinfrescante è trascurabile. Gli altri benefici restano validi.", "A quest'ora non fa caldo: gli interventi non cambiano il comfort."), levers "Superfici verdi e permeabili (% della piazza)", "Coperture ombreggianti (% della piazza)" with "Tende, pergole, pensiline.", Nebulizzatori with "Funzionano meglio con aria secca e poco vento; effetto locale, vicino agli ugelli." (Lever takes a hint and wraps long labels), "Modifica gli interventi"; "Cosa ottieni" tiles (also dashboard and PDF) "CO₂ assorbita all'anno", "Acqua piovana trattenuta (m³/anno)", "Posti all'ombra (capienza teorica, 2 m² a persona)" (labels wrap) and the note "Ipotesi: 700 mm di pioggia l'anno; le superfici verdi e permeabili ne trattengono fino al 60%."; levels "Interventi leggeri / Interventi medi / Progetto completo" (models/scenarios, everywhere); tutorial step 2 "Scegli quanto rinfrescare" / "Sull'indicatore scegli il comfort…", step 1 "disegni il perimetro dell'area mettendo i pin sugli angoli"; map pill "· A quest'ora non serve rinfrescare", draw bar "Metti i pin sugli angoli: useremo la superficie per stimare i costi."; Sensori AI "…dove installare le centraline microclimatiche (temperatura, umidità, irraggiamento)…"; saves "Salvato su questo dispositivo (in modalità ospite non va nel cloud)." (sync notice and LabMedia); history "ogni modifica viene salvata in automatico"; screen-reader labels in Italian (map MAP_LOCALE "Mappa", "Segnaposto"; toasts "Notifiche (Alt+T)"; sector page "Scegli il settore"); the guest button set apart from the sign-in ones; straight apostrophes between letters turned into ’ in every on-screen string (string literals and JSX text; town and region names left as their data). The cookie notice (row 28) still waits for its final legal text.
- Biometrics in the credits (2026-11-07): Licenze's "Permessi del telefono" lists the biometric recognition (Face ID / Touch ID, fingerprint or face, Windows Hello: only when turned on for a professional profile, done by the phone, nothing biometric leaves it, off from the profile or the account panel), and a "Riconoscimento biometrico" card before Software credits the passkeys' open standard (Web Authentication, W3C and FIDO Alliance), says only a key's code is kept on the device, and names the marks' owners (Apple, Microsoft).
- Sensor code inside the app (2026-11-07): when a sensor is added (or its key renewed) the key box (views/v10/MySensors KeyBox) gives the device's code already written with the app's address (location.origin + /api/public/sensor-readings) and that key (models/sensor-code: curlCode, esp32Code, pythonCode; src/test/sensor-code.test.ts): radios ESP32 | Raspberry Pi | Prova veloce, for ESP32 two fields "Nome della rete Wi-Fi" / "Password del Wi-Fi" written into the sketch only (never saved), the code in a scrollable box, "Copia il codice", "Scarica" (sensore_esp32.ino, sensore.py, prova-sensore.txt) and "Fatto"; temperature and air-humidity sensors read a DHT22 on GPIO 4 / board.D4 and send {"temperature", "humidity"}, the other kinds an analogue reading to adapt (leggiValore / leggi_valore) sent as {"value"}; every 10 minutes. The key still shows only then ("Nuova chiave" to get it again).
- Sensor form fits the card (2026-11-07): in "I tuoi sensori" the "Cosa misura" and "Zona" choices wrap onto more lines (flex-wrap, each pill at most the card's width) and their fieldsets carry min-w-0 — a fieldset is as wide as its content by default, so the one-line row of kinds pushed "Temperatura delle superfici (termico)" past the card's edge.
- Less Supabase traffic (2026-11-07; the free plan's 5 GB of egress were passed on 31 Oct, ~7,7 GB in one day of Storage, when the history still showed whole originals): signed links to the account's files are made for 7 days and kept on the device (lib/signed-links keptLink/keepLink, localStorage mdt-signed-v1, reused while younger than 6 days, at most 3000), so a photo, a video or the account picture keeps the same address and the browser serves it from its cache instead of downloading it again (lab-media-store listing, lab-media-uploader, account-control's avatar; the hourly links before looked like new files); reloadFresh keeps the thumbnails' cache (mdt-thumbs-*) when it drops every other cache on an update, so they are not made again from the originals; VideoPlayer draws its frame strip only once the player is touched, from a copy that reads only what each frame needs (preload metadata, it was "auto": the whole video downloaded a second time). "Svuota cache" still drops the thumbnails, as it says.
- Lighter app (2026-11-07, measured first): (1) fewer automatic reads — the media and history sync (lib/use-media-sync) every 5 minutes instead of every minute, at once on sign-in, on the connection coming back and on return to the app (if 30 s have passed); the dashboard's approval answers every 2 minutes instead of 15 s, only while the page is in front, and at once on return; sensors (1 min on their tab), air temperature (15 min) and update checks unchanged. (2) 46 files never used removed (the unused shadcn ui components, views/v1, v7/VineStudio, ArGroundMap, CaseUpdates and its controller, CompareButton, CopernicusPanel, RealClimatePanel, ar-native, hooks/use-mobile): the build already left them out, so this tidies the project, not the phone's download; the Copernicus and weather data still flow through their clients. (3) code loaded only when needed: the account panel's plans/help/privacy/legal pages (React.lazy with a spinner), the sector page (the root mounts SectorGate lazily) and the recognised-profiles carousel on a locked section — the first load of a simulation page fetches about 35 KB less JavaScript; the measure: about 1,6 MB of JavaScript before the page's load event (≈ 450 KB compressed) plus ≈ 550 KB of other pages warmed afterwards on purpose, nothing downloaded again on later openings, one request (version.json) in 2 idle minutes as a guest.
- Leaving a profile, Google button, both sectors alike, panel by sector (2026-11-07): "Esci dal profilo" (account panel and ProfileCard) now signs out of the personal account too (views/v10/Biometric leaveProfile: the profile kept in the account — keepProIdInAccount, no pro_left, so the next sign-in brings it back —, dropped from the device, signOut scope local; with Face ID / fingerprint on for one of the account's profiles the account is only left on this phone, leaveAccount, to come back with the recognition; without an account only the profile goes) and opens the sector page on that sector. "Accedi con Google" is always in the panel: googleEnabled starts true and hides only when Supabase's settings say google: false (a failed or slow settings read used to hide it). The recognised profiles' carousel (KnownProfiles, with onPicked) is on every sector form of both sectors — the sector page, /scegli (which also remembers the sector being entered) and /identificazione/agricoltore|funzionario — loaded lazily. The account panel signed out lists the accounts by sector (BiometricSignIn, now "Settore urbano" / "Settore agricolo", the one in use first — the page open, else the profile in use): each profile tied to an account, "Entra con <recognition>" when kept with the recognition on, "Entra come <name>" when kept, else "Accedi come <name>" (saveProId, then signInAs: Google straight for that address).
- Sign-in always recognises the profile (2026-11-07): the account's old pro_left (written when "Esci dal profilo" left the personal account signed in) no longer holds the profile back on sign-in — leaving a profile now leaves the account too, so signing in means entering one's profile —; the adopt ignores it and writes pro_left false (keepProIdInAccount too). Emails are compared ignoring case everywhere a profile's account tie is checked (foreign, saveProId, attachAccount, syncKnownAccounts, retieKnown).
- Quick "Aggiungi account" with no profile yet (2026-11-07): when a sector has no profile kept on the phone (often the vineyard the first time) KnownProfiles no longer renders nothing: a dashed card "Aggiungi account · Entra con un account Google o email del settore …" with a white "Aggiungi" (the same addAccount as the carousel's last slide) shows on every sector form, so both sectors offer the quick add.
- Quick add like the urban sector (2026-11-07, supersedes the dashed card of "Quick Aggiungi account with no profile yet"): a sector with no profile kept on the phone shows the same 3D carousel as the other, with only its last slide "Aggiungi account" (centred, justify-center while empty), so the vineyard and the piazza look and work alike.
- Same sector layout, no in-between screens on opening (2026-11-07): an empty sector's carousel sits exactly like the other's (left-aligned first slide, no justify-center) and "Oppure registra un altro profilo" shows above the form in both sectors. The index preloader no longer fades as soon as the app has mounted: it also waits until the app has left "/" for its page (unless the tutorial is to show) and for lib/use-pro-id openingSettled — the opening's session read and, signed in, the account's profile (adopt) — then 250 ms, at most 4 s in all, so the empty "/", another page or the sector page never flash before the right simulation. LabSyncIndicator says nothing for the saves of the page's first 3 s (the stored state read back), so no "Salvato…" notice flashes on opening.
- No in-between screens on entering an account, Google from the panel enters its own profile (2026-11-07): the preloader also covers a return from Google's sign-in (?code=, error_description, #access_token) and any reload the app makes itself (lib/preloader preloadNext → sessionStorage mdt-preload, set by leaveAccount, the carousel's and the fingerprint's entries into an account), at least 0,5 s there (1,8 s on "/"), until openingSettled — which, back from Google, waits for the SIGNED_IN adopt (7 s cap then). The sign-in's adopt picks the sector as the first usable of: the sector page asked by name, the sector form being entered (mdt-sector-wanted), the one profile this phone tied to the account's email, the page open; then the account's only worked project, then its saved sector — so "Accedi con Google" in the panel with the vineyard account enters its farmer profile at once, whatever page or sector form was open.
- Entering without reloads (2026-11-07): entering a profile's personal account from the carousel or with the fingerprint no longer reloads the page (that showed the loading screen a second time): lib/app-nav goInApp dispatches "mdt-go", the root invalidates its queries, opens the simulation's Mappa tab and navigates in place. Back from Google's sign-in the root waits for openingSettled (the account's profile read, at most 6 s) under the preloader and goes straight to that profile's simulation (/vigneto or /urbano on Mappa), never through the sector page; only without a profile it goes back where it was.
- One loading only, "Aggiungi account" straight to Google (2026-11-07): the carousel's "Aggiungi account" goes straight to Google's account chooser (lib/account-sessions addGoogleAccount: prompt select_account, back to the same page; the account signed in stays kept on the phone, no leaveAccount reload, no panel; the panel only if Google cannot start), slide text "Scegli un altro account Google: il suo profilo si aggiunge qui.". A page load that is not "/" (back from Google, a reload the app asked for) is covered only by the plain dark canvas (preloader class "plain": no tree, no text, no minimum time, a 0,25 s fade), so the animated loading shows once, at the app's opening. The sign-in's adopt never stops because the account's history could not be read (owned kinds fall back to the phone's ties), and an account with no profile of the sector being entered opens that sector's form (openSectorGate(entering)), not the two-sector choice.
- Adding an account fixed (2026-11-07): back from Google (?code=) the account that was signed in before is no longer adopted while the new one's code is exchanged (useSyncProId skips the startup getSession adopt then; it had put the old account's profile back, moved the app to its simulation and only then switched), so only the account just chosen is read: its profile of the sector being entered comes in at once (and joins the carousel), else that sector's form opens with a toast "Sei entrato con <email> · Registra qui il suo profilo della piazza / del vigneto…".
- Back after a Google sign-in (2026-11-07): every Google sign-in (panel, "Aggiungi account", a recognised profile's signInAs) opens Google in place of the page (lib/account-sessions googleOAuth: skipBrowserRedirect, location.replace), and back from Google the root puts the page that was open before (mdt-auth-return) in the returning entry's place and pushes the simulation after it, so Back from the simulation goes to the page before the account chooser, not to Google's chooser.
- Any account can be added to either sector (2026-11-07, as asked; refines "One personal account, one sector"): "Aggiungi account" on a sector's form remembers that sector (lib/use-pro-id rememberAddingSector, sessionStorage mdt-adding-account, 10 minutes), and back from Google the sign-in's adopt puts the account just chosen in that sector whatever its saved one (pro_sector rewritten; its other sector's profile stays saved in the account): its profile of that sector comes in, else that sector's form opens to register it, after which it is in the carousel — as the urban sector already showed several accounts. Only a return from Google (?code=) takes the mark.
- Google's return in a new window (2026-11-07): in the installed app the return from Google can open in a fresh window, where sessionStorage is empty, so the sector being entered and the "Aggiungi account" mark were lost and a new account landed on the two-sector choice; mdt-sector-wanted, mdt-adding-account (both timed) and mdt-auth-return now live in localStorage. Tested with Supabase answered by Playwright route mocks (scratchpad oauth*.cjs: authorize → redirect with ?code, token exchange, user, history), the return opened in a new page: a new account added from the vineyard lands on the vineyard form, pro_sector agronomo.
- One account, one sector restored (2026-11-07, as asked; supersedes "Any account can be added to either sector"): "Aggiungi account" puts the account chosen in the sector it was added from only when that account has no sector yet (no pro_sector, no history of one project); an account already of the other sector keeps it (its own profile comes back, or the lock shows). Checked with the mocked sign-in: a new account added from the vineyard becomes agronomo, an urban account added from the vineyard stays funzionario.
- The account just added shows at once (2026-11-07): back from Google with a new account (signed in, of this sector or of none, no profile of this sector tied to its email on the phone) the sector form shows above the carousel an "Account aggiunto" card (SectorGate NewAccountCard: its picture, its email, "Ultimo passo: registra qui sotto il suo profilo del vigneto / della piazza…", a white "Completa" that scrolls to and focuses the form's name), since the person read the unchanged carousel as the account not added. Mocked end to end: register the profile → it is tied to that email, joins the carousel, the account is agronomo and the vineyard opens.
