- No form for an added account (2026-11-07, as asked; supersedes the "Account aggiunto … Completa" step): back from Google with a new account added from a sector's form, the sign-in's adopt makes its profile of that sector at once from the Google account (name = Google's full name, else the email's local part; agricoltore: no licence, no region; funzionario: the account's email, no town nor office), saves it in the account (pro_id, pro_last, pro_own, pro_sector), ties it to the email in the phone's list (so it joins the carousel) and opens that simulation, with a toast "Account aggiunto: <email> · Ho creato il suo profilo da agricoltore / da funzionario… I dettagli li completi quando vuoi dal profilo." proSummary reads just "Agricoltore" / "Funzionario comunale" while region or town is empty. The NewAccountCard stays only for an account that somehow comes back without one. Mocked end to end.
- Shared projects planned, not built (2026-11-07): docs/piano-progetti-condivisi.md holds the plan (phase 0 the simulation in the account — today the lab-store lives only on the device —, then projects with members, roles and invites, project history/media, realtime, comments) and docs/progetti-condivisi/mock.html its five mock screens. Put aside "for another day" at the user's word: do not start it unasked.
- One account, one sector, strict again (2026-11-07, as asked; supersedes "Switching an account's sector", "Sign-in follows the sector being entered" and the sector moves in the carousel and the fingerprint entry): an account whose saved pro_sector has its own profile stays in that sector whatever form or page it signs in from (adopt: savedHolds); only an account with no sector yet, or a saved sector without its profile (a wrong mark), takes the sector being added to or entered, or the one its history shows. AccountSectorLock no longer offers "Usa questo account per il settore …" (nor its confirm): "Entra come <own profile>" and "Esci e usa un altro account" (white when the account has no own profile), text "Per il settore … accedi con un altro account, o creane uno nuovo."; picking a recognised profile or entering with the fingerprint never moves an account's sector (switchAccountSector no longer called; saveProId refuses with "Questo account è del settore …"). Mocked: an urban account added from the vineyard stays urban, a new account added from the vineyard becomes agricultural.
- Sector lock without "Entra come" (2026-11-07, as asked; refines "One account, one sector, strict again"): AccountSectorLock shows only the notice ("Questo account è del settore …", the account's email, "Per il settore … accedi con un altro account, o creane uno nuovo.") and the white "Esci e usa un altro account"; the "Entra come <own profile>" button is kept in the code behind LOCK_ENTER_OWN (false) in views/v10/Identify, true restores it.
- Sector lock on its own page (2026-11-07, as asked; refines "Sector lock without Entra come"): AccountSectorLock is no longer a card under the carousel (it fell below the screen) but a full-screen dark page of its own over the form and the sector page (portal to body, z above the sector page, the entrance's Backdrop): "Indietro" (history back, to the sector choice), an amber shield, a big thin "Questo account è del settore …", the text — now "è legato alla piazza / al vigneto" (SECTOR_TO; it read "a la piazza") — and the white "Esci e usa un altro account".
- No way round the sector lock (2026-11-07, as asked): signing in just now (SIGNED_IN, adopt's signIn) from the other sector's form — its "Aggiungi account" (adding mark) or the sector page open on it (gateAsk / mdt-sector-wanted) — with an account whose saved sector holds its own profile no longer enters that account's profile and simulation: nothing is put in use, the account's data stay as they are, and that form opens, where the lock page shows ("Questo account è del settore …", "Esci e usa un altro account"). The account panel's per-sector sign-in buttons (BiometricSignIn) do the same while the sector page is open on the other sector (getSectorGateKind): the account comes in (kept session, or its Google sign-in), its profile does not, and the lock page shows. A new account added from a sector's form still gets its profile at once. Mocked: an urban account added from the vineyard shows the lock, a new one opens the vineyard.
- Lock page "Indietro" leaves the account (2026-11-07, as asked): on the sector lock page "Indietro" keeps the profile in the account, drops it from the device, clears the sector being entered and leaves the personal account (leaveAccount: kept on the phone, not ended; no panel) — the app reloads signed out on the sector choice "Da dove vuoi usarmi?".
- Lock page exits to the sector choice (2026-11-07, as asked): "Esci e usa un altro account" on the sector lock page leaves the account like "Indietro" — kept on the phone, profile kept in the account — and lands on the sector choice "Da dove vuoi usarmi?", never on the account panel (leaveAccount openPanel false, to /benvenuto).
- Sector lock page redesigned (2026-11-07, as asked): Indietro a glass pill; centred, fading up one after the other (tw-animate, none with reduced motion): the account's picture (useAccountAvatar, else a person icon) 96 px in a ring of the account's sector colour with a soft glow and the sector's badge on its corner (sky blue + Building2 for the piazza, emerald + Grape for the vineyard; SECTOR_TONE_UI), an amber kicker "Un account, un settore", the big thin title with the sector word in its colour, the email in a glass pill, two sector cards side by side (its own lit with "Il suo settore", the other dimmed with a lock and "Bloccato"), the explanation; at the foot the white 56 px "Esci e usa un altro account" and "Il profilo di questo account resta salvato: lo ritrovi quando rientri."
- Phone's Back on the sector lock page (2026-11-07, as asked): the lock page takes the phone's Back itself (its own history step __sectorLock; the sector page's form step ignores the pop while lockShown.on) and does what "Indietro" does — leave the account (kept on the phone), and land on the sector choice with location.replace (leaveAccount replace), so Android's back swipe no longer goes to Google's account chooser and from there back to the lock page. Both lock buttons replace too, and set sessionStorage mdt-gate-guard: the sector choice then holds the phone's Back (a __gateGuard step re-pushed on every pop), so it never steps back into Google's chooser.
- Google's pages dropped from the history (2026-11-07, as asked: the back swipe from the lock page landed on Google's account chooser): Chrome skips history steps pushed without a tap when going back, so the lock page's own step could not hold Android's back swipe. Now googleOAuth records history.length before leaving for Google (localStorage mdt-auth-hist; a copy of the page is pushed first when it is the only entry), and back from Google the root, once the account is read, steps back past Google's pages to the page before the sign-in (history.go, measured 300 ms later so the sector page's own steps count) with mdt-after-google holding where to go; that opening pushes it, which drops Google's pages from the history. The lock decision survives the step (lib/use-pro-id rememberLockAsk / readLockAsk / clearLockAsk, localStorage mdt-lock-ask { kind, user }, 30 minutes, cleared by the lock's buttons and on sign-out): the opening after the step shows the lock again, and any other opening with it pending (the phone's Back went past the lock) leaves the account for the sector choice like "Indietro". Mocked in the same window: lock → Back → sector choice signed out → Back stays; a new account lands on the vineyard and Back never reaches Google.
- Google sign-in inside the app (2026-11-07, as asked: in the installed app Google's pages opened in a window of their own that stayed behind the app, and Android's Back landed on Google's account chooser): every Google sign-in (account panel, "Aggiungi account", a recognised profile's signInAs) first asks the browser's own Google account chooser (lib/google-one-tap googleInApp: FedCM navigator.credentials.get with Google's configURL https://accounts.google.com/gsi/fedcm.json, the OAuth client GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID 359809801969-…apps.googleusercontent.com — the Supabase Google provider's own —, a sha-256 nonce, loginHint for a recognised profile, mediation required, 30 s at most) and turns Google's ID token into a session with supabase.auth.signInWithIdToken (the raw nonce only when the token carries it): no navigation, so nothing of Google's stays in the history or behind the app. Then account-sessions googleOAuth waits for the account to be read (use-pro-id nextAdoptDone, released by the SIGNED_IN adopt) and opens its profile's simulation (goInApp), unless the sector page is asked (form or lock). The adopt now takes the "Aggiungi account" mark on any sign-in (signIn), not only a ?code return. When the chooser cannot be used — no FedCM, refused, closed or no answer: the browser does not tell these apart — the redirect to Google's pages is used as before. The client's "Authorized JavaScript origins" in Google Cloud must list the app's address. Mocked: the chooser's token signs in, an urban account added from the vineyard shows the lock and Back leaves it, a new account lands on the vineyard; without FedCM answering, the redirect follows after 30 s.
- Google sign-in inside the app through Google's library (2026-11-07, fixes "Google sign-in inside the app": the direct FedCM call with a hand-written params { nonce } made Google open its own page with "Required parameter is missing: response_type", 400 invalid_request): googleInApp now loads Google Identity Services (https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client) and calls google.accounts.id.initialize (client GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, hashed nonce, use_fedcm_for_prompt, login_hint for a recognised profile, context signin) and prompt, which asks the browser's chooser with Google's own parameters; the credential goes to signInWithIdToken as before. Not shown or skipped (prompt's moment), no library, or no answer in 30 s → the redirect to Google's pages. Mocked: chooser token → lock / vineyard as before; library failing → redirect after 30 s.
- Recognition at opening only unlocks (2026-11-07, as asked; supersedes the opening's auto-entry of "Recognition enters at once"): opening the app signed out with a profile whose Face ID / fingerprint is on and whose account is kept on the phone still asks the phone's recognition by itself (useBiometricAutoEnter, 0,9 s in), but it only unlocks the app: it stays on the sector choice ("Da dove vuoi usarmi?") with a toast "App sbloccata · Scegli il settore: il tuo profilo ti aspetta lì.", nothing is entered by itself; lib/biometric markUnlocked lets verifyBiometric answer yes for 5 minutes, so picking the profile right after is not asked twice. Not recognised: "Sblocco non riuscito · … il riconoscimento ti verrà chiesto lì." (the account panel no longer opens by itself). Mocked: one recognition at opening, sector choice shown, still signed out.

(2) Google's own pages never open by themselves any more: account-sessions googleOAuth asks only the in-app chooser (googleInApp); when it cannot show, a toast "Scelta dell'account Google non disponibile ora · Riprova tra poco, oppure apri la pagina di Google." with "Apri Google" (googleOAuth forcePage) is the only way to the old page. (3) The account panel's "Profilo professionale" card with no profile has no more "Agricoltore · apre il vigneto" / "Funzionario comunale · apre la piazza" rows: "Nessun profilo attivo: scegli il settore per entrare nella piazza o nel vigneto." and one white "Scegli il settore", which closes the panel and opens the sector choice (openSectorGate(null)).
- Google's page back as the fallback (2026-11-08, reverts point (2) of "Three last touches"): with the in-app chooser not showing, the notice with "Apri Google" sat under the account panel and sign-in, sign-up with Google and "Aggiungi account" did nothing; googleOAuth again opens Google's page by itself when googleInApp cannot be used.
- Back to the 2026-11-07 evening state (2026-11-08, as asked): the account panel's "Profilo professionale" card with no profile shows again "Agricoltore · apre il vigneto" / "Funzionario comunale · apre la piazza" (the "Scegli il settore" button is gone) and the Google sign-in is exactly as the evening before (in-app chooser first, Google's page by itself as the fallback); kept only the dashboard's "Invia il progetto" without the "gratis" tag when signed in.

- AI camera phase 1, Rileva | Prova (2026-11-15; docs/proposta-fotocamera-ai.md): the "ARCore" pills (portrait top centre, landscape beside ✕, the tools panel) are replaced by one radiogroup "Modalità della fotocamera AI" Rileva | Prova (ArCamera modeSwitch; Prova = the grid and objects layers, via toggleAr, whose toasts no longer claim ARCore is on: the WebXR session in lib/arcore.ts is still never started, that is phase 3). Rileva shows the target pills and Scansiona; Prova hides the pills for "Tocca il terreno per posare la pianta · anteprima" and puts "Posa pianta" (handlePlace3DTree at the centre) in Scansiona's place; the duplicate "Posiziona Albero 3D" buttons (burst bar, Livelli panel) are gone. The tutorials' step 3 now explains Prova (the specimen balance was already not rendered).
- AI camera phases 2–6 (2026-11-15; docs/proposta-fotocamera-ai.md): Rileva's result card has "Aggiungi al progetto" (models/field-finds: the scan becomes a find on the map — the phone's GPS moved along the compass heading by the usual distance of that target, SCAN_DISTANCE_M, lib/find-position readFix/smallThumb; localStorage mdt-field-finds-v1, 120 per flow; "Nel progetto" once added), shown on the 2D map as draggable round thumbnails (map-area findElement, moveFind) and in the SimBar ("Rilevati con la fotocamera AI: …"); found trees count as already standing in the piazza (real objects, interventions keep off them). Prova opens views/ArLive: with ARCore (WebXR immersive-ar, requested inside the tap, the camera's own stream stopped first, dom-overlay HUD, hit-test reticle) the live view; without it the same tools as a preview over the camera ("Anteprima: senza ARCore le misure sono indicative": a floor 1,5 m under the eyes, tilt from the phone, fov 70). Tools: the flow's objects at real size (models/ar-objects AR_OBJECTS: Albero, Pergolato, Aiuola, Nebulizzatore · Telo d’ombra, Frangivento, Irrigazione; meshes in views/ar-object-meshes), Allinea (touch the drawn area's points 1 and 2 on the floor, alignTwoPoints; else GPS + compass at start, alignFromFix), Misura (two taps, metres), Annulla, "Riporta sulla mappa · n" (objectsToZones → zones of the drawn area, those falling outside it left out, zonesInside); messages are written over the camera (toasts sit under its dialog). Closing the camera ends the session and the camera restarts after it.
- Regola pavimento (2026-11-15, the preview without ARCore): the floor is guessed from the phone's tilt, so a "Regola pavimento" pill beside the yellow "Anteprima" note shows a turquoise horizon line with a round handle across the camera (role slider "Linea dell’orizzonte", arrows ±0,5°) to drag where the floor meets the background; it sets an offset added to the tilt (models/ar-objects horizonAt / pitchForHorizon, fov 70), kept on the phone (localStorage mdt-ar-floor-v1), "Fatto" closes it ("Pavimento regolato…"); the first preview on a phone opens on it; taps never place objects while it is open.
- Prova on phones only (2026-11-15, as asked "solo mobile"): the AI camera's Rileva | Prova switch shows only on a touch phone (isTouchDevice and the screen's short side under 600 px, isTabletViewport on screen width/height); on tablets and computers the camera stays on Rileva, so the AR live view, the preview and "Regola pavimento" never open there.
- Recognised plants in Prova (2026-11-15): each scan record keeps the species' shape and typical size (ScanSpecies form — columnar, conical, round, umbrella, vine, shrub —, heightM, crownM, evergreen, from the recognition), and «Prova» lists the flow's recognised species first in its object row (models/ar-objects plantObject / recognisedPlants: once each, newest first, at most 6, a leaf icon on a green pill), so a fir scanned is put back as a fir at its own height and crown: views/ar-object-meshes plantMesh draws conical (tiers of cones), columnar (tall narrow spindle with a tip), round (trunk and a lobed crown), umbrella (tall bare trunk and a flat wide crown), shrub (a low cluster of bushes), vine (a short row of vines on posts), evergreens a deeper green; height clamped 0,4–40 m, crown 0,4–25 m. Brought back to the map a tree becomes an Alberi zone of its crown, a shrub an Aiuole zone (in the vineyard a vine a lot, another plant a windbreak). Records saved before have no shape and are skipped. src/test/ar-objects.test.ts.
- Prova slimmed (2026-11-15, "troppo incasinata"): ArLive's HUD is two bands. On top one row: ✕, one status line that also carries every message (green when done, amber for a warning, two lines at most; an "Anteprima" tag inside it without ARCore) and, in the preview, a round "Regola pavimento" button that turns into ✓ while the horizon line is shown. At the bottom one row of what to put (the recognised plants first, with a small leaf) and, beside or under it, three controls only: Annulla and Misura as round icons and a white "Riporta · n". Allinea is no longer a button: "Riporta" asks the drawn area's two points when no corner alignment exists ("Tocca i 2 punti"), then sends by itself; the yellow banner, the alignment pill and the long green notices are gone. In landscape (Tailwind landscape:) the objects and the three controls share one row.
- Shutter in Prova (2026-11-15): the bottom row of ArLive has a round white shutter between Misura and "Riporta" (data-no-ui-click, a count badge once used). In the preview it draws the camera's picture as the screen shows it (cover) and the 3D objects over it (reticle hidden, a render just before the copy) into one JPEG up to 2× the screen, foto-ar-prova-<time>.jpg (so the history reads it as an AI shot), handed to the camera's onCapture like any shot — the camera stays open — with a white flash, a haptic tick and "Foto salvata nel progetto" in the status line. With ARCore the browser does not give the page the camera's image, so the shutter says "Con ARCore fai uno screenshot del telefono".
- Zoom dial in Prova's landscape (2026-11-15, as asked): in the preview without ARCore, in landscape, ArLive shows the AI camera's own ArcZoomDial on the right edge (scaled to fit the height) with the shutter (72 px) at its centre like Rileva; the bottom row and the top line leave room for it and the bottom row's shutter hides there. The zoom (1–5×) crops the camera's picture from its middle (CSS scale) and narrows the 3D camera's view by the same amount (fovAt: 2·atan(tan 35° / zoom)), so the objects stay on their spot of the floor; taps, "Regola pavimento" and the shutter's photo (the video drawn at the same zoom) follow it. Hidden while adjusting the floor and in portrait; with ARCore the browser drives the camera, so no dial.
- One AI button in Prova (2026-11-16, "ancora troppo incasinata"): the object pills no longer sit on screen. The bottom row is a round AI button (Sparkles in a dark disc inside a turquoise–violet–green conic ring with a turquoise glow; it turns into a white ✕ while open; aria-expanded, "Funzioni AI · <current choice>"), Annulla, the shutter (portrait; in landscape it stays in the zoom dial) and the white "Riporta · n". The AI button opens a tray over the camera, above the row (absolute, so nothing moves; at most the screen less 9 rem, scrolling): "Piante riconosciute dall’AI" (leaf chips), "Interventi" and "Strumenti" — Misura and, without ARCore, Regola pavimento (no longer a button on top). Picking anything closes the tray; the status line says what a tap puts. While adjusting the floor the bottom shows only a white "Fatto". The top row is ✕ and the status line alone (in landscape it stops short of the dial).
- Ruler zoom in Prova (2026-11-16, as asked, supersedes "Zoom dial in Prova's landscape"): inside Prova the arc dial gives way to the minimal shared ZoomDial (three chips — neighbouring lens stops beside the live value — and the light ruler that slides under an amber mark on a sideways swipe, folding back after), centred just above the bottom row in portrait and landscape alike (hidden while the AI tray is open, while adjusting the floor and with ARCore); the shutter is back in the bottom row, between Annulla and "Riporta", in landscape too, and the top line runs the full width again. Rileva keeps its arc dial in landscape.
- Shutter on the right in Prova's landscape (2026-11-16, as asked, refines "Ruler zoom in Prova"): in landscape ArLive's shutter (72 px) sits on the right edge, vertically centred, where Rileva has it, with the minimal ZoomDial (chips and ruler) right above it (hidden while the AI tray is open, while adjusting the floor and with ARCore); the bottom-left row keeps only the AI button, Annulla and "Riporta", so the scene gets as much of the screen as possible. - Vertical zoom in Prova's landscape (2026-11-16, as asked, refines "Shutter on the right in Prova's landscape"): the zoom above the shutter is a column — ZoomDial vertical: the bigger stop on top, the live value in the middle, the smaller below, moved also by an up-and-down swipe (up zooms in, dialZoom on the vertical move) with the ruler standing upright to its left (.zoom-ruler[data-vertical], turned 90°, the numbers kept upright, the amber mark across) —, centred over the shutter; the status line on top stops short of it (landscape:pr-[7.5rem]). The horizontal ZoomDial elsewhere is unchanged.
- Prova's bar folds, ruler over the column (2026-11-16, as asked; refines "Vertical zoom in Prova's landscape"): ArLive's status bar on top is a button (aria-expanded): folded it is a small pill — a dot, two words of what a tap does (the object's name, "Misura", "Cerco il pavimento…"), the yellow "Anteprima" tag and a chevron —; a tap opens it to the whole sentence (now "Tocca il pavimento per posare: <oggetto>", no longer "Tocca a terra") with a line on how it works (and, in the preview, that the measures are indicative); another tap folds it; messages open it while they last. - Zoom pills like portrait in Prova's landscape (2026-11-16, as asked; supersedes the vertical column of "Vertical zoom in Prova's landscape" and "Prova's bar folds, ruler over the column"): in portrait ArLive's bottom row is a three-part grid (AI and Annulla on the left, the shutter in the very middle of the screen, Riporta on the right), so the zoom chips above it sit centred on the shutter; in landscape the zoom is the same horizontal ZoomDial as portrait (chips side by side, the ruler above them on a sideways swipe), centred over the shutter, which stays on the right edge at mid height (2,25 rem in from the edge so the chips fit); ZoomDial edge sets the ruler against the screen's right edge (.zoom-ruler[data-edge]). - Zoom column beside the shutter in Prova's landscape (2026-11-16, as asked with a marked screenshot; supersedes the landscape part of "Zoom pills like portrait in Prova's landscape"): in landscape the whole zoom sits just left of the shutter, centred on it — ZoomDial vertical again: the chips in a column (the bigger stop on top), an up-and-down swipe (up zooms in) and the ruler standing upright to their left (.zoom-ruler[data-vertical]) —; the shutter is back 1 rem from the right edge at mid height and the status line runs the full width. - Upright ruler readable (2026-11-16, as asked "il righello"; refines "Zoom column beside the shutter in Prova's landscape"): the vertical ZoomDial's ruler now reads like its chips — the bigger numbers on top (turned −90°), longer (11 rem), its ticks right beside the column and its numbers to their left, white, bigger and with a dark halo so they read over the scene —; it follows the finger, so a downward swipe on the column brings the bigger numbers under the amber mark (zoom in; dialZoom on the negated vertical move).
- Upward swipe zooms in (2026-11-16, as asked; refines "Upright ruler readable"): on the vertical zoom column an upward swipe zooms in (dialZoom on the vertical move as it is), the ruler keeping its bigger numbers on top and sliding the other way, like a value going up.
- Arc dial at rest, pills with the AI tray (2026-11-16, as asked; refines "Zoom column beside the shutter in Prova's landscape"): in Prova's landscape preview the zoom at rest is again the camera's own ArcZoomDial on the right edge with the shutter at its centre, as in Rileva (scaled to fit the height, the status line stopping short of it); pressing the AI button (the sparkles) opens the tray and the dial gives way to the zoom pills in a column beside the shutter (vertical ZoomDial with its upright ruler, upward swipe zooms in); closing the tray brings the dial back. Portrait unchanged; with ARCore no zoom.
- Rileva with icons, no words (2026-11-16, as asked): in the AI camera's Rileva the target pills are icons only (FOCUS_ICONS: Vegetazione a deciduous tree, Suolo a shovel, Foglia a leaf, Chioma a bunch of grapes, Interfila rows; 26 px, .ar-focus-icon; the name stays in aria-label and title), and Scansiona and Strumenti are round 52 px icon buttons (ScanSearch, SlidersHorizontal; .is-icon, in landscape too) with no words; Rileva | Prova keeps its words.
- AI fan instead of the drawer (2026-11-16, as asked: "il cassetto … diverso e minimal", the user chose the fan): in Prova the AI button (sparkles) no longer opens a dark drawer with headings and pills but a fan of round glass icons coming out of it over a quarter circle, up → right, kept above the bottom row (ArLive AiFan, role menu): three groups on an inner arc (118 px) — Piante AI (Leaf), Interventi (a tree in the piazza, grapes in the vineyard), Strumenti (Ruler) — and, on a wider arc (192 px, every other one at 252 px when there are more than four so names never overlap), the icons of the open group (the group of the current choice at first; a tap on another group switches): the recognised plants with a green ring and their form's icon (TreePine conical/columnar, TreeDeciduous, Shrub, Grape), the interventions (OBJECT_ICONS: Albero tree, Pergolato fence, Aiuola flower, Nebulizzatore drizzle, Telo d'ombra umbrella, Frangivento wind, Irrigazione droplets), the tools (Misura, Pavimento); the chosen one turquoise, names 10 px under each with a dark halo. Each icon comes out of the button 28–40 ms after the one before (.ar-fan-item, none with reduced motion); a tap picks and folds the fan, a tap anywhere else folds it (a transparent backdrop). TrayGroup is gone.
- AI camera open on test previews (2026-11-16, as asked: the user could not sign in on the Vercel preview, so could not reach the AI camera): on a PREVIEW_BUILD (vite dev and VERCEL_ENV=preview, never production) the AI camera opens for everyone, guests too and also in a browser tab (CameraDock useAiReady and LabMedia aiReady); production keeps the sign-in and the installed app. Recognition already worked without an account (ar-detect optionalSupabaseAuth).
- Google sign-in on test previews (2026-11-16): Google's in-app chooser answered "Errore 400: origin_mismatch" on the Vercel preview, whose address is not among the OAuth client's authorised JavaScript origins (and changes at every build), so on a PREVIEW_BUILD googleInApp answers "unavailable" at once and the sign-in goes through Google's pages (the Supabase redirect); production keeps the in-app chooser. For the redirect to come back to the preview, the preview address must be in Supabase's allowed redirect URLs, else Supabase sends the sign-in to the Site URL.
- ARCore start made sturdier (2026-11-16, after "The specified session configuration is not supported" on the user's phone, which also closed the camera): requestArSession (ArLive) first leaves the camera's own fullscreen (Chrome's AR overlay takes the screen itself), then tries lighter configurations while the tap still counts — hit-test required with dom-overlay, then all optional, then hit-test optional only, then the bare session — moving on only on NotSupportedError; a session granted without hit-test still runs and says the floor cannot be found there. ArCamera marks the AR start as a transition (arTransitionRef, 1,5 s after the answer), so the fullscreen exit it causes is not read as Back and the camera stays open (falling back to the preview when ARCore is refused).
- Leaving AR, landscape kept (2026-11-16, as asked: "in landscape l'AR torna in portrait", "dammi la possibilità di chiudere l'AR"): requestArSession no longer leaves the camera's fullscreen first (outside it the app is locked portrait): it asks ARCore in fullscreen with dom-overlay always required (hit-test required, then optional), so the HUD and its exit are always there; only when both are refused it leaves fullscreen for one more try and takes it back if that fails too, then the preview. ArLive's top-left ✕ is a labelled pill "‹ Esci da AR" that ends the session and goes back to the plain AI camera (Rileva); endLive then asks the camera's fullscreen again (camera-chrome regainFullscreen, exported), so its landscape comes back; in Prova the phone's Back (a fullscreen exit) leaves AR for Rileva instead of closing the camera.
- Rotation back after AR (2026-11-16, "non ruota con la AR"): an AR session takes the screen's orientation and on its end Chrome puts back the manifest's portrait without telling the page, while syncAppOrientation's cache still read "any" and never asked again, so after AR the camera stayed portrait-locked (turned by CSS, the page letterboxed). lib/ar-orientation resetAppOrientation forgets the cached lock and asks again; ArCamera calls it when the AR session starts, on its end (endLive) and once more 600 ms later.
- Turning the phone during AR (2026-11-16, "non ruota durante l'AR"): Chrome locks the screen to the orientation an ARCore session started in and a page cannot unlock it, so ArLive watches gravity (devicemotion) and, when the phone is held the other way for 0,8 s, shows "Tocca per ruotare la vista AR" (the objects placed reset); the tap calls ArCamera rotateLive: the session is ended (endLive skipped while restarting), 0,6 s for the screen to turn, the orientation lock asked again, then a new session in the same tap (ArLive remounted by key n); refused, the preview.
- Pills in Prova's landscape, always (2026-11-16, as asked; supersedes "Arc dial at rest, pills with the AI tray"): in Prova's landscape preview the arc dial no longer shows: the zoom is always the pills in a column just left of the shutter with their upright ruler (vertical ZoomDial, upward swipe zooms in), tray open or not. The arc code stays behind ARC_IN_PROVA (false) in ArLive; Rileva keeps its arc dial.
- Piazza scan without a target selector (2026-11-16, as asked: the tree | shovel pills "non hanno più ragione di esistere"; the user chose the piazza only): the urban AI camera's Rileva has no target pills any more — one automatic scan (models/ar-focus "auto", Piante e suolo, seeks "any": the AI describes the plant when there is one — species, crown, shade — else the paving's soil and survey, depaving included; scanPrecheck never stops it before the AI, scanMissing accepts a plant or the ground, the server's prompt says a missing plant is not an error) and the record is named by what was found (foundFocus: "Alberi e ombra" with a plant, else "Suolo e depavimentazione", so finds and reports read as before). selectableFocus gives the selector's pills: none in the piazza, the vineyard's three (Foglia, Chioma, Interfila) unchanged. src/test/ar-focus.test.ts.
- Old grid «Prova» removed (2026-11-16, as asked: "il reticolato dove piazzare gli alberi a caso non serve più"): the AI camera's Livelli panel no longer has the grid button (the layer that drew a grid on a guessed floor and put a tree where tapped, with "Posa pianta"), and «Prova» is the AR view only (ArCamera proving = liveAr; Rileva from Prova ends it through endLive). The old code (toggleAr, handlePlace3DTree, the grid layer) stays but is not reachable.
- Flash in Prova (2026-11-16, as asked): ArLive's top row ends with a round flash button (Zap / ZapOff, white with a yellow bolt while on, aria-pressed) that lights the camera's own torch through lib/camera-torch, shown only in the preview and only where the phone offers a torch (with ARCore the browser owns the camera and gives the page no torch); it starts as the AI camera left it, a refusal is written in the status line, and closing the AR view switches it off (ArLive cleanup, ArCamera endLive setTorchOn(false)).
- AI camera multiple shot by holding (2026-11-16, as asked: "le foto multiple così non va bene"; the user chose hold-to-burst; supersedes the AI camera's "Singolo | Multiplo" switch): ArCamera has no Singolo | Multiplo radios any more (top bar, burst bar, tools panel); a tap takes one photo as before, holding the shutter shoots a series (useHoldBurst, no mode change: shoot keeps a multiple shot only while held). The series shows at the bottom left beside Scansiona as a rounded thumbnail of its last photo with a turquoise count badge; a tap on it opens the strip (burst = strip open; the species card hidden meanwhile; portrait just above the zoom, .ar-burst-shelf.is-tools-closed 11,25 rem; landscape top left) to drop shots, its header reading "n foto scattate" with a white "Aggiungi n" that hands them to the project and keeps the camera open (backToSingle); closing the camera or taking a single photo hands them over too.
- Multiple shot reviewed in a sheet (2026-11-16, as asked: "le miniature così non vanno bene", the user chose the sheet with a grid; supersedes the floating strip with "Migliore" and the small ✕): a tap on the series' thumbnail (bottom left) opens a dark sheet rising from the bottom over the dimmed camera (ArCamera burstSheetElement: slate oklch 0.25, handle, "n scatti" with "Tocca una foto per scartarla" / "n da scartare · tocca di nuovo per tenerli", "Chiudi"; a grid of square photos, 3 columns in portrait and 6 in landscape, each numbered — a tap sets one aside, greyed and shrunk with a ✕, another tap keeps it; "Scarta tutte" in red and the white "Aggiungi n", which hands the kept ones to the project and keeps the camera open); closing the camera or a single photo still hands over the kept ones (getOrderedShots leaves the discarded out). The old strip (burstShelfElement) is no longer rendered.
- Series thumbnail in the landscape corner (2026-11-16, as asked with a marked screenshot): in landscape the multiple shot's thumbnail (burstThumb) sits in the bottom right corner, under the shutter and the tools button (absolute, 1,6 rem from the right and 0,9 rem from the bottom, safe areas added), no longer beside Scansiona; in portrait it stays bottom left beside Scansiona.
- AR diagnosis line (2026-11-16, "l'AR parte ma non va"): with ARCore, ArLive's open status bar adds one small mono line of what the session really got — pavimento (hit-test source) sì/no, overlay (domOverlayState) sì/no, frames drawn so far, floor found, the enabled features and any start error — and a session without hit-test says so in the status line (toasts sit under the camera, so they went unseen). Remove once ARCore works on the user's phone.
- Tutorial once on previews too (2026-11-16, as asked): previews no longer show the tutorial at every opening (lib/intro-seen introSeen and the root's landing drop PREVIEW_BUILD): like production it shows at the first access on the device, then the app opens on the profile's simulation; "i" and Preferenze privacy still show it again.
- Haptics in the AI camera by weight (2026-11-16, as asked: "quando rileva il pavimento o qualcosa dammi un feedback aptico, a seconda del peso"): Rileva — a plant caught in the viewfinder ("Agganciato") a short tick (25 ms, at most every 2 s), a scan done a firm double pulse (40-60-40), a scan that found nothing a long buzz (90); Prova — the floor found a firm double pulse (40-60-40) the first time and a short one (30) when found again after 1,5 s lost, a measuring or alignment point 30, and an object put down by its weight (models/ar-objects placeHaptic: footprint × height on a log scale, a light tick for a misting pole up to a double thud for a big tree; src/test/ar-objects.test.ts). All through lib/haptics hapticTick (no vibration with reduced motion, nor on iPhones, whose browser has none).
- Why the preview, said (2026-11-16, the user's Prova ran as "Anteprima" with no word why): when Prova falls back to the preview, ArLive gets the reason (ArCamera liveAr.why) and writes it in the status line at the start and in the open bar's help — the browser has no WebXR ("aprila in Chrome su Android"), WebXR says no immersive-ar ("serve «Google Play Services per AR» aggiornato dal Play Store"), or the session's own error ("ARCore non è partito (…)").
- ARCore without the overlay (2026-11-16, the user's phone answered "The specified session configuration is not supported" to every session with the HUD overlay): requestArSession tries, while the tap still counts and moving on only on NotSupportedError, overlay + floor, overlay alone, floor without overlay, the bare session — in fullscreen first, then once more out of it (fullscreen taken back if nothing starts); a session without the overlay still runs (Chrome shows its own close button, Back ends it and returns to Rileva, a tap on the floor puts the object chosen before). When nothing starts, every refusal is reported together ("overlay+pavimento: NotSupportedError · …") in the preview's reason, readable in the open status bar.
- «Prova» renamed «AR» (2026-11-16, as asked): the AI camera's mode switch reads "Rileva | AR" (the AR radio's label for screen readers "AR, realtà aumentata"), the info pill and the portrait card read "AR" while it is on, and the camera's own tutorial step 3 is "3 · AR" ("passa da «Rileva» ad «AR»"); internal names (setCamMode "prova", proving) unchanged. Where these notes say «Prova» for the AI camera, the screen now says «AR».
- ARCore asked once the camera is free (2026-11-16, every session config refused on the user's phone): AR mode now stops the AI camera's stream, clears the video's source and waits 450 ms before asking the session (ARCore opens the phone's camera itself; asked while ours is still being released, the session can be refused as "not supported"; Chrome keeps the tap's activation about 5 s). The reason of a fallback to the preview stays in the status line whole, unclamped, until the bar is tapped.
- No second fullscreen notice from AR (2026-11-16, the user asked to remove Chrome's "trascina dall'alto e scorri dal bordo… per uscire dalla modalità a schermo intero" banner): that notice is Chrome's own and a page cannot hide it; it shows whenever the page enters fullscreen. requestArSession no longer leaves the camera's fullscreen to retry and comes back (which showed it again over the camera): the session is asked in fullscreen only.
- ARCore asked out of fullscreen again (2026-11-16, the user's Xperia 10 VII refused every session config — bare one included — while the page was in fullscreen, and "prima andava" with the first AR version, which left fullscreen first; supersedes "Leaving AR, landscape kept" and "No second fullscreen notice from AR" on that point): requestArSession leaves the camera's fullscreen before asking, then asks the lock of the orientation the phone was held in (landscape only; it may be refused, then the AR opens portrait) and tries overlay + floor, overlay, floor, bare; a fallback to the preview takes the fullscreen back (regainFullscreen), and leaving AR does too.
- AR blocked for the site, said (2026-11-16, found with the test page public/prova-ar.html — four buttons asking immersive-ar bare, with hit-test, with dom-overlay, after the camera — on the user's Xperia: Chrome had the site's "Realtà aumentata" permission blocked, and then refuses every session as NotSupportedError "The specified session configuration is not supported" while isSessionSupported says true; allowing it in the site's settings made it start): when every try is refused as NotSupportedError the AR mode's reason reads "Chrome ha bloccato la realtà aumentata per questo sito. … Autorizzazioni (o Impostazioni sito) → Realtà aumentata → Consenti, poi riprova." The installed app shares the site's permissions.
- AR sizes and photo (2026-11-16, ARCore working on the user's phone once the site's AR permission was allowed; a 9 m tree filled the room; the user asked for everything offered): (1) two fingers resize the last object put down, 0,1–3× its real size (window touch listeners in ArLive; the height said live in the status line, "Albero · 3,1 m di altezza (vero: 9 m)"; taps around a pinch never put a new object; PlacedObject.scale, objectsToZones scales the footprint with it); (2) «Modellino» in the AI fan's Strumenti (Box icon; "Grandezza vera" to turn it off): every object at 1:10 (MODEL_SCALE), to try indoors or on a table — "Riporta" refuses while it is on, as the positions are not the real ones; (3) the shutter with ARCore: the session asks the optional "camera-access" and the photo is taken in the next XR frame (captureXrPhoto: the camera's image read through XRWebGLBinding.getCameraImage, the objects drawn again from the same eye into an off-screen target, both flipped upright and put together into a JPEG, foto-ar-prova-<time>.jpg, handed to the project); a session without camera-access shows no shutter (its room kept), instead of the old "fai uno screenshot" message. The camera capture with ARCore could not be tried here (no ARCore in the test browser).
- Multiple shot in AR (2026-11-16, as asked): ArLive's shutter takes one photo on a tap and, held (HOLD_MS), a series — a photo every 200 ms until the finger lifts (useHoldBurst), with ARCore too (each frame asked through wantShot "multi") —, files named foto-ar-prova-multiplo-<time>.jpg so the history reads them as "AI · Multiplo"; every AR photo goes to the project like the camera's shots (onShot → ArCamera onCapture → upload, then the Cronologia). The flash stays the preview's only: with ARCore the phone's camera belongs to ARCore and the browser gives the page no torch.
- Two fixes (2026-11-16): the multiple shot's review sheet scrolls its grid inside a box of its own (min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto, the grid content-start, each photo block aspect-square): laid straight in the sheet's flex column, with many shots its rows were squeezed and the photos piled on one another. With ARCore the AR's top row keeps a dimmed flash button (ZapOff, aria-disabled) whose tap says Chrome gives ARCore the camera and no flash, and to turn a light on.
- AR multiple shot reviewed like Rileva's (2026-11-16, as asked): the thumbnail and the review sheet are shared components (views/BurstReview: BurstThumb, BurstReviewSheet, burstThumbOf), used by ArCamera and ArLive; in AR a held shutter keeps the series (no longer straight to the project), its thumbnail sits beside Annulla, a tap opens the same sheet (inside the AR HUD, so its taps never put objects with ARCore), "Aggiungi n" hands the kept ones over, and closing the AR view hands over what is left. With ARCore the AR HUD keeps 1,5 rem at the top (the overlay has no safe area) and a status message shows whole, unclamped.
- One multiple-shot series for AI and AR, labelled, and the flash from ARCore (2026-11-16, as asked): the AR view's multiple shot goes into the AI camera's own series (ArLive prop burst: ArCamera's shots, open state, discarded set and actions; shots tagged src "ar" / "ai"), so the same thumbnail and sheet show it in AR and back in Rileva, kept when AR closes and handed over to the project (then the Cronologia) with "Aggiungi n" or when the camera closes; the sheet tags each photo "AR" (violet) or "AI" (turquoise), and the history labels AR photos (foto-ar-prova…) "AR" / "AR · Multiplo" and the scans' "AI" / "AI · Multiplo" (models/shot-kind shotLabel; src/test/shot-kind-ar.test.ts). With ARCore the flash button (no longer dimmed) leaves ARCore for the preview with the torch on (ArCamera flashPreview → ArLive torch), as ARCore holds the camera and gives no torch.
- Lighter pages (2026-11-16, "alleggerisci l'app", measured on the build): the AI camera is loaded only when first opened (views/ArCameraLazy: React.lazy from LabMedia, mounted closed then opened on the next render as dialogs must be) — three.js (~130 kB compressed) left every simulation page's first load, LabFlows 391 → 234 kB; the dashboard's 24-hour chart is its own lazy piece (views/v10/DayChart, recharts ~100 kB compressed, a soft placeholder while it comes) — the dashboard page 401 → 22 kB; routes/dashboard.tsx dropped its unused recharts import. The update's precache leaves out three.js, three-layer, ArCameraLazy and jszip too (kept by the runtime cache once used).
- Flash from ARCore fixed (2026-11-16): tapping the flash in AR (ARCore) opened the preview with the torch off, because the AR view was mounted before the new camera stream existed and was never given it (streamRef changes do not re-render). ArCamera now bumps a state when a stream arrives (setStreamN) so the AR view gets it, asks getUserMedia up to 4 more times 600 ms apart after an ARCore session (the camera can still be busy), and ArLive tries the torch up to 5 times 400 ms apart, saying so when it cannot.
- Torch found later (2026-11-16, "non funziona il flash"): some phones list the torch only once the video runs, or only through ImageCapture's photo capabilities (fillLightMode flash/torch), so lib/camera-torch probeCameraTorch asks up to 8 times 300 ms apart, and setCameraTorch(track, on, force) tries even when the capabilities do not list it (advanced constraint, then the plain one). The AI camera, the AR preview and the in-app camera show the flash button once the probe finds it.
- AR pergola on the ground (2026-11-16, "l'unico che non tocca terra è il pergolato"): seen through the phone its four thin corner posts, 2 m out from the tap, were often off screen or unnoticed, so the roof looked afloat; views/ar-object-meshes pergolato now has 20 cm posts every 2 m or less round its edge, each on a stone foot, beams on all four sides, and its paving (a see-through sand square) with a square shadow drawn on the floor under it (no round contact shadow for it in ArLive).
- AR pergola without the floor sheet (2026-11-16, screenshot "non poggia per terra"): in a small room the 4 m pergola's far posts stand behind the wall, and with no occlusion the see-through sand square drawn across the floor showed through the wall as a lifted slab, so the posts read as floating on it; the square and its big shadow are gone, each post's stone foot gets its own small round shadow (0,34 m) on the floor.
- ARCore depth hides the AR objects (2026-11-16, as asked "prova con la profondità di ARCore"): the first session try asks the optional "depth-sensing" (views/ar-depth DEPTH_INIT: cpu-optimized, luminance-alpha or float32, both old and new key names), so a phone without it still starts; patchGpuDepthQuery answers null for three.js's own XRWebGLBinding.getDepthInformation on a CPU-depth session (it throws there and would stop the loop). DepthOccluder: every frame frame.getDepthInformation(view) is turned into metres in an R32F texture and a full-screen pass drawn first (renderOrder −1000, colour off, depth test on with AlwaysDepth — with the test off WebGL writes no depth) writes it into the depth buffer through normDepthBufferFromNormView (normalized view y from the top) with an 8 cm bias, so posts behind a real wall or sofa fail the depth test while shadows and the reticle on the floor stay. Checked off-device with a fake 1 m depth map: a box at 0,6 m drawn, one at 3 m hidden; not yet tried on ARCore. The AI fan's Strumenti shows «Profondità» once a depth map arrives (off: objects seen through walls again) and the diagnosis line says "profondità sì/no". The photo with ARCore keeps the occlusion too.
- ARCore depth off again (2026-11-16, screenshot "no peggio di prima"): on the user's Xperia the AR with depth-sensing came out worse than without, so the depth is no longer asked (ArLive AR_DEPTH false: no depth try, no occluder, no «Profondità» in the fan); views/ar-depth stays in the code, true brings it back.
- ARCore depth as a test (2026-11-16, as asked after "tremavano" and "sparivano nei posti sbagliati"; supersedes "ARCore depth off again"): the depth is asked only when its test is on (views/ar-depth depthWanted / setDepthWanted, localStorage mdt-ar-depth-v1, off by default). In AR with ARCore the AI fan's Strumenti has «Profondità (prova)» / «Spegni profondità», which restarts the session (ArCamera rotateLive through onRotate) with or without depth-sensing; with a depth map arriving it adds «Gira mappa n/4» (depthFlip / setDepthFlip, mdt-ar-depth-flip-v1: 0 y from the top, 1 as the framebuffer, 2 and 3 the same mirrored sideways — the shader's flip uniform, to find by trying the orientation that hides posts behind people and furniture) and «Senza copertura / Con copertura» to compare without restarting.
- ARCore depth on by default (2026-11-16, as asked "metti la profondità, gli oggetti devono riconoscere l'ambiente circostante"; refines "ARCore depth as a test"): depthWanted is true unless this phone stored "off" (mdt-ar-depth-v1 "on" / "off"), so every ARCore session asks depth-sensing first and real walls and furniture hide the objects; the fan's «Spegni profondità» / «Profondità» restarts the session without or with it, «Gira mappa n/4» and «Senza / Con copertura» stay.
- Depth map read the other way up (2026-11-16, screenshot "così sparisce tutto": the pergola and half the reticle hidden): the map's default orientation is now 1, the framebuffer's own y (depthFlip default 1, key mdt-ar-depth-flip-v2 so an earlier choice does not stick), and the margin is 12 cm plus 5 % of the distance, as the depth map is coarse and noisier far away; «Gira mappa n/4» still tries the others.
- Pergola put by its near side (2026-11-16, "sospeso ma solo il pergolato": every other object stands on the tapped spot, the pergola's posts fell 2 m beyond it, behind desks and walls): in ArLive placeAtHit a pergola (and a shade cloth) is put with the middle of its near side on the tapped spot, turned to face the phone (centre moved along the phone→spot direction by half its depth less 12 cm, times the model scale; rotation.y from that direction, PlacedObject yaw = −rotation.y for footprintRing), so its two front posts stand where one looks.
- Realistic trees and ARCore's light in the AR (2026-11-16, as asked after the 3D library advice; "nel caso torniamo a prima": revert this commit to go back): three.js stays. (1) views/ar-tree realTree: EZ-Tree (@dgreenheck/ez-tree 1.1.0, MIT, credited in Licenze; imported from its sources through the vite alias "ez-tree-src", as its build inlines 3 MB of textures — the bark and leaf pictures are separate files, left out of the precache like the rest of the AR) grows a procedural tree with bark and leaf textures for «Albero» and the recognised plants (not vines): conical/columnar → Pine, umbrella → Ash, shrub → Bush 1, deciduous → Aspen, else Oak, Small/Medium/Large by height; scaled to the object's real height with its foot at y = 0, the crown squeezed to its width (0,35–1,6×); broadleaves keep 70 % of their leaves, conifers all; a new seed each time. ArLive placeAtHit puts the simple shape at once and swaps it for the grown tree when ready (contact shadow kept); if EZ-Tree fails the simple shape stays. Checked off-device: oak 9 m and pine 10 m stand from y 0 to their height, ~26 k triangles for two. (2) every ARCore session asks the optional "light-estimation": three's XREstimatedLight lights the scene with the room's or the piazza's light (the fixed hemisphere and sun hidden while it runs, back when it stops) and its reflections become the scene's environment once they arrive.
- No rotate notice in AR (2026-11-16, as asked "non mi deve uscire questo avviso quando ruoto"): ArLive ROTATE_PROMPT false — turning the phone during an ARCore session no longer shows "Tocca per ruotare la vista AR"; the AR stays in the orientation it started in (Chrome's lock; a restart without a tap would be refused). true brings the notice and its restart back.
- Realistic pergola in the AR (2026-11-16, as asked "prepara il pergolato"; supersedes the box pergola of "AR pergola on the ground" and "AR pergola without the floor sheet"): views/ar-pergola realPergola, built in code with nothing to download — larch-brown timber with its own grain (a 256×512 canvas of wavy grain lines, knots and weathering, drawn once; the grain along each piece), posts every 3 m or less on the two long sides on steel base plates with side cheeks over concrete footings flush with the floor (each foot a small round shadow), a pair of main beams clamping each line of posts and running past the end posts, 45° knee braces, bolt heads, rafters every 60 cm overhanging the beams, battens every 25 cm on top; merged into one mesh per material (8 draw calls, ~1.900 triangles for 4×4 m). views/ar-object-meshes "pergolato" uses it; placement (near side on the tapped spot, facing the phone) unchanged.
- Climbers on the AR pergola (2026-11-16, as asked "rampicanti"): views/ar-pergola climbers adds a wisteria-like plant — leaf clusters (a canvas of nine leaves in five greens with veins, drawn once, alpha-tested, double-sided) over about two thirds of the roof and thicker along the beams, short curtains drooping from the long edges, stems winding up two corner posts and a middle one, and 46 hanging lilac flower bunches under the roof; one InstancedMesh for the leaves (~800) and one for the flowers, each its own shade (10 draw calls, ~3.900 triangles for the whole pergola).
- Real shadows in the AR (2026-11-16, "È calcolata l'ombra?": before, only small round discs under the feet): views/ar-shadow ArShadows — a light of no brightness of its own that only casts (the scene's lighting unchanged), PCF soft shadow map 1024², its box following the objects put down (up to 30 m), from the direction of the strongest real light ARCore estimates (XREstimatedLight's directional light, kept at least ~10° high), else a high sun; an invisible ShadowMaterial floor (38 %) at the floor's height receives it, so the pergola, its climbers, trees and every object throw their real shadow on the real floor, in the ARCore view and in the preview. castShadows marks every solid mesh (see-through veils and the foot discs excluded).
- Realistic bed and misting pole in the AR (2026-11-16, as asked "cambia il modello 3D anche al prato e piantalo a terreno, stessa cosa per i nebulizzatori"; supersedes their box shapes): views/ar-bed realBed — "Aiuola" dug into the floor: a border of stones sunk 5 cm (instanced, each its own grey), a band of round pebbles half buried, dark soil level with the floor (canvas texture), tufts of grass (crossed alpha-tested blades drawn once on a canvas, up to 900 instanced, each its own green) and small flowers on stems (up to 70); views/ar-mister realMister — "Nebulizzatore" on a concrete footing flush with the floor, steel flange with four bolts and a collar, brushed pole with its water pipe and clips beside it, a crown of six arms with brass nozzles, and live mist: 360 droplets leaving the nozzles, slowed by the air, settling and fading (Points moved once a frame). Neither gets the round contact shadow; both cast real shadows (ArShadows).
- Tagline (2026-11-16, as asked): the tutorial's hello reads "Simula gli interventi di raffrescamento, prima di implementarli." (views/v10/intro.tsx, was "Prova gli interventi prima di farli.").
- Dev map registry (2026-11-16): in dev only (import.meta.env.DEV), SatelliteMap2D registers each map on window.__mdtMaps so the demo-video scripts can drive it (the wide shot and the flyTo zoom onto the drawn area); production builds carry nothing.
- Intro arrow under the hello (2026-11-17, as asked): on phones held upright the hello's white arrow (views/navigation-arrows NavigationArrows, fixed) is placed about 18 px under its last line ([data-intro-lead], "Ecco come funziona, in quattro passi."), measured on mount, 700 ms later and on resize, at most 86 % of the screen, so the longer three-line tagline never leaves it on the text; elsewhere the CSS position stays.
- Benvenuto tiles (2026-11-17, as asked): the Piazza and Vigneto tiles read "Simula gli interventi in città" / "Simula gli interventi in vigna" (were "Prova gli interventi …"), in line with the new tagline.
- In-app camera multiple shot like the AI camera's (2026-11-17, as asked; supersedes the in-app camera's "Singolo | Multiplo" switch, its strip of shots with bins and "Migliore", its "Fine · n foto" and the ✕ question "Hai scattato n foto"): SystemCamera has no switch; a tap takes one photo (and closes, as before), holding the shutter shoots a series until the finger lifts and the camera stays open (mode "burst", asked by name, keeps every tap in the series). The series shows as views/BurstReview BurstThumb in CameraCaptureControl's left slot beside the shutter (its last photo, the count in a turquoise badge; upright and sideways alike); a tap opens the shared BurstReviewSheet (grid, a tap sets a photo aside, "Scarta tutte", "Aggiungi n" hands the kept ones over and the camera stays open). Closing the camera any way (✕, Back, swipe) or taking a single photo hands the kept ones over too. BurstReview's thumb and sheet take BurstPreview (id, thumb, src), since the in-app camera's files are still promises while they encode.
- AI recognition and AR trees by genus (2026-11-17, as asked "migliora il riconoscimento delle piante"): the scan sends where and when it was taken (ArCamera request: place — the flow's chosen location, rounded to 0,01° (about 1 km) in the prompt with its label, none while no place is chosen — and month; controllers/ar-detect input place/month) and the prompt adds models/ar-recognition placePrompt: among look-alike species prefer those grown or wild in that area and consistent with the season, only as a tie-breaker, the visible characters decide. In the AR a recognised plant is drawn after its genus (models/plant-profile plantProfile: about 50 genera — pines, firs, cedars, cypresses, junipers, thujas, birch, plane, poplar, willow, oaks with the evergreen ones darker, beech, chestnut, ash, robinia, lime, maple, hackberry, elm, horse chestnut, olive, laurel, holly, magnolia, eucalyptus, citrus… — each with EZ-Tree base shape, bark, leaves, tints, leaf share and size, a crown width used only without the species' own; Pinus pinea umbrella; unknown genera by form as before; presetName), views/ar-tree applying it and turning EZ-Tree's yellow aspen leaves green once (canvas hue-rotate, shared). The plain «Albero» stays the oak. src/test/plant-profile.test.ts.
- Stone pine model (2026-11-17, as asked "crea un modello apposta per il pino domestico"): views/ar-pine realStonePine, built in code with nothing to download — a tall bare trunk with a slight lean and red-brown plate bark (canvas), forking at about three quarters of the crown's base into 4–6 upward limbs with a secondary split each, under a broad flat umbrella crown (a dome about a quarter of the height thick, flat underneath: a dark inner half-sphere and underside so it never looks see-through, plus up to 2400 instanced needle tufts — crossed alpha-tested planes of a drawn needle tuft, each its own green — denser at the rim, some along its underside); the whole tree scaled to exactly the species' height, crown as wide as its crown size; a seed varies lean, limbs and tufts. models/plant-profile gives Pinus pinea, and any pine read as umbrella-shaped, custom "stonePine", which views/ar-tree builds instead of EZ-Tree (src/test/plant-profile.test.ts).
- Italian cypress model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso per il cipresso"): views/ar-cypress realCypress, built in code like the stone pine — a narrow flame-shaped column (cypressRadius: about 42 % of its widest at the foot, widest about 28 % up, tapering to a point), so dense it is all foliage: a dark inner lathe of the outline, a little lumpy, under up to 2600 instanced upright sprays of scale-leaves (crossed alpha-tested planes of a drawn spray, each its own dark cool green, denser where the flame is widest), a short fibrous grey-brown trunk showing only at the foot and a slight sway of the top; exactly the species' height, its widest point the crown size kept between 8 % and 30 % of the height. models/plant-profile gives Cupressus sempervirens, and any Cupressus read as columnar, custom "cypress", built by views/ar-tree instead of EZ-Tree; other cypresses (arizonica…) and junipers keep the conifer one (src/test/plant-profile.test.ts).
- Olive model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso per l'olivo"): views/ar-olive realOlive, built in code like the stone pine and the cypress (sharing ar-pine's rng and limb, now exported) — a short, thick, gnarled trunk (gnarl: each ring of the tube pushed in and out in ridges with a slow twist and a slight foot flare; pale grey bark with a network of dark fissures, canvas) leaning and splitting at about a quarter of the height into 3–4 main limbs opening like a vase, each with 2–3 branches ending in a rounded clump of leaves, plus one over the middle; each clump a grey-green inner mass under leafy twigs (crossed alpha-tested planes of a drawn twig of narrow lance-shaped leaves, up to 3600 instanced, grey-green with about one in six silver, the underside turned); crown 0,8–1,6× the height across, exactly the species' height, standing on y = 0. models/plant-profile gives Olea custom "olive", built by views/ar-tree instead of EZ-Tree.
- Every recognised plant built from its shape (2026-11-17, as asked "procedi con 2, 3 e 4, senza TRY"; supersedes the EZ-Tree profile by genus of "AI recognition and AR trees by genus" — models/plant-profile is gone): (2) the scan asks the AI for plant.specimen — the photographed specimen as seen, each value null when not judged: crown columnar|conical|ovoid|round|spreading|umbrella|vase|weeping|irregular|shrub, widthRatio (crown width / height), density 0–1, clearTrunk (share of bare trunk), stems, leanDeg, leaf needle|scale|broad|compound|narrow, leafColor and barkColor #rrggbb (models/ar-recognition schema, withPlantAlternatives defaulting it to null; models/plant-shape cleanSpecimen clamps it; Detection.species.specimen; kept in the scan log ScanSpecies.specimen and in the AR object ArObjectDef.plant.specimen). (3) views/ar-plant realPlant builds any plant from a TreeShape: stems leaning as seen, bare up to the crown, a crown of that outline (crownRadius, crownDepth) — cones and columns as one continuous leafy surface, every other outline as rounded clumps of leaves spread evenly (golden angle, one height band each) at the ends of branches with gaps where the branches show, more and overlapping for shrubs, curtains of thin hanging leaves from the rim for a weeping crown — leaf tufts of the kind's drawn texture (needles and scale-leaves and narrow leaves shared with ar-pine, ar-cypress, ar-olive; broad and compound drawn here) in the shape's greens, bark plain, patchy (plane tree), white (birch) or fissured; exactly the species' height. (4) models/plant-shape shapeFor merges: the specimen's crown, width, density, bare trunk, stems and lean first; the card of about sixty species and genera common in Italian squares and vineyards (CARDS: tilia, platanus, celtis, acer, ulmus, aesculus, quercus — ilex, suber, pubescens —, populus — nigra columnar, alba —, salix babylonica weeping, fraxinus, robinia, sophora, albizia, melia, magnolia, laurus, ligustrum, ficus, prunus, lagerstroemia, nerium, pittosporum, rosmarinus, lavandula, pines, cedrus, abies, picea, larix, juniperus, thuja, taxus…) for the leaf kind and the colours (the specimen's colours only tint them by a third, as a photo's light changes them); the form for any other species. customModel keeps the stone pine, the Italian cypress and the olive on their own models; views/ar-tree uses realPlant for every other recognised plant (the crown width from the species' typical crown when the AI did not judge the specimen's), and EZ-Tree only for the plain «Albero» (an oak). src/test/plant-shape.test.ts.
- Lime tree model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso modello apposta per il tiglio"): views/ar-linden realLinden, built in code like the stone pine, cypress and olive — a tall straight trunk with a slight sway, grey bark of fine interlacing ridges (canvas), a flared foot with burrs and short suckers, bare to about a quarter of the height, running on as a leader; 9–17 scaffold branches climbing steeply and arching out (golden angle), each ending in a clump of leaves with a side clump on the way, a clump on top and a drooping ring at the lower rim; a dense egg-shaped crown (lindenRadius: broadest a little below the middle) over one egg-shaped inner body (a lathe at 70 %, slightly lumpy), half the leaves spread evenly over its surface and half in the clumps, all kept within the egg; leaves are twigs of heart-shaped, toothed, veined leaves (canvas), mid green with about one in seven paler (the underside); the crown 0,45–0,9× the height across, exactly the species' height. models/plant-shape customModel gives Tilia "linden", built by views/ar-tree.
- Plane tree model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso per il platano"): views/ar-plane realPlane, built in code like the other own models — a massive straight trunk in camouflage bark (canvas: a grey-olive ground under rounded flaking patches of cream, ochre and green-grey, some with a darker lifted rim) with a flared foot, forking at about a third of the height into 4–6 heavy limbs radiating up and out, each splitting in three; a broad irregular dome (planeRadius: widest about a third of the way up), airier than the lime's — a smaller dark inner dome so the pale limbs show — with a third of the leaves over the dome and two thirds in the clumps at the limbs' ends, all kept within it; leaves are twigs of large palmate five-lobed leaves with shallow sinuses (canvas), mid green with some paler; seed balls (brown spheres on long stalks, up to 70) hang under the outer clumps; the crown 0,6–1,15× the height across, exactly the species' height. models/plant-shape customModel gives Platanus "plane", built by views/ar-tree.
- Holm oak model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso per il leccio"): views/ar-holm realHolmOak, built in code like the other own models — a short stout trunk of near-black bark cracked into small square plates (canvas), leaning a little, now and then two stems from the ground (about one in three), splitting at about a fifth of the height into 3–5 limbs per stem, each splitting in two; a very dense round evergreen dome that comes down low (holmRadius: widest a little below the middle, still over half as wide at its foot), a dark inner body nearly as big as the crown so no sky shows, a ring of clumps round the lower rim; leaves are twigs crowded with small oval leathery leaves, some with a few spiny teeth (canvas), very dark green with about one in ten grey (the felted underside); the crown 0,7–1,2× the height across, exactly the species' height. models/plant-shape customModel gives Quercus ilex (and Q. rotundifolia) "holmOak", built by views/ar-tree; the other oaks keep the generator and their cards.
- Own models for the common plants (2026-11-17, as asked "fai per tutte le piante e alberi che mi suggerisci"): views/ar-broadleaf buildBroadleaf, the builder of the broadleaved own models (stems leaning as set, scaffold branches arching to the outline with clumps at their ends and on the way, an inner body of the outline, leaves over the outline and in clumps kept within it, fountain-like curtains for a weeping crown, extras), with drawn() (canvas textures, cached) and dots() (instanced flowers and fruits); views/ar-species SPECIES gives it a card each (outline, proportions, trunk, branches, drawn bark and leaves, colours, extras) for hackberry (Celtis), southern magnolia (Magnolia, white flowers), horse chestnut (Aesculus, digitate leaves and white candles), maple (Acer but negundo, palmate leaves, a few red), birch (Betula, white bark with black lenticels, light crown), deciduous oaks (Quercus but ilex, rotundifolia and suber, lobed leaves, wide crown), weeping willow (Salix babylonica / sepulcralis / pendula, curtains), Lombardy poplar (Populus nigra or a columnar Populus) and oleander (Nerium, many stems, pink, white or red flowers); the dome outline closes at the top (dome: above = 1 − m — an earlier version left every dome flat-topped). Three models apart: views/ar-cedar realCedar (Cedrus: a straight dark trunk to a nodding leader, tiers of long flat branches, the lower longest, needle tufts lying flat, blue-green; cedarRadius), views/ar-palm realPalm (Phoenix, Washingtonia, Trachycarpus, Chamaerops, Butia, Syagrus, Livistona, Sabal: a thick trunk of diamond leaf-base scars swelling into a pineapple, 60–80 pinnate fronds as bent strips folded in a V on a drawn frond picture, young ones standing, old ones arching out and down, the oldest yellow), views/ar-vine realVine (Vitis: a stretch of trained row w long — end posts, four wires, a vine about every metre with a gnarled trunk to the first wire, a spurred cordon each way, shoots up into a leaf wall of drawn five-lobed toothed leaves and dark purple bunches of berries); views/ar-tree wantsRealTree now lets the grapevine through. models/plant-shape customModel maps all of them (about twenty plants with a model of their own); the rest keep views/ar-plant. src/test/plant-shape.test.ts.
- Weeping willow model (2026-11-17, as asked "migliora il salice piangente"; supersedes its card in views/ar-species, which made a haystack): views/ar-willow realWillow — a short thick leaning trunk of deep ridged grey-brown bark splitting at about a fifth of the height into 4–6 great limbs that climb then arch over and down like umbrella ribs, each with four side arcs; hundreds of hanging twigs (up to 520 strands) from points along them, straight down in segments with a slight sway and outward drift, the outer ones down to a little above the ground, the inner ones shorter so trunk and limbs show between them, plus a cap of leaves over the limbs; the twig picture (canvas) is dense — narrow leaves filling most of its width — and drawn with alphaTest 0,28, as a sparse one vanished entirely on smaller or farther trees (alpha lost in the mipmaps); its instanced meshes are not frustum-culled; fresh yellow-green; about 0,85–1,4× the height across, exactly the species' height. models/plant-shape customModel "weepingWillow" (Salix babylonica, sepulcralis, pendula) is built by views/ar-tree with it.
- Hackberry model (2026-11-17, as asked "fai lo stesso per il bagolaro"; supersedes its card in views/ar-species): views/ar-celtis realCeltis — a stout trunk of smooth elephant-grey bark (canvas: soft blotches, fine horizontal wrinkles, dark lenticels), faintly fluted in five lobes and swollen at the foot (flute), slightly leaning, splitting at about a third of the height into 3–5 limbs climbing like a vase, each splitting in three; a broad rounded but uneven crown (celtisRadius, widest a little below the middle, closed at the top) over a lumpy inner body at 55 %, half the leaves over it and the rest in clumps, plus a fringe of drooping twigs at the lower rim; leaves are twigs of lance-shaped toothed leaves with a long curved tip and an uneven base (canvas), dark green, about one in eight grey-green (the underside); small dark purple drupes (up to 140) among the outer leaves; the crown 0,7–1,15× the height across, exactly the species' height. customModel "celtis" (genus Celtis) is built by views/ar-tree with it.
- Pergola cloth and ivy (2026-11-17, as asked "Per il pergolato metti un telo sopra le travi oltre che dell'edera"; after a brief return to the lilac climber the user chose the ivy again, "meglio edera"; supersedes the wisteria of "Climbers on the AR pergola"): views/ar-pergola adds shadeCloth — an off-white woven canvas (canvas texture) resting on the battens with a faint sag between them, covering the rafter ends, a rolled hem all round and a scalloped valance hanging from the two long sides; slightly see-through but writing depth, so it throws its real shadow (ArShadows) — and ivy (Hedera) instead of the lilac-flowered climber: glossy dark lobed leaves (canvas twig of 3- and 5-lobed leaves with pale veins, a rare reddish one) thick round every post (a few only started), a garland along the main beams, tufts over their ends and long trailing strands hanging from the beams and the cloth's edges; one InstancedMesh, not frustum-culled (~8.400 triangles for a 4×4 m pergola).
- Innaffiatoio in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "Inserisci anche un innaffiatoio e aggiorna gli interventi"): the piazza's AR interventions (models/ar-objects AR_OBJECTS urban, so the AI fan's Interventi with a Sprout icon) add "Innaffiatoio" — a pop-up rotary sprinkler, footprint the 6 m circle it waters (h 0,15 m), brought back to the map as an Aiuole zone (zone "prato", green permeable ground). views/ar-sprinkler realSprinkler: a dark green body ring flush with the floor, a grey riser, a black turret with two brass nozzles turning about once every 7 s, 700 water drops thrown in ~35° arcs from them landing all over the circle (points moved once a frame), and a darker wet patch under the throw; no round contact shadow. The simulation's own levers are unchanged.
- Innaffiatoi lever (2026-11-17, as asked "E le manopole dentro simulazione"): the piazza's Simula has a fifth lever "Innaffiatoi" (urban.sprinklers 0–20 by 1, Droplets, teal; hint "Bagnano aiuole e terreno: asciugando rinfrescano un poco l’aria, soprattutto nelle ore calde."): each cools the piazza's average by SPRINKLER_COOLING 0,05 °C (piazzaEstimate's fifth argument, the 5,2 °C cap shared; sliding the arc keeps misters and sprinklers and moves trees, aiuole and shade for the rest), costs 250–600 € (UNIT_COST.sprinkler, interventionCost, dashboard/report row "Innaffiatoi"), is kept with the place (place memory), the history (HistoryMix sprinklers, mixRows, summary, reopen, auto-history) and the CSV/GeoJSON export (sprinklers_count); on the map piazzaLayout places them spread over the aiuole (misterSpots on the lawn cells, else the paving off the pergolas; PiazzaLayout.sprinklers), drawn in 2D as fixed 5 px teal dots (sim-sprinklers) and in the open 3D as their 3 m wet circle; the SimBar legend adds "Innaffiatoi n"; Google 3D does not draw them yet. The AR's «Innaffiatoio» icon is now Droplets. src/test/sprinklers.test.ts.
- Map and AR in step (2026-11-17, as asked "Gli interventi sulla mappa si ritrovano nella modalità AR se sto inquadrando quel punto esatto … e viceversa"): once the AR floor is aligned with the map (the drawn area's two corners touched, else the phone's GPS and compass at start, said as approximate), views/ar-plan buildPlan puts the project's interventions in the real world on the very spots the map's layout gives them (models/area-layout piazzaLayout / vineLayout with the levers, zones and edits, so the same as the 2D and 3D maps): piazza trees (simple shapes, at most 30), aiuole as green ground, pergolas (realPergola sized and turned to each shade patch, at most 6), misting poles and sprinklers; vineyard shade cloths on posts, drip lines and main pipes as tubes, windbreak hedges. They follow the levers (planKey), cast real shadows, and the status line says "Dal progetto: 6 alberi · 2 pergolati …"; the AI fan's Strumenti has «Nascondi progetto» / «Mostra progetto». The other way (already there): objects put in AR go back to the map with «Riporta» as zones (the zone seeding then raises the levers), and sprinklers put in AR also add to the Innaffiatoi lever. src/test/ar-plan.test.ts.
- Realistic project in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "voglio la forma realistica"; refines "Map and AR in step"): the map's trees in AR are the realistic EZ-Tree «Albero» (views/ar-plan growPlan: the simple shape at once, then up to three grown trees cloned, each turned and sized a little differently, swapped in once ready and only while the plan is still on screen), and the aiuole the realistic bed (realBed sized and turned to each lawn patch, at most 8; past them or for a patch under 0,8 m the plain green ground). Pergolas, misting poles and sprinklers were already the realistic models.
- Realistic vineyard objects in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "fai realistici anche teli e frangivento nel vigneto"): «Telo d’ombra» is views/ar-shadenet realShadeNet — galvanised posts every 4 m or less round its edge on concrete feet, steel cables along the edges and across, a knitted dark green raschel net (canvas weave, alpha-tested, so it throws a dappled shadow) sagging a little between the cables, an edging tape and corner guy wires to ground anchors; «Frangivento» is views/ar-windbreak realWindbreak — a living hedge of shrubs about every 1,1 m, short trunks at the foot, lumpy dark bodies with an uneven top, covered in instanced sprays of small glossy leaves. Both are used when put by hand (ar-object-meshes) and for the map's project in AR (ar-plan: a net sized and turned to each cloth patch, at most 6, else the plain sheet; a hedge along each piece of the windbreak line, as tall as its strength).
- No caps on the project in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "Togli i 2 limiti"): views/ar-plan draws every tree, pergola, aiuola and shade net of the map's layout (the 30 trees, 6 pergolas, 8 beds and 6 nets caps are gone); the trees stay three grown trees cloned.
- One pergola per block in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "unisci i pergolati vicini in uno solo"): views/ar-plan pergolaBlocks joins the shade patches whose boxes, in the larger one's direction, overlap or lie within 0,6 m (the grid's row strips of one block, a zone and the strips round it) and puts one realPergola over each block, turned like its largest patch. realPergola gets middle lines of posts and paired beams every 4,5 m or less on a deep pergola (zRows; the ivy climbs and runs along them too), so a big one stands like a real structure. src/test/ar-plan.test.ts.
- Realistic drip irrigation and pergola merge chosen by hand (2026-11-17, as asked "Nel vigneto fai l'irrigazione realistica" and "fammi decidere a me se unirli"): views/ar-drip realDrip lays along a line of floor points a black 16 mm drip line waving a little on the soil (straight at its fitted ends), a dark green dripper every 50 cm with a darker wet patch under it and a drop forming and falling about every 1,1 s, a wire stake every 3 m; or (main) a straight 32 mm black pipe with a blue stripe and a tee every 6 m. Used by «Irrigazione» put by hand (a main pipe across its start feeding the drip line) and by the map's drip lines and main pipes in AR (ar-plan). The AI fan's Strumenti in the piazza's AR, with the project shown, has «Pergolati separati» / «Unisci pergolati»: joined (pergolaBlocks) or each shade patch its own pergola as on the map, kept on this phone (localStorage mdt-ar-merge-pergola-v1, joined unless turned off).
