- Pergolas separate by default in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "Separati"; refines the merge choice): the map's pergolas stand in AR each on its own shade patch as on the map, unless «Unisci pergolati» is chosen (mergePergolas reads mdt-ar-merge-pergola-v1 "on"; buildPlan's merge defaults to false).
- Short fan labels and the AI camera's tutorial (2026-11-17): in the AR fan's Strumenti the project tools read «Progetto» (turquoise while shown) and «Unisci» / «Separa» (aria says the whole), as longer names were cut; the piazza's AI camera tutorial no longer speaks of the removed target pills ("1 · Inquadra … la fotocamera capisce da sola cosa c’è", "2 · Scansiona …", step 3 also says the map's interventions show in place once aligned).
- Lighter opening (2026-11-17, as asked "Alleggerisci l'app senza toccare i modelli 3D"; measured on the production build, the piazza opened by an identified officer: what loads before the page is ready went from 1.359 KB of code + 300 KB of styles, 413 KB compressed, to 1.171 KB + 240 KB, 334 KB compressed, −19 %; the 3D models untouched): (1) the AI camera's styles (every rule whose selectors are all .ar-*, their media blocks and ar-* keyframes, ~65 KB) moved from styles.css to src/styles/ar-camera.css, imported by ArCamera, so they come with the camera's lazy chunk — .ar-zoom-lens(es) and .ar-close-swipe stay in styles.css, as the in-app camera uses them; screenshots of the camera before and after match; (2) the dashboard route's search check is a plain function, no longer zod, which the route settings dragged into every page (~85 KB); (3) loaded only when needed: the Sensori AI tab's SensorAdvisor and MySensors (LabFlows lazy, on the tab's first opening), the gallery review sheet (views/GalleryReviewLazy on the first pick, mounted closed then opened like ArCameraLazy, then kept mounted as it follows the upload), the identification form and intro of a locked section (ProfileGate lazy), and the simulation's ⋮ menu (views/v8/SimMenu with radix's dropdown and positioning, ~40 KB: SimShell shows a plain ⋮ button until the first tap — which loads and opens it at once — or until the page is idle). vite.config: MDT_SOURCEMAP=1 builds with source maps, for measuring chunk contents.
- Size kept for the next objects in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "gli elementi nuovi inseriti devono avere la stessa scala di dimensione del primo"): the size given with two fingers to the last object (ArLive sizeRef, 0,1–3×) becomes the size of every object put afterwards (its PlacedObject.scale, so «Riporta» brings it back to the map at that size; a pergola or a cloth put by its near side reaches by it too), and the status line says "<oggetto> · n m di altezza, in scala come il precedente (k %)"; pinching back to real size makes the next ones real size again.
- AI camera info card (2026-11-17, as asked): in portrait Rileva the card above the zoom ("Piante e suolo · Inquadra e avvia la scansione…") no longer shows on opening: it appears only with something to say (a scan's species or soil, a recognised species, AR); and while the zoom ruler is out (ZoomDial onRulerChange → zoomRulerActive) the card rises 2,6 rem with it (.ar-portrait-specimen-card.is-zoom-active), so the ruler never covers it.
- Camera back after AR (2026-11-17, "la fotocamera si blocca se esco da AR"): leaving AR from the Rileva pill restarted the AI camera while ARCore still held the phone's camera, which then gave no frames and the picture stood still. endLive now ends the session first and asks the camera again 400 ms after it has ended; getUserMedia after AR is retried up to 8 times 700 ms apart; and 2,5 s after a restart a watchdog checks that frames arrive (video readyState ≥ 2, currentTime moving, track live) — if not, the camera is asked again up to 4 times, then the camera's error card says "La fotocamera non è ripartita dopo l’AR. Tocca Riprova." with a new "Riprova" button (the camera restarted) beside "Usa la fotocamera di sistema".
- Realistic models in the open 3D and species trees (2026-11-17, as asked "usa i modelli reali 3D", both the map's 3D and the AR): views/ar-plan layoutPlan builds the realistic objects of a layout already worked out through any point mapper (buildPlan, the AR's, now calls it), and views/v10/three-layer (OpenMap3D, the open 3D) uses it instead of its prisms — pergolas with cloth and ivy, aiuole, misting poles with their mist, sprinklers, shade nets, hedges and drip lines, cast shadows on the shadow ground, vines still plant by plant —, redrawing about 20 times a second while mist or water moves (not while hidden), its points a few pixels each (the map's camera has no view space). Seen from afar (layoutPlan far): realBed caps its pebbles (1200 near, 300 far, wider and bigger past the cap — a 40 m strip of beds had taken half a million triangles, now ~22 k a bed) and draws its ground grass-green; realShadeNet is a see-through dark sheet (the weave read as a solid slab). The map's trees, in the AR and the open 3D, grow as the species recognised with the AI camera in that flow (ar-plan planTreeDefs: trees only, taken in turn, crown as wide as the 30 m² the simulation counts and height in the species' proportion, 4–16 m; growPlan one grown model per species), else the realistic oak; the AR's status line names them ("Dal progetto: 6 alberi (Tiglio, Platano) · …").
- Realistic models in Google's photographic 3D (2026-11-17, same request): views/v10/g3d-models builds the AR's realistic models — pergolas with cloth and ivy, aiuole (far), trees as the recognised species (else the oak, three variants), misting poles, sprinklers (new there), shade nets (far) and hedges — with three.js and hands them to LocationMap3D as <gmp-model-3d> in data: URLs (Google's renderer does not read blob: URLs, checked), each kind and size (rounded to half metres) built once per session: instanced meshes and node transforms baked (the map ignores node scales), points left out, only the colour map kept at most 512 px (EZ-Tree's leaves are waited for, up to 8 s, else a drawn leaf — exported before their image arrived they came out white), colours lifted a little. Placed RELATIVE_TO_GROUND at the drawing's hand adjustment (height, north, east), heading = −yaw in degrees (clockwise from north, checked with a marked bar), tilt 270; removed and laid again 250 ms after the levers stop like the shapes. With models available build3DOverlay(…, models) no longer draws the simple trees, pergolas, beds, misting pole heads, nets and hedges (ground shapes, shadows, mist veils and discs, vine zones and rows, lines stay); an older build without Model3DElement keeps the shapes. Also fixed: an unused misting-pole badge was never hidden (spreading Google's LatLngAltitude lost its lat/lng getters and threw), so poles taken away kept their number.
- Maps key per environment on Vercel (2026-11-17, "Vista 3D fotografica non disponibile ora" on the test previews): the browser key on Vercel was the project's first one, whose allowed sites include production but not the preview addresses (…-digital-twin-team.vercel.app: RefererNotAllowedMapError, so gm_authFailure and the simplified 3D). VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_BROWSER_KEY is now two variables: the old key for production only, the newer key (the one in .env.local) for preview, and the test branch's latest build was redeployed to take it. A Vite variable is read at build time: change it, then redeploy.
- Saves only for real changes (2026-11-17, as asked "se ruoto la mappa non deve salvare niente, solo se cambio luogo e faccio gli interventi"): models/lab-store setUrban / setVineyard drop a patch that changes nothing (unchanged: every patched key equal by JSON) — turning the map or the phone, or a view mounting again, rewrote the same values and each showed "Salvataggio… Salvato" —, and keep without the notice (emit quiet: localStorage and listeners, savedAt untouched) a GPS step within the same place (onlyNearMove: location alone, samePlace) and the season's air the clock lays on the vineyard's zones (a patch with climateKey). A place changed, a lever, a zone, the area or an imported weather still show it. src/test/lab-quiet-save.test.ts.
- Maps not rebuilt on every save (2026-11-17, as asked "ogni volta che salva fa il refresh della mappa… anche con il GPS"): SimMap keyed its 2D and 3D maps (and built them, through their lat/lng/label effects) on the exact coordinates and label of the place, so each GPS fix of a few metres or the same place saved again under its street's name remounted the map from scratch. It now anchors on the place (a ref kept until samePlace says the place really changed, about 30 m, or the place leaves / enters "pending"), and every map, pin fallback, pin-fix key and place-area lookup reads that anchor; a real change of place still opens the new place's map.
- Photographic 3D without reloads (2026-11-17, "lo fa nella mappa 3D ancora"): LocationMap3D's overlay is three layers, each laid again only when its own content changes — the drawing (area, zones, ground shapes; simKey without the shadows), the shadows (build3DOverlay layer "shadows", keyed on them alone, as they follow the simulation's clock every minute) and the realistic models, now kept in a pool keyed by kind, spot, heading and height: a change adds or removes only the models that changed, the others stay on the map (checked: a clock change kept 28 of 28 models). Before, any change took every shape and model away and laid them again, and the 3D seemed to reload. The ResizeObserver re-frames the camera only when the panel's width changes or its height by more than 15 % (40 px at least): the few pixels the page's bars take while scrolling put the camera back.
- GPS standing still (2026-11-17, screenshot: "Salvato…" and the 3D map gone blank with nothing done): with the GPS on, a phone lying still kept changing place — the "sharper fix" rule compared each fix with the accuracy of the last one applied, kept per mount and reset to unknown at every opening, so the next slightly better fix moved the place by tens of metres (often past samePlace's 30 m: a new place, a save, the map rebuilt, the area of the place left). Now each place keeps the accuracy of the fix that set it for the session (SimulationGrid placeAccuracy by coordinates); a sharper fix corrects only a coarse place (set from a fix worse than 100 m) and only past max(30 m, twice its error); otherwise the place moves only past max(100 m, twice the fix's error).
- GPS timeout not shown as an error (2026-11-17, screenshot of the red "Posizione non disponibile per ora…"): a fix that does not come within the watch's 20 s (indoors) no longer writes the red line once a place is set or a fix was had in this switch-on — the GPS just keeps trying; with no place yet it says "Sto ancora cercando la posizione: il GPS resta attivo e riprova da solo.", and every fix clears any earlier message (it used to stay on screen after the GPS was back).
- AR: modellino fitted, moving, obstacles (2026-11-17, as asked "nebulizzatori e innaffiatoi non sono in scala… calcola la scala ottimale… spostare gli oggetti… rileva la profondità", then "il cerchio blu… in proporzione… non fare sparire gli oggetti… riconoscere gli ostacoli"): three.js points ignore the object's scale, so the misting pole's mist, the sprinkler's water and the drip line's drops kept their full size in the «Modellino» and when pinched; views/ar-points pointsFollowScale sets their size from the object's world scale each frame (fixed-pixel points, the map's 3D, left alone). «Modellino» no longer fixes 1:10: switched on it fits a scale to the space (models/ar-objects modelScaleFor: the largest object placed or chosen fits in about half the space ahead — ARCore's depth map median, DepthOccluder.span(), else twice the distance to the floor spot — and stays lower than the phone above that floor; at most 1:2, rounded to 1:5, 1:10, 1:20…), said in the status line with the depth found. «Sposta» in the AI fan's Strumenti (once something is placed): a tap picks the nearest placed object (nearestPlaced, a violet ring under it), with ARCore it rides on the reticle, the next tap puts it down; «Riporta» uses the new spot; leaving the tool puts it back. The reticle is as wide as the chosen object's footprint at the size it would have (model and pinch; a small ring for the other tools), drawn after the depth pass without writing depth, so it never vanishes nor hides objects. Obstacles from ARCore's depth (DepthOccluder.depthAt, read like the occluding pass): points of the object's volume nearer to a real surface than to the phone mean something stands in the way — the reticle turns amber, a new object is brought towards the phone 15 cm at a time until free (said), else left with a warning; a move onto an obstacle says so. The occluding pass is more conservative (margin 22 cm + 8 % of the distance) so objects are not wrongly hidden. src/test/ar-model-scale.test.ts. Not tried on ARCore here.
- Aiuole joined in AR (2026-11-17, as asked "aggiungi Unisci anche per le aiuole"): in the piazza's AR, with the project shown, the AI fan's Strumenti has «Unisci aiuole» / «Aiuole unite» (Flower2) beside «Unisci pergolati» / «Pergolati uniti» (Fence): the map's lawn patches that touch or nearly touch become one realistic bed (ar-plan layoutPlan joinBeds, the same pergolaBlocks grouping), else each patch its own bed as on the map; kept on this phone (localStorage mdt-ar-merge-beds-v1, separate unless turned on). The fan's names wrap on two lines instead of being cut. src/test/ar-plan.test.ts.
- AR menu simpler, pergolas and aiuole join by coming close (2026-11-17, as asked "ripensare il menù AR… quando avvicino pergolato e aiuole un feedback adattivo"; the user chose "Ventaglio più semplice"; supersedes «Unisci pergolati / aiuole» in the fan and the separate-by-default choice): the AI fan keeps its three groups with fewer, whole names (items 5 rem wide, names on two lines): Strumenti is Misura, Sposta, Modellino (on while active, no "Grandezza vera"), Progetto, Profondità (no "Spegni …") or Pavimento; the joining toggles are gone and the depth map's test tools («Gira mappa», «Senza copertura») are behind DEPTH_TOOLS (false). The map's project in AR joins its touching pergolas and aiuole by itself (buildPlan merge and joinBeds always on). By hand: a pergola or an aiuola brought towards another of its kind — the one in hand with «Sposta» (it now follows the reticle in the preview too, a tap anywhere puts it down there) or the one about to be put at the reticle — gives an adaptive feedback (models/ar-objects JOINABLE, footGap, nearFeedback: from NEAR_GAP 2,5 m the other one is outlined yellow, brighter as the gap closes, the phone ticks faster and stronger, every 700 → 140 ms and 8 → 24 ms, the top line says "Vicino al pergolato: 1,2 m · avvicina ancora per unirli"; within JOIN_GAP 0,6 m the outline turns green with a double pulse, "Si uniscono: lascialo qui…"); put down there the two become one object covering both (joinFeet, turned like the larger; PlacedObject.from keeps them, «Annulla» separates them again; «Riporta» brings the joined one as one zone). src/test/ar-join.test.ts. Not tried on ARCore here.
- AR menu as a sheet (2026-11-17, as asked "rifacciamo il menù AR"; the user chose "Foglio a scomparsa"; supersedes the AI fan of "AI fan instead of the drawer" and "AR menu simpler"): the AI button (sparkles) in AR opens views/ArLive ArSheet — a slate sheet rising from the bottom over the dimmed camera (oklch 0.25, rounded top, handle, "Cosa vuoi fare?" and ✕; .ar-sheet slides up, none with reduced motion; up to 78 % of the height, 92 % in landscape, centred at most 32 rem wide, scrolling inside) with one section per group: "Piante riconosciute dall’AI" (green ring, scientific name and height), "Interventi" (each with one line on what it is for, OBJECT_HINT, and its size) and "Strumenti" (Misura, Sposta, Modellino, Progetto, Profondità or Pavimento, each with what it does and its state); rows have the icon in a round tile, the whole name and the line, the current choice turquoise with a check (aria-pressed). A tap picks and closes it; a tap on the veil or ✕ closes it. The fan (AiFan) is gone. src/test/ar-sheet.test.tsx.
- AI camera never stays frozen (2026-11-18, "quando esco da AR si blocca la fotocamera AI"; refines "Camera back after AR"): the one-off check 2,5 s after a restart is replaced by a watchdog that runs as long as the AI camera is open (ArCamera's camera effect): every 0,5 s, when the page is in front, not scanning and not in the AR view, it reads the video's time; still for 2,5 s it asks the video to play, still for 4 s it asks the camera again (silently, up to 5 times in a row, the count reset once the picture has run a second), then "La fotocamera non è ripartita. Tocca Riprova."; an ended video track restarts the camera at once. After an ARCore session the camera is asked again 900 ms (was 400) after the session has ended. Not tried on ARCore here.
- Flash without freezing the camera (2026-11-18, "anche quando uso il flash si blocca"): lib/camera-torch setCameraTorch's fallback (a phone refusing the advanced torch constraint) applied {torch} alone, which replaces every constraint of the track — the camera's size and frame rate were dropped and some Android phones restarted the camera with the picture standing still; it now keeps the track's own constraints and adds the torch (also for the in-app camera and the AR preview). The AI camera asks the video to play again after each torch switch, keeps the flash the person turned on across the watchdog's restarts (torchKeepRef, put back on the new camera 0,6 s in; cleared on leaving AR), the watchdog also watches the AR preview without ARCore (it shares the camera; skipped only during an ARCore session), and the flash from ARCore (flashPreview) asks the camera again 900 ms after the session has ended, like leaving AR. Not tried on the user's phone here.
- Release to main (2026-11-18, as asked "copia tutte le modifiche della fotocamera AI - AR dentro main con la fotocamera AI disattivata"; the user chose to bring the whole test branch, as the AI/AR work is woven into the maps, levers, GPS and saves): main (production) gets every commit of main-sgme1w plus one commit on top that sets lib/ai-standby AI_STANDBY true, so in production the AI camera — and the AR inside it — reads "In sviluppo" and does not open; main-sgme1w keeps AI_STANDBY false for the tests. Bringing later work to main: merge main-sgme1w and keep AI_STANDBY true there.
- 2D map turns like the 3D (2026-11-18, as asked "la mappa 2D devo poterla ruotare come la mappa 3D"; supersedes "rotation off, as Google's satellite map does not turn" in SatelliteMap2D): two fingers (or a right-drag) turn the 2D map; Google's satellite base, which cannot turn by itself, is turned on screen by the same angle round the box's centre (the point both maps are centred on) — while turned its layer is a square as wide as the box's diagonal, centred, so every corner stays covered; facing north it is the box itself, so Google's own logo and credits stay in its corners, and while turned a small "Google · Immagini e dati mappa" credit sits bottom left. Turned, a round compass under the recenter button (Navigation2, its red arrow pointing north) turns the map back with north up; the recenter button and the area framing also bring north up.
- AR objects moved and turned by touch too (2026-11-18, as asked "ruotare e spostare gli oggetti sia con lo spostamento della fotocamera … sia con il touch … feedback adattivi"): in ArLive (ARCore and the preview alike, tools Metti and Sposta-idle; not while measuring or aligning, nor on the HUD's controls) one finger on a placed object — found by its 3D shape under the finger (screenRay, a tree by its crown too), else by its footprint near the floor spot (screenToFloor: the floor ARCore found or the preview's guessed one) — drags it along the floor after 8 px, keeping the finger's offset; two fingers on an object (else the one dragged, else the last put down) turn it with a twist (clockwise on screen = clockwise from above) and resize it with a pinch (0,1–3×, as before). Feedback while dragging: a violet ring under it, a tick every half metre longer for a heavier object (models/ar-objects dragTick, 8–22 ms), a firm buzz and "qui c’è un ostacolo davanti" when ARCore's depth sees something in the way, "Sposto <oggetto> · n m" in the top line, and the pergola/aiuola join outline and pulses (proximity also follows the touched object), the drop joining them like Sposta; while turning, a tick every 15° and a firmer one on each quarter turn (turnDetents), the angle said with the height. «Sposta» with the reticle now also turns the object as the phone turns (camYaw: the object keeps its angle to the phone; the same 15°/90° ticks); the yaw is kept on drop and put back when leaving the tool. Taps around a drag or twist never put a new object (pinchUntil). src/test/ar-join.test.ts. Not tried on ARCore here.
- AI camera tutorial updated (2026-11-18, as asked): six steps for both flows — Inquadra, Scansiona (urban: «Aggiungi al progetto» brings it to the map), AR (the AI button opens «Cosa vuoi fare?», pick an intervention or a recognised plant, tap the floor), Sposta e ruota (one finger drags, two fingers turn and resize, «Sposta» follows the reticle and turns with the phone; vibrations every half metre and 15°, stronger at an obstacle), Unisci e riporta (urban: pergolas/aiuole join when brought close, «Modellino», «Riporta», the map's interventions in place once aligned; vineyard: «Riporta» only) and Scatta (tap for one photo, hold for a multiple shot). The "seen" keys moved to ar-tutorial-seen-urban-v3 / ar-tutorial-seen-v3, so it shows once more to everyone.
- AI camera tutorial closes from outside (2026-11-18, as asked): a tap on the blurred veil around the tutorial card closes it like «Fine» (closeGuide, marking it seen); the card itself keeps its swipes and buttons.
- Rileva without Analisi, Livelli and the heat map (2026-11-18, as asked "Analisi, livelli e termico, che facciamo?"; the user chose the four-point plan): the simulated heat map (the flame, its legend and its "Livelli" button — a picture not measured by anything) can no longer be turned on, the "Livelli" and "Analisi" buttons are gone, the detailed result card opens by itself once a scan has found a plant, soil or a survey (foldable to a line, closable with ✕; it now carries the "Scansione n di N" pager), and the button bottom right ("Strumenti", sliders) is «Scansioni» (ClipboardList): it opens the AR's own sheet (ArLive ArSheet, "Cosa vuoi fare?") with Esito dell’ultima scansione, Scansioni fatte · n (the card on the newest record, paged from there), Togli l’ultimo esemplare and Elimina tutto (asked as before); with nothing scanned yet one row says how to start. The thermal drawing code stays, unreachable. The tutorial's step 2 says the result opens by itself and the button right of the shutter reopens the scans.
- Lighter opening, second pass (2026-11-18, as asked "Alleggerisci l'app"; measured on the production build, the piazza opened by an identified officer: JavaScript before the page's load event 1.172 → 1.111 KB, the history piece 101 → 72 KB): loaded only when needed — the simulation's arc (UrbanThermometer / VineThirst, React.lazy in LabFlows, so the piazza no longer loads the vineyard's and the reverse; SimMap's VineZonePicker lazy too), the in-app camera (views/SystemCameraLazy, mounted on its first opening and kept), «Esporta report» (views/v10/ExportSheetLazy from the dashboard, on its first opening, then kept for its background pill) and "Dispositivi collegati" (AccountDevices lazy in the account panel); the share-window file rules (forShare, zoneOfName, MIME) moved to lib/share-files so the history no longer pulls the whole ExportSheet.
- «Scansioni» only once there are scans (2026-11-18, as asked "nascondilo"): in Rileva the button right of the shutter is shown only when its sheet has something (the last result, scans recorded, specimens), with the number of scans in a small badge on its corner; with nothing scanned only the scan button and the shutter show (the empty-state sheet is gone). The tutorial's step 2 says the button appears from the first scan.
- Scan card closed and called back (2026-11-18, as asked "fammi richiamare questa Tab se la tolgo, metti un'opzione nel cassetto"): the card above the shutter in Rileva (the scan's species or soil with «Aggiungi al progetto») has a ✕ in its corner (beside the swipe that already took it away; a toast says where to find it again), and while it is hidden the «Scansioni» sheet lists first "Mostra la scheda", which puts it back.
- AR touch fixed (2026-11-18, "non riesco a scalare più gli oggetti e a ruotarli"): ArLive's touch gesture listened on the window while bubbling, and the camera's own gesture layer stops touchstart before it gets there, so no drag, twist nor pinch ever began; the listeners are now captured (capture: true), and the control check no longer counts "[role=dialog]" (the whole AI camera is a dialog) — only buttons, links, fields, sliders, the AR sheet, the tutorial and the multiple-shot sheet. Checked on a simulated phone: two fingers on a placed tree resize and turn it.
- Light 3D on iPhone and iPad (2026-11-18, "su iPhone il 3D si blocca se faccio un intervento nella simulazione"): Safari gives WebGL little memory, and laying the realistic models (g3d-models GLB in data: URLs in Google's photographic 3D, three.js's layoutPlan in the open 3D) froze the 3D at the first lever moved. lib/ios isIOS (iPhone, iPod, iPad also when it reads as a Mac with touch): LocationMap3D LITE_3D draws the light shapes (build3DOverlay without models, no Model3DElement pool) and OpenMap3D skips the three.js layer (the extruded slabs); Android and computers keep the realistic models. Not tried on an iPhone here.
- Realistic models on iPhone, light and by hand (2026-11-18, as asked "procedi con 2 e 3 solo per iPhone"; refines "Light 3D on iPhone and iPad"): on iPhone and iPad the 3D (photographic and open) shows plain shapes by default, and a switch under the map in 3D, "Modelli realistici · spenti: forme semplici / versione leggera per iPhone" (SimMap IosModelsSwitch, iOS only; lib/ios iosRealModels / setIosRealModels, localStorage mdt-ios-real-3d-v1; lib/use-ios-3d useModels3D → "real" elsewhere, "shapes" or "lite" on iOS), turns on their light version: Google 3D's models (g3d-models modelSpots lite) bake at most 220 copies of each instanced part (leaves, pebbles, flowers), the kept ones up to 1,6× bigger to cover the gaps, pictures at most 256 px, one tree variant, built one at a time with a pause (a queue) and cached apart ("lite:" keys); the open 3D's three.js layer (simThreeLayer lite) uses a 1024 shadow map and redraws mist and water 5 times a second instead of 20. Android and computers unchanged. Not tried on an iPhone here (src/test/ios-3d.test.ts covers the choice).
- Objects after ARCore's flash (2026-11-18, "se premo flash nella fotocamera AR non posso mettere oggetti"): the flash with ARCore leaves ARCore for the preview (flashPreview), and a phone that had never adjusted the floor opened that preview on «Regola pavimento» (ArLive adjusting: taps move the horizon line, put nothing, only a white "Fatto" at the bottom), so it looked as if objects could no longer be put. Coming from the flash (torch prop) the preview no longer opens on it, and the status line says "Flash acceso: sei nell’anteprima senza ARCore. Tocca il pavimento per posare gli oggetti; le misure sono indicative." Checked on a simulated phone with a fake torch: in the preview, objects are put before and after the flash. The ARCore path itself could not be tried here.
- Objects after the flash, second pass (2026-11-18, screenshot: after ARCore's flash the preview showed no reticle and taps put nothing): the preview's taps are read on the window while capturing (pointerdown/pointerup, a still tap under 12 px, controls, the AR sheet, the tutorial and the multiple-shot sheet left out) instead of on its canvas only; every AR renderer frees its WebGL context when it closes (forceContextLoss — after an ARCore session the phone could be out of contexts), and a preview canvas whose context is lost is built again (webglcontextlost → glLost). Checked on a simulated phone (taps before and after the flash, two-finger resize and turn); the phone's own ARCore path could not be tried here.
- Scan results in the drawer (2026-11-18, as asked "l'esito della scansione mettilo nel cassetto con possibilità di eliminare più esiti"; supersedes the auto-opening result card of "Rileva without Analisi, Livelli and the heat map"): the detailed result no longer opens over the camera after a scan; the «Scansioni» drawer is views/ScanSheet — "Scansioni · n" with every result of the flow, newest first (its photo, species or soil, one line, "Scansione n · hh:mm" and the specimens), a tap opening that result's full card (openEsito); «Seleziona» turns the rows into checkboxes with Tutti / Nessuno and a red "Elimina n", asked in place ("Eliminare questi n esiti? Non si possono recuperare.", Annulla | Elimina n) and done by models/ar-scan-log removeScans (photos dropped with them; deleting them all also clears the live result); under the list Mostra la scheda, Togli l’ultimo esemplare and Elimina tutto as before. A scan that fails or finds nothing shows for 4,5 s a pill above the shutter "Analisi non riuscita · dettagli in Scansioni" (a tap opens the drawer), and the drawer shows its message in amber on top. The quick card above the shutter with «Aggiungi al progetto» stays. Tutorials' step 2 updated.
- Scan never cut by the host (2026-11-18, screenshot "Analisi interrotta" with nothing scanned; the logs showed the recognition server function killed by Vercel at 60 s, "Task timed out after 60 seconds"): every AI call has a time limit (lib/ai-responses BUDGET_MS 46 s; Gemini: the first model FIRST_MS 30 s, then — on a timeout — the first fallback model, the lighter one, with what is left; the schema and overload retries only while time is left; OpenAI's request aborted at 46 s; a timeout answers 504 "Il servizio AI ha impiegato troppo: riprova la scansione.", never a pause of the service), the botanical checks (GBIF, Catalogue of Life) 5 s each, and detectArObject answers by 55 s whatever happens ("L’analisi ha impiegato troppo: riprova la scansione."), so the camera always gets a readable message in «Scansioni» instead of a cut request.
- A scan's result as a page of the drawer (2026-11-18, as asked "questo pannello si deve aprire cliccando la voce … e si va in un'altra pagina sempre dentro il cassetto con un'animazione a slide e si torna indietro con uno swipe"): in views/ScanSheet a tap on a result (e.g. "Noce") slides the drawer to its second page (the two pages side by side on a track, translate3d, 320 ms on cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1), none with reduced motion): "‹ Scansioni", the result's name and ✕ on top, then its full card (ArCamera recordEsito with its own "Scansione n di N": photo with the reference inset, species, cooling, specimens, soil, notes) and "Scorri verso destra per tornare alle scansioni"; the page follows a finger swiped to the right (touch-pan-y, a clearly sideways move past 10 px) and goes back past 70 px, else springs back; ‹ goes back too. The floating result panel over the camera is no longer opened from the drawer.
- Back swipe from right to left (2026-11-18, as asked "lo swipe di ritorno non funziona e fallo da destra verso sinistra"; refines "A scan's result as a page of the drawer"): the two pages of views/ScanSheet sit in one grid cell, each moved on its own (paneStyle); opening a result the list leaves to the left and the card comes from the right, then the list waits on the right; back is a swipe from right to left read with touch events (touchstart/move/end on the card's page — the pointer events used before never reached it on the phone; a mouse drag works too): the card follows the finger to the left with the list coming in from the right, past 70 px it finishes, shorter it springs back; ‹ Scansioni goes back the same way; the line under the card reads "Scorri verso sinistra per tornare alle scansioni". Checked with a simulated finger.
- Back drawn as a going back (2026-11-18, as asked "da destra verso sinistra tornando indietro non avanzando"; the user chose "Dito verso sinistra" with a back animation; refines "Back swipe from right to left"): in views/ScanSheet the finger still moves from right to left, but both pages shift to the right by as much as it has gone — the card slides away to the right and the list comes back from the left, where it went when the result opened —, past 70 px it finishes from there, shorter it springs back; ‹ Scansioni does the same back move.
- Back swipe from left to right (2026-11-18, as asked "da sinistra verso destra"; supersedes the finger direction of "Back swipe from right to left" and "Back drawn as a going back"): in views/ScanSheet the result's page goes back with the finger moving to the right — both pages follow it to the right (the card leaves to the right, the list comes back from the left), past 70 px it finishes from there, shorter it springs back; a leftward or vertical move is left alone; the line under the card reads "Scorri verso destra per tornare alle scansioni".
- Objects kept through ARCore's flash, reticle in scale (2026-11-18, as asked with a screenshot "quando attivo il flash gli oggetti che ho inserito scompaiono … il mirino … deve essere in scala"): the flash with ARCore remounts the AR view as the preview, which lost the objects put; now ArLive hands them over (carryOver: each placed object, joined ones included, set relative to where the phone stood and looked — the preview's camera stands at the origin looking along −z —, yaw turned by the phone's heading) through onFlashPreview → ArCamera liveAr.carry → the new ArLive's carry, which draws them again (addPlacedMesh: mesh, round shadow, grown tree) and keeps them in the list, said in the flash notice; the preview also draws the list again after a lost WebGL context. The reticle is in scale at every size, not only in «Modellino»: the chosen object's footprint at real size (or the model's or the size given with two fingers), up to 30 m across instead of 4 m, its line 6 % of the radius; in «Sposta» the footprint of the object in hand; a small ring only for measuring and aligning. The ARCore path could not be tried here.
- Dead camera code removed (2026-11-18, as asked "alleggerisci il codice"): views/ArCamera lost what no screen reached any more — the old floating thermal summary and its info dialog, the simulated heat map (thermal, its palette), the old grid «Prova» (toggleAr, handlePlace3DTree, the grid tap, the "Posa pianta" slot), the old multiple-shot strip and bar (burstShelfElement, burstBarElement, the carousel and lib/use-burst-dock with its test), the specimen balance and its controls, the framing hint, and the air-temperature request the balance made at each opening (2.695 → 2.040 lines); ArLive's unused chip. src/styles/ar-camera.css keeps only the rules of classes still used (69 dead classes and 4 unused keyframes dropped, 66 → 32 KB). The camera's lazy piece went from 81,6 to 77,2 KB compressed and its styles from 9,9 to 5,8 KB; screenshots before and after (Rileva, the Scansioni drawer, a result's page) match, all tests pass.
- Better floor in AR (2026-11-18, as asked "migliora il riconoscimento del piano di posa della fotocamera AR"): with ARCore the floor was the first hit ever seen, kept for good (often a table), the reticle took the hit's tilt (on a wall it stood up) and shook with every frame. Now (models/ar-floor, src/test/ar-floor.test.ts): every hit is read with how much it faces up (the pose's Y axis; flat ≥ 0,9, wall ≤ 0,42), the first flat one among ARCore's answers is taken, and FloorTracker keeps the flat hits of the last 20 s and calls floor the lowest 4 cm level that gathers at least 6 of them (a lone low hit is noise, a table above never becomes the floor), refined as more come — objects standing on the old floor follow it when it moves more than 3 cm; placementFor puts things on the floor, on a raised flat surface only in «Modellino», and when the hit is a wall, a slope or a table at real size on the floor straight under the screen's centre (the viewer's ray met with the floor, the reticle dimmer, 50 %); the reticle lies flat and is eased towards its spot (easeTo, 35 % a frame, straight there past 0,6 m) and holds the last good spot 400 ms instead of blinking; the status line says when the reticle is on a raised surface or on the floor under the point looked at, and the help says how to find the floor (light, a patterned floor; shiny or plain floors are hard). The preview without ARCore is unchanged. Not tried on ARCore here.
- AR after the flash, turned sideways, and lighter (2026-11-18, as asked "con il flash continua a non farmi mettere più oggetti; quando ruoto in orizzontale la hud rimane sempre in verticale; alleggerisci i processi AR, il telefono rallenta; non toccare i modelli 3D realistici"): (1) the preview's floor came from the phone's tilt read as beta − 90, right only with the phone upright — held sideways the floor was guessed far off and taps put nothing; it is now the back camera's elevation from beta and gamma (asin(−cos β · cos γ)), the same whichever way the phone is held. (2) After ARCore's flash the screen's orientation was not asked again (Chrome puts back the app's upright lock when a session ends), so the preview and its controls stayed upright: flashPreview now calls resetAppOrientation like leaving AR (at once and 600 ms later); and when the camera's surface is turned by CSS (no fullscreen), ArCamera turns the AR view's root the same way (same box and rotate as the surface) and ArLive maps every tap and touch back into the turned canvas (toCanvas; rotation prop). (3) Lighter, models untouched: the renderer draws at most at 1,5× the screen's pixels and ARCore's frame at 80 % (setFramebufferScaleFactor 0,8); the preview draws 30 frames a second; ArShadows draws its shadow map only when an object moved, turned, grew or the light changed (autoUpdate off, a signature per frame, at least once a second); ARCore's depth map is read every other frame; the diagnosis line's frame counter no longer re-renders the whole AR view every second unless its bar is open. Checked on a simulated phone (taps before and after the flash, and with a sideways orientation event); ARCore itself could not be tried here.
- ARCore retried after a spent tap (2026-11-18, screenshot "ARCore non è partito (overlay+pavimento+profondità: NotSupportedError · overlay+pavimento: SecurityError)"): on the user's phone the depth configuration was refused, and that refusal spent the tap's permission, so the next configuration was refused as SecurityError and the AR fell back to the preview. Now a depth configuration refused as NotSupportedError is remembered on this phone for a day (views/ar-depth markDepthRefused, localStorage mdt-ar-depth-na-v1; depthWanted false meanwhile; turning «Profondità» on by hand clears it), so the next start goes straight to the configuration the phone accepts; and when the preview comes from a failed start, a white «Riprova ARCore» button sits under the top bar (ArLive onRetry → ArCamera retryArCore: the preview and its torch closed, then startArCore — the AR start shared with the Rileva | AR switch — inside that fresh tap); the reason says to tap it. Not tried on ARCore here.
- Test builds labelled (2026-11-18, as asked "nel ramo test scrivi build test"): on a PREVIEW_BUILD (the test branch's Vercel previews, vite dev) the account panel reads "Versione dell’app · Build test MDTW-…" (lib/app-version buildLabel's test flag); production keeps "Build MDTW-…".
- AR HUD turns with the phone (2026-11-18, screenshot: in landscape the ARCore HUD stayed upright): Chrome keeps the screen in the orientation an ARCore session started in, so ArLive reads gravity (devicemotion) during the session and, held another way for 0,35 s, turns the HUD by CSS (±90° from screen.orientation.angle, its box swapped to the screen's height × width, centred, 250 ms) so buttons and text read upright; the camera view itself needs no turn. Not tried on ARCore here.
- AR sheet scrolls when turned, top buttons centred (2026-11-18, screenshot: in landscape the «Cosa vuoi fare?» sheet did not scroll): with the HUD turned (ARCore held sideways) or the preview's root turned by CSS, ArSheet takes turn (±90°): its height is capped at 92 % of the turned box (the screen's width; 78dvh of the screen's height overflowed it, so the list never had anything to scroll) and its list is scrolled by hand along the turned axis (touch listeners, touch-none).
- Top row back in place (2026-11-18, as asked "rimettimi i tasti dove stavano"): the AR's top row (Esci da AR, status, flash) is no longer centred in landscape nor while the HUD is turned: Esci da AR and the status on the left, the flash at the right end, as before; the turned sheet's scrolling stays.
- Demo key limit said (2026-11-18): the test previews' browser key turned out to be a Google "Maps Demo Key" (project 248736144542, gmpDemo) whose daily quota for Maps JavaScript runs out ("Maps Demo Key limit reached: Your daily quota for Maps JavaScript 2D has been met", then gm_authFailure, so both the 2D Google base and the photographic 3D fall back); the user chose to leave it so (the open 2D and the simplified 3D take over until the next day). The fallback line under the 3D says only "Vista 3D fotografica non disponibile ora: mostro la mappa semplificata" again: the "Motivo: … · Build …" line added to find this out was removed at the user's word.
- ARCore view in fullscreen (2026-11-18, as asked "metti la fotocamera AR a tutto schermo come la fotocamera AI"): the session is still asked out of the page's fullscreen (the user's Xperia refuses it inside), and once it runs ArLive arFullscreen asks fullscreen on the overlay root (navigationUI hide, even when the overlay already counts as fullscreen, as Chrome's AR overlay left Android's navigation bar on screen — seen on the user's Xperia), at once and again on the first tap inside the AR view (the starting tap is spent by the session), on the first start and on a restart; if the phone ends the session within 2,5 s of that request, this phone is remembered (localStorage mdt-ar-fullscreen-v1 "fail") and never asked again. The preview without ARCore already takes the camera's fullscreen back. Not tried on ARCore here.
- ARCore asked in fullscreen first again (2026-11-18, as asked: the AR view showed Android's bars while the AI camera covers them; refines "ARCore asked out of fullscreen again"): the refusals once seen in fullscreen on the user's Xperia were later traced to the site's blocked AR permission, so requestArSession now asks every configuration while the page stays in the camera's fullscreen (tryArConfigs), so the AR view covers the system bars; only if all are refused there (NotSupportedError) the phone is remembered (localStorage mdt-ar-in-fullscreen-v1 "fail") and the fullscreen is left for the old tries out of it — that retry may answer SecurityError (the tap spent), and «Riprova ARCore» starts it with a fresh tap, out of fullscreen from then on; a start in fullscreen marks "ok". The depth configuration is blamed (markDepthRefused) only for refusals out of fullscreen. Not tried on ARCore here.
- Turned AR HUD kept off the corners (2026-11-18, screenshot: held sideways, the AI button and Annulla sat on the screen's rounded corner): while the ARCore HUD is turned by CSS its two ends get 1,75 rem of room (paddingLeft/Right in hudTurnStyle), so the bottom row's AI button, Annulla and Riporta and the top row's Esci da AR and flash come a little towards the middle.
- AR joins only inside their own zone (2026-11-18, as asked "aggancia i pergolati e le aiuole solo se le copie entrano nel proprio spazio verde di area"; the user chose "La sua zona"; refines "AR menu simpler, pergolas and aiuole join by coming close"): two pergolas or two aiuole join only when the joined footprint lies whole inside one zone of its kind drawn on the map — a Pergolato zone (ombra) for pergolas, an Aiuole zone (prato) for aiuole — (models/ar-objects joinFitsZone: the four corners turned to the map through the AR alignment, arToEn → fromEN, each inside the same zone; "noalign", "nozone", "out" otherwise; src/test/ar-join-zone.test.ts). Otherwise the outline stays yellow (no green, no double pulse), the top line says "Non si uniscono: allinea prima l’AR alla mappa (Riporta, i 2 punti) / disegna sulla mappa una zona Pergolato / Aiuole / uniti uscirebbero dalla zona …", and put down there they stay apart ("Non uniti: …"). In the «Modellino» (spots not the real ones) the zones are not asked.
- The project in AR joins by zone too (2026-11-18, "continua a unirmi gli elementi dello stesso tipo"; refines "AR joins only inside their own zone"): the map's project shown in AR (ar-plan buildPlan, merge and joinBeds always on) joined every block of touching pergola or aiuole patches by itself; now pergolaBlocks takes a fits test and joins a block only when its box lies whole in a Pergolato (ombra) or Aiuole (prato) zone drawn on the map (corners turned to the map with arToEn → fromEN, insideRing), else each patch stays its own pergola or bed as on the map (src/test/ar-plan.test.ts). The open 3D keeps its patches apart as before. The "Non uniti: …" line now clears after 3,5 s instead of staying on screen.
- Objects seen after ARCore's flash (2026-11-18, "gli oggetti spariscono dopo il flash"): the preview opened by the flash mounts before the camera's new stream exists, so its 3D canvas went into the camera box first and the <video> React added later went after it, over it — the carried objects and the reticle were drawn but hidden behind the picture. The preview's canvas now has z-index 1 over the video, whatever comes first. Not tried on ARCore here.
- No zoom with the flash in AR (2026-11-18, as asked, "ancora" with a screenshot of the preview with the flash lit): the AR preview shows no zoom while the flash is on — opened by ARCore's flash (ArLive torch) or lit in the preview (torchOn) — and the picture goes back to 1×; with the flash off the plain preview keeps its zoom.
- Objects stay put when turning in the AR preview (2026-11-18, "se muovo il telefono l'oggetto si muove appresso a me"): the preview without ARCore (also the one opened by the flash) turned its camera only up and down, so the objects followed the phone when it turned; now ArLive's deviceorientation reads the camera's heading too (alpha, beta, gamma and the screen's angle composed like three's device-orientation controls, the first reading taken as straight ahead, lightly smoothed; floorRef.yaw) and the preview's camera turns with it, so the objects stay where they were put while the phone turns. Walking still cannot be followed without ARCore. Not tried on a phone here.
- Account to reconnect said (2026-11-19, screenshot: "Piano Premium" with the sign-in form and no cloud bar): the phone can remember an account's email (mdt-registered-email, set when a recognised profile is entered while its kept session had expired) with no live session, and the panel then read as signed in (Premium) while showing Accedi | Registrati and no "Spazio nel cloud" (the bar needs a real session). Now such an account (local-<email>) gets an amber "Account da ricollegare · <email>" card in the panel explaining that photos, videos and history do not go to the cloud until it is reconnected, with "Ricollega l’account" (signInAs: Google's sign-in for that address; an email/password account opens the form with the email written); the Accedi | Registrati form hides behind "Accedi con un altro account". leaveAccount also forgets the remembered email, so leaving an account no longer reads as Premium. Fixed too: the panel crashed reading account.id for a guest session (id?.startsWith).
- No sign-in form once signed in (2026-11-19, screenshot: signed in, the panel still showed "Entra con impronta…", Accedi | Registrati and no cloud bar): the panel showed the account section only while its mode was not "signin", and the mode starts as "signin", so a signed-in account always got the sign-in form. Now the account section shows whenever a real session exists; the form shows over it only when the panel is asked to sign in as a named email (OPEN_ACCOUNT_EVENT with detail.email: askSignIn, cleared when the panel closes and when an account is read). Checked with a mocked session: account card, "Spazio nel cloud", Dispositivi collegati and Esci; no form, no biometric buttons.
- Cloud bar and app-only Svuota (2026-11-19, as asked): the account panel's "Spazio nel cloud" bar is thinner (h-1.5) and its colour blends with the share used (lib/use-cloud-usage usageTone: turquoise at 0, green at 50 %, yellow at 72 %, orange at 87 %, red at 100 %, interpolated; the ring round the account picture uses it too). "Svuota" asks in place what to delete — Foto and Video checkboxes, each with its count and size — and says it deletes only the photos and videos saved by this app (Google Drive, Gmail, Google Foto and the rest of the account untouched); "Elimina n" removes only the chosen kinds of the profile's project from the app's cloud storage (removeMedia), the line under the bar reads "… salvati dall’app".
- "Questo dispositivo" on its own line (2026-11-19, as asked): in "Dispositivi collegati" the turquoise tag sits whole on its own line under the device's name (whitespace-nowrap, inline-block), no longer split across two lines beside a long name.
- Fingerprint entry signs in for real (2026-11-19, screenshot: entered with the fingerprint, the account panel still showed the sign-in form): when the account's session kept on this phone was missing or expired, the recognition (enterWithProfile) and the carousel's pick (SectorGate KnownProfiles) only put the profile in use, wrote the remembered email and said "Sei entrato con …" without any real sign-in. Now in that case the profile goes in use and its account's real sign-in starts at once (signInAs: Google's sign-in for that address, back to the profile's simulation; an email/password account opens the panel with the email written), with a toast "Accedo con <email> · La sessione dell’account su questo telefono è scaduta: entra una volta, poi l’impronta ti farà entrare da sola."; once in, the session is kept and the fingerprint enters by itself again. The account panel also reads the account again whenever the remembered account changes (use-signed-in subscribeAuthChange and the storage event), so it never stays on a stale signed-out view. Checked with a mocked Supabase: a recognised profile with no kept session goes to Google's sign-in for its address and no longer writes the remembered email.
- Every recognised address stays enterable (2026-11-19, as asked "lo deve fare per ogni indirizzo riconosciuto registrato"): "Esci dal profilo" and the panel's "Esci" (lib/exit-to-gate, not global) no longer end the account's session on the server (supabase signOut scope local revoked it, so the session kept on the phone stopped working and the fingerprint could only fake an entry): they call lib/account-sessions forgetHere — the page's own session dropped from storage and memory (auth _removeSession, no server call, SIGNED_OUT ignored by the kept-sessions listener), the remembered email cleared — so every personal account seen on this phone stays kept and its recognised profile (carousel, "Entra come …", fingerprint) enters it again at once; only "Esci da tutti i dispositivi" (global) ends the sessions and forgets them. saveProId no longer writes the remembered email from a profile's own email field (it made a municipal officer's profile read as signed in). Checked with a mocked Supabase: no /logout call on leaving, the session kept, "Continua come …" enters the account with its cloud bar and no sign-in form.
- People and constraints of the place (2026-11-19, as asked "una stima di quante persone ci sono sul luogo … vincoli paesaggistici, sovrintendenza"; one commit on the test branch only, to revert in one go): the phone asks the open map servers once per place (lib/site-context-client loadSiteContext: OVERPASS_URLS in turn, 15 s each, plus the place's elevation from the weather service; views/v10/SiteContext useSiteContext, kept for the session) — the official ISTAT and Ministry services could not be reached from the development container. models/site-context: siteContextQuery (within 300 m: bars and restaurants, shops, schools, churches, stops and stations, services, museums and attractions, hotels, markets, the buildings' count; and heritage, historic goods, protected areas and reserves, rivers 160 m, coast and lakes 320 m, woods 60 m), drawsFrom, crowdEstimate (the day's peak from homes — 8 people a building, 2 % out —, bars ×4, shops ×1,2, churches ×3, stops ×2,5, services ×2, schools ×12 at entry and lunch hours and none in summer, markets ×25 in the morning, museums ×12 and hotels ×3 weighed by the season's tourism; times hourShare — the evening stroll, the summer heat dip 14–17 — and SEASON_SHARE; at most one person every 1,5 m² of the area; a ±40 % range), siteConstraints (D.Lgs. 42/2004: cultural goods within 150 m, art. 10/21; a public square or street with historic goods around, art. 10 c. 4 lett. g; archaeology within 300 m; art. 142 landscape areas — coast, lakes, rivers, mountains over 1.200 m, 1.600 m in the Alps, parks, woods — authorisation art. 146, with the art. 142 c. 2 town-centre exemption said), interventionAdvice (piazza: pergolas and fixed misters need the Soprintendenza, trees of local species and beds are easier, some planting is exempt under DPR 31/2017 all. A, ask for the archaeological check before digging; vineyard: tall shade cloth and windbreaks usually need the landscape authorisation, sometimes simplified, all. B). src/test/site-context.test.ts. UI (views/v10/SiteContext): the piazza's Simula ends, after "Cosa ottieni", with "Persone nella piazza" (CrowdCard, "stima": the season and hour of the simulation, "≈ low–high persone", per 100 m², the busiest hour, a 24-bar day chart with the chosen hour lit, how many the interventions' shade covers, what lies within 300 m, the note that it is not a count) and "Vincoli dell'area" (ConstraintsCard, "indicativo": a verdict, each constraint folded with its law, level probabile / possibile, why and what it means, "Per gli interventi", links to the Ministry's maps of protected goods and landscape constraints, and that only the Comune's technical office and the Soprintendenza can say for sure); the vineyard's Simula ends with the same "Vincoli dell'area" for the vineyard. Nothing is shown while no place is chosen.
- People and constraints in the dashboard and the report (2026-11-19, as asked; refines "People and constraints of the place", test branch only): the dashboard's Piazza ends, after the zones, with the same "Persone nella piazza" (at the dashboard's season and the hour its reading uses, sum.hour; the shade places of its summary) and "Vincoli dell'area" cards, the Vigneto with "Vincoli dell'area"; they read the place where the simulation does (models/site-context siteCenter: the drawn area's middle, else the point) and share its cached answer (siteKey). The PDF report (routes/dashboard ExportReport) adds, after "Cosa hai messo", a "Persone nella piazza · stima" card (crowdRows: Quando, Persone presenti, Densità, Momento più affollato, Ombra degli interventi, Zona turistica) and a "Vincoli dell'area · indicativo" card (constraintRows: each constraint with its level, law, why and meaning, the advice for the interventions, and the verification note), moved whole to the next page like the other short cards; the copied text adds the same rows ("Persone · …", "Vincoli · …"). Checked: a report saved from the export sheet carries both cards (pdftotext). src/test/site-context.test.ts.
- Surroundings read reliably, feasibility first, no automatic interventions (2026-11-19, screenshot "Non riesco a leggere i dintorni" on the phone; as asked "quando scelgo un'area non mettermi già in automatico gli interventi fammi vedere prima se sono fattibili"): (1) the one heavy map query (parks and woods asked by distance, slow for huge areas, beside the app's other map queries, so refused or timed out) is two light ones asked one after the other (models/site-context siteDrawsQuery; siteConstraintsQuery with is_in for the parks, reserves and woods the point lies in), each through the map servers in turn twice over with a pause after a 429 or 504 and 25 s each (lib/site-context-client ask); one failing keeps the other (SiteContext drawsOk / constraintsOk); the cards say why ("tempo scaduto", "risposta 429", "senza rete"…) with a "Riprova" button. (2) A place never seen starts with no interventions (lab-store setUrban: trees, aiuole, shade, misters, sprinklers 0; setVineyard: no zone mix; defaults 0 too), no longer the levers of the place left; a place already seen still gets its own back. (3) A zone drawn for an intervention no longer raises the levers by itself: a toast "Zona disegnata: interventi non ancora messi · Proposta: … Prima guarda i vincoli dell'area in Simula" with "Mettili" applies them (SimMap, piazza and vineyard). (4) Simula opens, before the levers, with "Fattibilità dell'area" (views/v10/SiteContext FeasibilityStrip: the constraints found in one line, amber, or none, turquoise; a tap scrolls to "Vincoli dell'area"), piazza and vineyard. src/test/place-memory.test.ts updated.
- A new area starts free of interventions (2026-11-19, as asked "una volta scelta l'area … l'area deve essere libera da interventi. La ricerca delle fattibilità inizia con la circoscrizione dell'area"): finishing the drawing of a new area (SimMap finish, no area saved before for the place) puts every intervention to zero — the piazza's trees, aiuole, shade, misters and sprinklers (placed cells cleared), each vineyard zone's irrigation, shade cloth and windbreak (0 on the zone) — with a toast "Area salvata · libera da interventi · … Prima guarda la fattibilità dell'area in Simula, poi scegli gli interventi." and "Fattibilità", which opens Simula (where "Fattibilità dell'area" sits before the levers); redrawing an existing outline (Modifica) keeps the interventions chosen.
- Surroundings never spin for ever (2026-11-19, screenshot: "Guardo cosa c'è intorno…" and "Cerco beni tutelati…" spinning with no end, "misuro la piazza…" too: the phone's map servers kept the requests queued): the people query asks only counts (models/site-context siteDrawsQuery: ten "out count" statements in DRAW_ORDER — bars and restaurants, shops, schools, churches, stops, places to visit, lodgings, markets, services, buildings —, read by drawsFromCounts; a few hundred bytes however busy the place); lib/site-context-client ask sends each query to every map server at once (OVERPASS_URLS plus the maps.mail.ru and overpass.osm.jp mirrors), 14 s each, the first good answer wins and the others are cancelled, one round only ("Riprova" asks again), with a fallback for phones without AbortSignal.timeout; a place's surroundings read in full are kept on the phone for a week (localStorage mdt-site-v1, the 30 latest places), so the cards fill at once the next time. Checked with mocks: the main servers hanging and one mirror answering, the cards fill in seconds; every server hanging, "Non riesco a leggere i dintorni ora (tempo scaduto)" with Riprova after about 30 s.
- The search starts from the area (2026-11-19, as asked "non deve iniziare la ricerca se non circoscrivo l'area"): the surroundings (people and constraints) are asked only once an area is drawn for the place (useSiteContext disabled otherwise: drawnAreaFor in UrbanThermometer, VineThirst, the dashboard and the report), read at the area's middle; before that "Fattibilità dell'area" says "Circoscrivi l'area sulla mappa: la ricerca di vincoli e persone parte da lì." and "Persone nella piazza" / "Vincoli dell'area" show the same line with "Disegna l'area" (views/v10/SiteContext DrawFirst → openSimTab …, "mappa"); the report leaves the two cards out. Checked: with no area no map query is sent.
- Constraints load, better people estimate (2026-11-19, screenshot: the people card filled, "Vincoli dell'area" said "Non riesco a leggere i dintorni ora" with no reason; as asked "migliora la ricerca"): the constraints query no longer carries is_in (not every map server answers it, and it sank the whole query): models/site-context siteConstraintsQuery asks only what lies around (cultural goods, historic goods, rivers, coast, lakes, woods), siteParksQuery asks apart the parks, reserves and woods the point lies in; lib/site-context-client asks the three together (Promise.allSettled; the parks one optional, 9 s), so the card waits for the slowest, not their sum, and a failure says its reason (SiteContext why, shown by the cards); kept per place in localStorage mdt-site-v2. People: the counts add offices and parks / playgrounds / pitches (DRAW_ORDER offices, leisure; crowdEstimate: offices × 1,5 in working hours, parks × 4 in the afternoon and evening), and the surroundings' people are spread over a usual 3.000 m² piazza (size factor (m² / 3.000)^0,7, 0,15–2), so a small area holds fewer than a large one; the card lists offices and parks too. src/test/site-context.test.ts. Checked with mocks: a server refusing is_in no longer stops the constraints, both cards fill in about 10 s.
- Nothing found, nothing said (2026-11-19, as asked "se non trova niente non scrivere questo messaggio"): with no constraint found, "Vincoli dell'area" shows no verdict line nor "Per gli interventi" (interventionAdvice returns nothing then), only the links to the Ministry's maps and the small note; "Fattibilità dell'area" at the top of Simula is hidden; the report leaves the constraints card out (constraintRows returns no rows). The people count, when it timed out while the constraints answered, is asked once more by itself with 22 s (lib/site-context-client) before the card says it could not read the surroundings.
- Place shape read reliably (2026-11-19, as asked "migliora il riconoscimento delle aree a volte non funziona"): the place's mapped shape (surface and centre: "Misuro la piazza…", the pin) is asked to every map server at once — lib/overpass-race raceOverpass (OVERPASS_URLS plus the maps.mail.ru and overpass.osm.jp mirrors, 14 s each, the first answer wins, the others cancelled), shared with the surroundings (site-context-client) and the app's server (getPlaceArea, no longer one server after another) —, and lib/place-area-client loadPlaceArea races the app's server and the phone's own query, keeping a found shape per place on the phone for a month (localStorage mdt-place-area-v1, 60 places). Recognised more often: market squares and village greens count as squares, closed pedestrian ways without area=yes and park relations are asked, and a multipolygon whose outer edge is split over several ways is chained into rings (models/place-area joinOuter). src/test/place-area-client.test.ts.
- Surroundings read on the whole drawn area (2026-11-19, as asked "migliora il riconoscimento dell'area nelle stime"): people and constraints are no longer read from the area's middle only. models/site-context zoneFilters asks the map servers round the drawn outline (Overpass around with the closed polyline, so distances count from the area's edge as the law measures them) and inside it (poly:, outline at most 40 points); what draws people within max(150, 300 − the area's half-size) m of its edge, the constraints at their legal distances from it; parks and reserves by is_in at the middle and four corners (probePoints), the height the highest of them; constraint distances from the area (distToPlace: 0 inside, else to the nearest edge). The ring rides in useSiteContext / the cards (ring prop) and the cache key (siteKey ringTag, localStorage mdt-site-v3), so a redrawn area is read again. The card's query never spins for more than 45 s (then "tempo scaduto" with Riprova) and runs even when the phone believes it is offline (networkMode always). src/test/site-context.test.ts.
- Surroundings fast on the first try (2026-11-19, "all'inizio esce sempre tempo scaduto… a volte va a volte non va"; supersedes the round-the-outline filters of "Surroundings read on the whole drawn area"): both queries use one box round the area ([bbox:…], the map servers' fastest filter; models/site-context placeBox: the area's box grown by max(150, 300 − its half-size) m for the counts, 330 m for the constraints), the constraints come back with their shapes cut to that box (out tags geom(box)) and their distance from the area's edge is measured on the phone on their nearest point (elDist, CONSTRAINT_M: heritage 250, historic 300, river 160, coast and lake 320, wood 60). Each part is asked at the same time by the phone (every map server) and by the app's server (controllers/site-context.functions getSiteEls, which builds only the app's own three queries), the first answer winning, in up to three rounds of 20 s by itself (parks one round of 12 s); the card waits at most 75 s, says after 12 s "I server delle mappe sono lenti in questo momento: continuo a cercare da solo." and only then the reason with Riprova. The reading starts as soon as an area is drawn (SimMap finish → SiteContext prefetchSite, the same query and cache), so Simula usually finds it ready.
- A reload keeps the open tab (2026-11-19, "fa la ricerca e mi riporta su mappa"): the app reloads by itself when its files belong to another version (a new test build published while it is open: reloadFresh) and every opening of a simulation started on Mappa, so the user was thrown from Simula back to Mappa in the middle of the search. views/v8/console now writes the simulations' tabs to sessionStorage (mdt-sim-tabs-v1) on pagehide and, when this load is a reload within 30 s, startOnMap puts that tab back after hydration instead of Mappa; any other opening still starts on Mappa. The surroundings' server function is imported statically (lib/site-context-client), no extra chunk to miss. Seen in the preview's logs meanwhile: the map servers time out from Vercel too, so the phone's own query and the per-place cache matter.
- Interventions suggested on the map (2026-11-19, as asked "suggeriscimi su mappa gli interventi possibili dopo la scansione dei vincoli"): on the Mappa tab, under the area's bars, once an area is drawn, "Interventi possibili · dai vincoli" (views/v10/SiteContext SuggestedInterventions, the same surroundings query and cache as Simula, read at the area's middle with its outline) lists each intervention with a state from the constraints found — "Si può fare" (green), "Con autorizzazione" (amber), "Sconsigliato" (red) — and why on a tap (models/site-context feasibleInterventions: piazza — trees with authorisation near cultural goods, beds free, fixed pergolas sconsigliato in a protected space and with authorisation in a landscape area, fixed misters with authorisation under any constraint, sprinklers free, anything dug needing the archaeological check near archaeology; vineyard — drip free, shade cloth with authorisation under a constraint, a hedge windbreak free), then "Proposta per n m²: …" (a mix sized to the usable surface: what is sconsigliato left out, what needs authorisation kept light) and "Metti gli interventi consigliati", which sets the levers (vineyard: every zone) so they show on the map, with a toast leading to Simula. Indicative, said in the card. src/test/site-context.test.ts.
- Proposal drawn in the area (2026-11-19, as asked "qui i suggerimenti grafici nell'area" on the Simula map preview): while an area is drawn and nothing is in place yet (every lever at zero; not in "Solo quello disegnato"), SimMap lays out the interventions the constraints allow (SiteContext useSuggestion → feasibleInterventions) inside the area as a proposal — the same piazzaLayout / vineLayout drawing, faded to about 60 % on the 2D map (SatelliteMap2D proposal → map-area setSimGhost now takes a strength) — on both the Simula preview and the Mappa tab, with a pill over the map "Proposta con i vincoli trovati / · nessun vincolo trovato" and its mix ("10 alberi · aiuole 30 % · 4 innaffiatoi"), a white "Metti" that puts it in (the same as the Mappa card's "Metti gli interventi consigliati") and ✕ that sets it aside for that outline (localStorage mdt-proposal-off-v1, by ringTag). The vineyard's zone pills give way to it on the preview while it shows. Once a lever is moved the area shows the real interventions again.
- Shade sails instead of pergolas (2026-11-19, as asked "cambia i pergolati con vele"; supersedes the pergola looks above — internal keys stay: shade, ombra, pergolato, PERGOLA_COLORS, pergolaParts): the piazza's shade is drawn and named as shade sails ("vele ombreggianti"). 3D (AR, open 3D, Google 3D): views/ar-sail realSail — the patch cut into sails of at most 6 m a side, each woven fabric (off-white and sand in turn) stretched between four galvanised steel posts, two corners high and two low so it twists like a tensioned sail, edges cut concave, a little sag, a dark hem and steel tension lines to the posts, posts on concrete footings with soft round shadows (ar-plan, g3d-models key "sail:", ar-object-meshes "pergolato"); views/ar-pergola stays unused. Simple shapes (area-layout pergolaParts / pergolaRingParts): thin steel posts at most 6 m apart (8 m lite) and one fabric sheet at 3–3,08 m. 2D (map-area): the strips joined into blocks (shadeBlocks: touching strips filling ≥ 85 % of their rectangle), each cut into sails (sailsOf: ≤ 6 m, corners set in, concave edges) drawn as fabric with a dark hem and small steel post dots over the shade on the ground (sim-pergola now a 28 % dark veil; the slats are gone). Words: "Vele ombreggianti" in the legend and suggestions, zone kind "Vele", AR object "Vela ombreggiante" ("Tessuto teso su pali d’acciaio, smontabile"), lever hint "Vele in tessuto tese su pali, tende.", AR joining "vele", texts of Posizione migliore / Solo disegnato / constraints. Feasibility: sails are light and removable, so near cultural goods or in a landscape area they are "Con autorizzazione" (no longer "Sconsigliato"), and the proposal keeps them at 10 %.
- Side-pole sails that open like butterfly wings (2026-11-19, as asked "vele estraibili palo vele ombreggianti … queste" with a link to a side-pole shade whose two fabric wings open horizontally like a butterfly's — the user chose both: on a single pole and opening/closing by themselves; supersedes the four-post sails of "Shade sails instead of pergolas"): models/area-layout papillonUnits (in a w×d rectangle the poles stand on the long edge, rows ≤ 4 m deep, one every ≤ 2·r, r the wings' reach ≤ 4,2 m), papillonFan (the pole and the arc of the wings, half-angle 90° · open, at least 4°), shadeOpenness (open with the simulation's sun: full from 15°, closed under 5°, closed in winter), shadeBlocks (moved here from map-area), rectFrameOf, papillonsOf (lat/lng fans, poles, ribs). SimMap works out shadeOpen from the simulation's season and hour (sunHeightDeg) into SimLayout.shadeOpen; closed (< 0,3) the shade casts no shadow; the SimBar legend adds "· aperte / · chiuse a quest’ora / · si stanno aprendo". 2D: each block's fans in fabric colours with a dark hem, their ribs (sim-sailribs) and the poles. 3D: views/ar-papillon realPapillons({w,d,h,open}) — wood-clad pole on a steel plate and footing, anthracite head and arm, four ribs a wing fanning out with the fabric between them sagging a little, a warm LED strip under the arm; closed, the fabric gathered in a bundle under the arm — used by ar-plan (blocks via shadeBlocks unless joined by zone), g3d-models (key "pap:w×d:open" in quarter steps) and the open 3D (three-layer passes open), and the AR object "Vela ombreggiante" (always open there); simple shapes (pergolaParts / pergolaRingParts, now the same) a slim pole and the fan as one sheet. Words: lever hint "Vele su palo laterale: le ali si aprono nelle ore calde e si richiudono da sole la sera, di notte e d’inverno.", AR hint "Palo laterale, ali di tessuto che si aprono e si chiudono", the feasibility lines. views/ar-sail stays unused. src/test/area-layout.test.ts.
- Side-pole sails with a designer look (2026-11-19, as asked "falli un po' di più da design"; refines the 3D model of "Side-pole sails that open like butterfly wings"): views/ar-papillon realPapillons — a slim round anthracite pole tapering upwards on a flat round base with a satin ring near its foot, a capsule head, a slender tapered arm with a warm LED line under it, and two wings of taut warm-white fabric (a little emissive, as daylight shows through it from below) rising gently towards their tips like a gull's wing, five slim round ribs a wing ending in satin caps, the edge cut in soft rounded scallops between them with a thin dark piping; closed, a slim fabric sleeve along the arm. Used by the AR, the open 3D and Google 3D as before.
- Round canopy on a central pole (2026-11-19, as asked "sembra a imbuto non va bene, poi fai la falda a tutto tondo"; supersedes the side pole and the wings rising like a gull's of "Side-pole sails with a designer look" and the layout of "Side-pole sails that open like butterfly wings"): models/area-layout papillonUnits cuts the shade rectangle into cells as square as possible, at most 6 m a side, a pole in the middle of each with a round canopy of radius half the cell's shorter side (≤ 3,3 m), neighbours meeting without overlap; papillonFan is now the canopy's circle (24 points, reach canopyReach = r·(0,12 + 0,88·open)), papillonsOf's ribs every third point, pergolaParts a slim central pole and the round sheet. views/ar-papillon realPapillons: the same anthracite tapered pole, base and satin ring, a hub with a satin finial and a warm LED ring under it, and a round canopy sloping gently down from the hub as a shallow dome (never a funnel), eight slim ribs with satin caps and struts from the pole, soft scallops with a dark piping; as it closes it folds down towards the pole, closed a furled sleeve with a satin band. Texts: "Vele tonde su palo centrale…", AR "Palo centrale, falda tonda di tessuto…".
- Leaning trunk over a round seating platform (2026-11-19, as asked "prendi spunto da queste con il tronco inclinato e una base circolare dove poter sostare e mangiare"; refines "Round canopy on a central pole"): views/ar-papillon realPapillons stands each round canopy on a round platform (platformRadius = r·0,55, 1,1–1,7 m): a low stone disc with a wooden deck, a round wooden bench along its edge (seat 0,43–0,48 m, 40 cm deep, on ten slim anthracite legs) and a round table in the middle (0,45–0,6 m radius, 0,74 m high, satin rim, one pedestal on a round foot); the trunk rises from a steel plate between table and bench and leans so its top (hub) stands over the platform's middle (about 20°), satin ring near the foot, struts from the trunk; closed, the furled sleeve follows the lean. The simple shapes (pergolaParts) add the platform as a 12 cm wooden disc. Texts: lever "Vele tonde su tronco inclinato, con panca e tavolo rotondi sotto…", AR "Tronco inclinato, falda tonda e sotto panca e tavolo rotondi".
- Sails close by themselves, animated (2026-11-19, as asked "anche che si chiude la vela in automatico"): views/ar-papillon realPapillons keeps its canopy in a group of its own ("papillon-canopy") drawn again as the openness changes, with state in userData["papillon"]; setShadeTarget(root, target, onFrame) makes every canopy under root move smoothly to the target (TRAVEL_S 4 s the whole way, stepped from the first fixed mesh's onBeforeRender, so any scene drawing it animates it; the canopy folds down towards the trunk, then furls into a sleeve 0,6–1,5 m long; its meshes cast shadows like the fixed parts). realShadeOpen(lat, lng, date) is the openness under the real sun now (open from 15° of sun, closed under 5° and in the winter months). AR (ArLive shadeAuto): every sail put by hand, carried or drawn from the project is built open and then follows the real sun over the place, again every minute; one put down while the sun is low, set or in winter closes before one's eyes with "La vela si chiude da sola: il sole è tramontato / è basso / d’inverno resta chiusa. Si riapre da sola nelle ore calde." Open 3D (three-layer): the plan is built at the openness it had before and moves to the simulation's sun (shadeWas → setShadeTarget, the map repainted each step), so moving the simulation's hour shows the sails opening or closing. Google 3D keeps its models at quarter steps of openness (no animation there).
- Desktop layout, phase 1–2 (2026-11-19, as asked "con la proposta desktop che mi suggerisci"; test branch): from 1024 px of width (lib/use-desktop useIsDesktop, matchMedia (min-width: 1024px), live on resize) the menu stands at the side — __root SideNav: a fixed 240 px column with the compact logo and "MICRO DIGITAL TWIN", the bottom bar's items (itemsFor icons) with the desktop pills' names (navFor), the open page lit with a turquoise inner edge, locks as in the pills — and the header (account, "i", theme) sits at its foot (lg:fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-60); the top pills (SmartNav) hide at lg, the page and footer move right (lg:pl-60). No camera on desktop. In the simulations (SimShell) Mappa and Simula become one tab "Mappa e simulazione" (DESK_TABS; Sensori AI stays): the full SimMap on the left, sticky under the tabs and scrolling inside itself when taller than the screen, the simulation and its photos and videos on the right (no map preview there); phones and tablets below 1024 px are unchanged. A 30 s video of it for a slide (scratchpad deskvid) uses the mobile video's style (stock opening, laptop frame on the dark teal backdrop with the brand line, captions, end card).
- Menu pills in the app's own look (2026-11-19, as asked "togli il blu attivo usa lo stesso tema dell'app"): the tablet/desktop pill menu (__root SmartNav) no longer lights the open page in cobalt blue (nav-glass-on, bg-nav-accent): the open pill is the same slate pill as the app's tabs (TabRow: bg-secondary, secondary-foreground, a faint inner top light), the others plain text at 85 % with a soft hover, in dark and light alike. The phone's bottom bar is unchanged.
- Menu label "Cronologia" on tablet and desktop (2026-11-19, as asked): the tablet pills and the desktop side menu name the first entry (to /benvenuto) "Cronologia" like the phone's bottom bar, no longer "Benvenuto"; the page itself keeps its title "Benvenuto".
- Sails in the AI camera: joined as one, opened by light (2026-11-19, as asked): two or more sails joined in the AR (as the pergola joined) are one continuous canopy over all their trunks (views/ar-papillon realPapillons joined: a rounded-rectangle fabric dome — a superellipse — over the whole footprint, peaking in the middle, twelve ribs, scalloped piped edge, each trunk's top meeting it; ar-object-meshes passes joined when the footprint is larger than one sail), no longer a row of separate round ones.
