A sail put down in the AR is built closed and opens by itself only with light: ArLive reads the light twice a second — ARCore's light estimate (primary light and ambient, arLightLevel) while it runs, else the camera picture's mean brightness — smoothed, and lightTarget opens above 0,34 and closes below 0,2 (hysteresis); crossing them every sail opens or closes in about 4 s ("C’è luce: le vele si aprono da sole." / "È buio: le vele si chiudono da sole."); the real sun position no longer drives them in the AR (the 3D maps keep the simulation's sun). src/test/sail-light.test.ts. Not tried on ARCore here.
- Desktop dashboard in two columns (2026-11-20, desktop/tablet phase 3): from 1024 px (lg) each dashboard tab (views/v10/Dashboard PiazzaDashboard / VineDashboard) is a two-column grid: on the left the result card and "La giornata" / "Le tre zone", on the right "Cosa ottieni", "Cosa hai messo", the zones, people and constraints, then the records (history, Registro / Quaderno, Invia il progetto, Premium); each column min-w-0 so nothing overflows. Phones and tablets below 1024 px unchanged.
- Wider history grids (2026-11-20, desktop/tablet phase 3): the photo and video piles (HistoryBlock PhotoPiles, also archive and Benvenuto) take their columns from the grid's own width (pileColumns: 2 under 576 px, 3, 4 from 704, 5 from 900, 6 from 1100; a ResizeObserver, the opened pile's panel still under its row), so phones keep 2, tablets 3–4, desktops 5–6 and the dashboard's half column stays readable; on the Cronologia page the day cards of interventions sit two by two from 1024 px. The detail of an intervention stays its own page (/cronologia/$id): beside the list it would need nested routes.
- Secondary pages on wide screens (2026-11-20, desktop/tablet phase 3): the sheets (account panel, Esporta report, camera sheet, gallery review) were already centred windows from 640 px through the shared dialog's max-sm classes, and the info pages (InfoPage max-w-2xl, ProfilePage and the identification pages max-w-xl) already a centred reading width; on its own page /piani now shows the Free and Premium cards side by side from 768 px (one under the other inside the account panel).
- Tablet held sideways like the desktop (2026-11-20, desktop/tablet phase 4): the simulations' "Mappa e simulazione" (map beside the simulation, SimShell) now follows lib/use-desktop useSideBySide (SIDE_BY_SIDE_QUERY: ≥ 1024 px, or landscape ≥ 900 px wide and ≥ 600 px tall, so a phone turned sideways keeps its own tabs); a tablet held upright keeps Mappa · Simula · Sensori AI one at a time; the side menu stays from 1024 px (useIsDesktop).
- Dashboard tabs bar inside the page on desktop (2026-11-20): its bleed to the screen's edges (mx calc(50%−50vw), px calc(50vw−50%)) is reset from 1024 px (lg:mx-0 lg:px-0), since beside the 240 px side menu it reached 120 px past the right edge and blurred the menu's labels.
- Tablet side rail with the camera (2026-11-20, as asked "tablet con il menù laterale, la fotocamera AI anche nella modalità tablet"): from 768 to 1023 px (md) the top pills give way to a fixed 80 px rail at the left (__root TabletRail): the compact logo, the bottom bar's items (itemsFor) as 24 px icons with their short name under them (11 px), the open page on the slate pill with the turquoise inner edge, locks as elsewhere, and between the second and third item the camera button (CameraDockButton, 56 px, its arch margin cancelled) that opens the same camera sheet, AI camera included (its own rules: AI_STANDBY, sign-in, installed app); the header (account, "i", theme) stands stacked at the rail's foot (md:fixed, w-20), the page moves right (md:pl-20) and the dashboard's tab bar stops bleeding (md:mx-0 md:px-0). Phones (bottom bar) and 640–767 px (pills) unchanged; from 1024 px the wide side menu as before, without camera.
- Tablet rail camera under the pages (2026-11-20, as asked "sposta la fotocamera AI in basso ai menù"; refines "Tablet side rail with the camera"): in TabletRail the camera button follows the last page item (Dashboard) instead of standing between the second and third; desktop keeps no camera.
- Photos from folders on desktop (2026-11-20, as asked "nella modalità desktop devo poter caricare le foto da cartelle"): on a computer (≥ 1024 px, fine pointer, not touch) the simulation's photos panel (LabMedia) shows "Carica" straight to the computer's files (no Galleria/Scatta/Registra menu, from 1024 px instead of 1280) and a "Cartella" button (FolderOpen) that picks a whole folder (input webkitdirectory, subfolders too); and photos, videos or whole folders dragged from the computer onto the panel show a dashed turquoise "Rilascia qui foto, video o cartelle" and, dropped, go the same way (lib/folder-files: filesFromDrop reads folders recursively through webkitGetAsEntry, mediaOnly keeps images and videos by type or extension; "Nella cartella non ci sono foto né video" otherwise). Everything goes to the gallery review sheet ("Dove l'hai scattata?", "Aggiungi N") and the usual upload. Phones and tablets unchanged; no camera on desktop.
- Tablet camera above the account (2026-11-20, as asked "sposta AI sopra al pulsante login nella modalità tablet così è più facile scattare"; supersedes "Tablet rail camera under the pages"): in TabletRail the camera button sits at the rail's foot (mt-auto), right above the account button, "i" and theme, within reach of the thumb; the pages stay at the top.
- Tab bars to the page's edges, dashboard records full width (2026-11-20, as asked): the simulations' sticky tab bar (SimShell [data-sim-tabs]) and the dashboard's Piazza | Vigneto bar run from the side menu to the screen's right edge on tablet and desktop (md: mx calc(50% − 50vw + 2,5rem) beside the 80 px rail, lg: + 7,5rem beside the 240 px menu, the same amount as padding so the pills stay where they were); on desktop the dashboard's records (history, Registro / Quaderno, Invia il progetto, Premium with the bodies' approvals) span both columns under them (lg:col-span-2), as in the right half the three approval cards were squeezed letter by letter, and each approval card's head wraps the state under the body's name when narrow (flex-wrap).
- Twin logo to the urban simulation (2026-11-20, as asked "quando clicco sul logo Twin mi deve riportare su simulazione urbana"): the logo at the top of the desktop side menu (SideNav) and of the tablet rail (TabletRail) leads to /urbano on Mappa (openSimTab urban), no longer to /benvenuto; a farmer is moved on to the vineyard by the section gate as everywhere.
- Tab bars stop at the scrollbar (2026-11-20, "esce fuori dai bordi" with a screenshot): on computers 100vw counts the page's scrollbar, so the bars that run to the right edge went under it; the root now keeps the page's width without the scrollbar in --page-w (documentElement.clientWidth, on resize and a ResizeObserver) and the bleed reads it (calc(50% − var(--page-w, 100vw)/2 + 2,5rem / 7,5rem), the same as padding), in SimShell and the dashboard.
- Pages as wide as the screen beside the menu (2026-11-20, as asked with two screenshots: on a 2000 px screen the page sat in a 1280 px column with wide empty margins): from 768 px (md) the main page and the footer drop the max-w-7xl limit (md:max-w-none, md:px-6), so on tablet and desktop the content fills the whole width between the side menu (rail or wide menu) and the screen's right edge; phones unchanged. The tab bars' bleed (--page-w) still lines up, the padding now 1,5 rem.
- Footer centred, dashboard records balanced, ring on the open menu item (2026-11-20, as asked): from 768 px the page footer note ("I risultati del simulatore sono indicativi…") is centred in the page (md:text-center). From 1024 px the dashboard's records (routes/dashboard UrbanRecords / VineRecords, given the history as a prop) are two columns — the project's history (HistoryBlock compact) on the left, the Registro interventi / Quaderno di campagna and "Invia il progetto" on the right — with Premium (approvals, Piano suggerito dall’AI) across both under them; below 1024 px one column as before. In the desktop side menu and the tablet rail the open page no longer has the turquoise half-moon on its left edge (inset shadow): its icon sits inside a turning turquoise ring with a soft breathing glow (.nav-orb / .nav-orb-ring / .nav-orb-glow, the open pile's conic arc on --pile-angle, scaling and turning in over 420 ms; a plain turquoise ring with reduced motion), on the same slate pill.
- Account panel wider on tablet and desktop (2026-11-20, as asked): from 768 px the account panel (views/account-control) is a centred window up to 900 px wide (calc(100vw − 8rem) at most, up to 860 px / 92dvh tall) laid out in two columns: on the left the "Profilo professionale" card (with its Windows Hello / fingerprint switch) and "Immagine account", on the right the account itself — signed in the account card, "Spazio nel cloud", "Dispositivi collegati", the device cache and Esci; signed out the reconnect card, the biometric entries and Accedi | Registrati —, then across both "Piani e informazioni", the app version and Twin. Phones keep the single column unfolding as a circle.
- Account panel over a blurred page (2026-11-20, as asked): from 768 px the account panel's veil (account-overlay) is fixed over the whole screen, black 45 % with a backdrop blur (md:backdrop-blur-md, the -webkit- twin from the closing rule), so the page behind is blurred; the page can still be scrolled under it (wheel or finger on the veil scroll the page, as the dialog is non-modal from 640 px) but nothing behind can be clicked, as the veil takes every tap; a tap on the veil closes the panel, like its ✕. Phones unchanged.
- AR on tablets too (2026-11-20, as asked; supersedes "Prova on phones only"): the AI camera's Rileva | AR switch shows on every touch device, tablets included (ArCamera onPhone = isTouchDevice(), no longer limited to a short side under 600 px), so tablets get the AR view (ARCore where the browser offers it, else the preview with its floor line); computers without a touch screen stay on Rileva.
- Desktop gauge bigger, desktop tutorial as speech bubbles (2026-11-20, as asked): from 1024 px the simulations' arc and its readings are larger (UrbanThermometer and VineThirst: the gauge, its labels and the perceived-temperature line max-w 27rem instead of 21rem, the reading text-7xl, the verdict chip 15 px, the end labels and the air-temperature line a step bigger). On a computer (≥ 1024 px) the tutorial page is not shown at the first access (the root's landing marks the intro seen and goes to the simulation); instead views/v10/DesktopTour (mounted lazily by the root) starts by itself once a simulation is on screen (no sector page, no preloader, no open dialog; localStorage mdt-desk-tour-v1 once ended or skipped): a guided tour of speech bubbles (TOUR: the menu, the tabs, the place search, 2D/3D, Disegna l’area, the arc, the levers, the account/"i"/theme corner — steps whose element is not on screen are left out), each target scrolled into view and ringed in turquoise, the rest of the page under four blurred dark panes that take every click, wheel and scrolling key (only the bubble's Salta / Indietro / Avanti · Fine and the arrow keys work). The "i" button on a computer opens the same bubbles simplified (no blur, a click outside, ✕ or Esc closes; phones and tablets keep the tutorial page). And with a mouse, hovering about half a second over a control shows its own small bubble (TIPS: tabs, search field, GPS, 2D/3D, Disegna l’area, the arc, the levers' buttons, "i", account), gone on leaving, scrolling or clicking.
- Footer lower, drop box, tour steps (2026-11-20, as asked): from 768 px the footer note sits further down (md:pt-14). On a computer (≥ 1024 px, fine pointer, not touch) the simulation's photos panel shows under Carica and Cartella a dashed box (LabMedia .lab-media-dropzone: folder icon, "Trascina qui foto, video o cartelle", "oppure clicca per sceglierli dal computer"; a click opens the file picker, a drop goes through the panel's own drag-and-drop to the gallery review). The desktop tour (DesktopTour TOUR) adds "Persone nella piazza" and "Vincoli dell’area" before "Account e aiuto".
- See-through simulation column, shorter account panel (2026-11-20, as asked): in the desktop "Mappa e simulazione" the right column (SimShell .desk-sim) draws its .fuse-canvas panels see-through (card at 38 % → 24 %, the page at 20 %, a 10 px backdrop blur), so the header's blue ambient glow shows through them. The account panel from 768 px: a compact header (avatar beside "Account", the email and the plan), tighter gaps, and the columns evened out — the device cache card and "Piani e informazioni" move to the left column under the account picture (shown once, in the right column on phones), Twin hidden (the app version stays at the foot).
- Drop box on tablets held sideways (2026-11-20, as asked): the dashed "Trascina qui foto, video o cartelle" box under the photos panel's buttons also shows on touch tablets in landscape (orientation landscape, ≥ 900 × 600 px, html[data-touch]), for files dragged in from the tablet's Files app or a split screen (the panel's own drop handling); its second line reads "oppure tocca o clicca qui per sceglierli". Tablets upright and phones unchanged.
- Guide from "i" blurred too, also on tablets held sideways (2026-11-20, as asked): the simplified guide opened by the "i" button (DesktopTour mode "simple") now blurs the page round the highlighted element like the first-access tour (the same four blurred dark panes), a tap or click on them closes it (as do ✕, Chiudi and Esc); the "i" button opens these bubbles on computers and on tablets held sideways (GUIDE_QUERY: ≥ 1024 px, or landscape ≥ 900 × 600 px; the root's "i" checks the same), while phones and upright tablets keep the tutorial page.
- Bigger gauge on upright tablets (2026-11-20, as asked): the larger arc of the desktop (max-w 27rem, reading text-7xl, verdict chip 15 px, end labels and the air-temperature line a step bigger) also applies from 768 px held upright (md:portrait: in UrbanThermometer and VineThirst); tablets held sideways below 1024 px keep the normal size, as their simulation column sits beside the map.
- Tablet held upright: no AI/Carica in the photos panel, a bigger camera sheet (2026-11-20, as asked): from 768 to 1023 px held upright the simulation's photos panel hides its AI and Carica buttons (the side rail's camera already offers AI, Foto, Video and Galleria), keeping Invia and Elimina, as on phones; the camera sheet (CameraDock) is sized to the upright screen there (md:portrait: up to 600 px wide, roomier padding and gaps, a bigger AI card with a 80 px ring, 80 px round Foto / Video / Galleria with bigger icons and labels, larger title and text).
- Camera sheet over a blurred page (2026-11-20, as asked): the camera sheet's veil (CameraDock overlayClassName) is black 45 % with a backdrop blur on every screen, and from 640 px it is fixed over the whole screen (as the account panel's), so the page behind is blurred while the sheet is open.
- VR headset and a steadier AR preview (2026-11-20, as asked "implementa realtà virtuale con visore su windows e lavora sul punto 4"): where the browser offers WebXR immersive-vr (a headset on the computer: Windows Mixed Reality, Quest through Link / Air Link, SteamVR, in Edge or Chrome) the Mappa tab shows, once an area is drawn, "Guarda nel visore VR" (SimMap VrButton, views/v10/vr-view loaded on the first tap): the project at real size round the area's middle — a wide ground (paving or soil), the area lighter with a white edge, the realistic models of ar-plan buildPlan (identity alignment, pergolas and aiuole joined, trees as the recognised species via growPlan), vine rows as green walls — lit by the simulation's sun (sky, light and shadows from sunAt); the controller's trigger teleports where its ray meets the ground (a turquoise ring), the thumbstick pushed sideways turns 30°; nothing saved. The AR preview without ARCore (Surface, any device without it) reads the orientation from RelativeOrientationSensor (Generic Sensor API, 60 Hz, screen frame) when it starts — steadier, and Chrome on Windows often fires no deviceorientation — else deviceorientation as before, and now follows the roll too (objects lean with the picture), smoothed. Not tried with a headset nor on a Surface here.
- Surface tablet mode (2026-11-20, as asked): a 2-in-1 going into tablet mode (keyboard folded or detached, Windows tablet mode) makes Chrome and Edge report a coarse primary pointer and fire the media query's change; lib/touch-device markSlate keeps html[data-slate] (touch device and (pointer: coarse)) in step, and back in desktop mode it drops. In tablet mode, from 1024 px (also touch tablets held sideways), the upright tablet's interface: the 80 px rail (TabletRail, .slate-on) with the camera / AI button above the account instead of the wide side menu (.slate-off), the header stacked 80 px wide, the page and tab bars aligned to it (.slate-pad, .slate-bleed), the simulations one tab at a time (useSideBySide false when isSlate), the photos panel without AI / Carica, and "i" opening the tutorial page. In desktop mode on a Surface (touch but fine pointer) the photos panel behaves like a computer's: "Carica" goes straight to the files with "Cartella" and the drop box, no Galleria / Scatta / Registra menu (the lab-media rules key off html:not([data-slate]) instead of not data-touch).
- No AI camera on a Surface in desktop mode (2026-11-21, as asked; refines "Surface tablet mode"): from 1024 px a touch 2-in-1 not in tablet mode (html[data-touch] without data-slate: keyboard attached, fine pointer) hides the photos panel's AI button too, like a computer; in tablet mode the rail's camera keeps the AI camera, and phones and tablets are unchanged.
- Silent Surface in desktop mode (2026-11-21, as asked): lib/ui-sounds uiClickVolume returns 0 on a tablet-sized touch screen whose primary pointer is fine (not SLATE_QUERY: a 2-in-1 with its keyboard, desktop mode), like a computer; in tablet mode, phones and touch tablets keep their taps.
- Profile carousel on wide screens (2026-11-21, as asked; the user kept the 3D carousel): on computers, Surfaces and tablets held sideways (SIDE_BY_SIDE_QUERY, SectorGate useWideProfiles) the "Ti abbiamo riconosciuto" carousel keeps its 3D but with cards of a fixed 25 rem (no longer 78 % of a wide column, which stretched and tilted them heavily), a gentler turn (26°, 70 px back, perspective 1600 px), the row wider than the form's column (up to 72 rem, centred) with the first card centred and both ends faded by a mask; phones unchanged.

Accedi · Il profilo che avevi salvato torna da solo."; the carousel's "Aggiungi account" stays the way in. The sector choice ("Da dove vuoi usarmi?") keeps the row above "Entra come ospite".
- Guide on the hard points only, on every page (2026-11-21, as asked): the hover bubbles (DesktopTour TIPS) stay only on controls whose use is not obvious — the GPS, the arc, «Solo quello disegnato», «Posizione migliore», «Sposta elementi», «Aggiungi zona», «Raggruppa foto e video», the Archivio — no longer on tabs, search field, 2D/3D, the levers' buttons, "i" or account. The first-access tour is unchanged.
- "Disegna l'area" opens the map drawing (2026-11-21, as asked): the button of the people and constraints cards with no area (SiteContext DrawFirst, also on the dashboard) asks lib/draw-request requestDraw, opens the simulation's Mappa tab (navigating to /urbano or /vigneto from elsewhere), and the map (SimMap, not the preview) starts drawing by itself (takeDrawRequest within 6 s, on mount or on the mdt-start-draw event), the map box scrolled into view.
- Bubble tip and standard image upload (2026-11-21, as asked): the guide's bubbles (DesktopTour Bubble) point with a whole triangle (a clip-path polygon, 18 × 10 px) instead of a turned square whose corner read as cut, in the bubble's own slate at 84 % with a backdrop blur and a faint white ring. On a computer (fine pointer: PC, Surface with its keyboard) "Carica", the drop box's click and the gallery pick open the system's standard file window (the gallery input, several files at once) instead of the browser's filtered file picker; the inputs and pickers accept every common image format — JPEG/JFIF, PNG, WebP, GIF, BMP, TIFF, HEIC/HEIF, AVIF — besides videos.
- Uploads with an account, blurred upload sheet, guide per page (2026-11-21, as asked "non carica le immagini e nemmeno le cartelle"; the storage logs showed the upload accepted as the signed-in user and, half a second later, its link and the listings asked as the anonymous role, refused as "Object not found", so the upload read as failed and the list came back empty; meanwhile /user was read dozens of times a second): lib/use-signed-in notifyAuthChange is coalesced (one round 60 ms after the last call) and setRegisteredEmail tells nobody when nothing changed; the account panel re-reads the session kept on the phone (getSession, no server call) and only on the storage keys that say who is signed in; lab-media-uploader asks the new file's link up to three times with the session read first, and without a link still lists the file from its copy on the phone (signed at the next listing) instead of marking it failed; lab-media-store ensureMedia waits for the session (up to 1,5 s) before listing the account, else tries again 3 s later instead of taking an empty list. The gallery review sheet (GalleryReview) has the camera sheet's blurred veil (black 45 % with a backdrop blur, fixed over the whole screen from 640 px). The "i" guide (DesktopTour guideFor) explains the main elements of the page open — simulations (tabs, place, 2D/3D, area, proposal, possible interventions, zones, view options, feasibility, arc, levers, people, constraints, photos), dashboard (tabs, export, season, La giornata / Le tre zone, Cosa ottieni, Cosa hai messo, people, constraints, Registro, Invia il progetto, Premium), Benvenuto / Cronologia / Archivio (start tiles, menu, offices, filters, kinds, grouping, archive, full history, clear), Profilo (profile card, account) — then the account corner; other pages fall back to the first-access tour.
- Esporta report over a blurred page (2026-11-21, as asked): the export sheet (ExportSheet) has the same blurred veil as the camera and gallery sheets (black 45 % with a backdrop blur, fixed over the whole screen from 640 px).

- Phone held sideways gets a compact rail (2026-11-21, as asked, seen mirrored on a TV: the tablet rail piled up on a 360 px high screen): on a screen wide but low (landscape, ≥ 768 px wide, ≤ 500 px tall; styles.css media block, classes rail / rail-logo / rail-label / rail-nav / rail-item / rail-orb / rail-cam on __root TabletRail) the 80 px rail shows also from 1024 px instead of the wide menu, without the logo and the names under the icons, the account / "i" / theme buttons at its foot smaller and closer, the menu scrollable if still too tall; the page and tab bars line up with it. Upright tablets, desktops and phones held upright unchanged.
- Guest mode locks extended (2026-11-24, as asked): in guest mode (`useGuestOnly` / `useGuest && !proId`, even with a remembered email or on preview builds), 3D map views (`SimMap`, `SimulationGrid`), the VR headset button (`SimMap` `VrButton`), sensors (`SimShell` locked tab indicator, `SimMenu`, `LabFlows` `GuestSensors`, `SensorAdvisor`, and personal sensor readings in `UrbanThermometer` / `VineThirst`), the AI camera (`CameraDock`, `LabMedia`, `intro`), AI suggestions (`Dashboard` `PremiumPart`, `FieldAI`, `SiteContext` `SuggestedInterventions` / `useSuggestion` / `ProposalPill`), "Persone nella piazza" (`SiteContext` `CrowdCard`) and area feasibility/constraints (`SiteContext` `FeasibilityStrip`, `ConstraintsCard`, `SimMap` area-save prefetch and toasts) stay locked with a lock icon and lead to profile identification (`openSectorGate`); `ProfilePage` `GuestCard` lists them. `src/test/components/guest-locks.test.tsx`.
- Retractable shade sails replace round umbrellas (2026-11-24, as asked; supersedes the round canopies and circular platform/table of "Leaning trunk over a round seating platform"): `views/ar-papillon` `realPapillons` builds architectural motorised retractable shade sails on four inclined anthracite steel masts per bay (shared without duplicates across multi-bay blocks, stainless base flanges, collars, masthead pulley sheaves and guy tie-rods with turnbuckles on perimeter wing masts), a diagonal satin-steel furling roller boom with tubular motor head and warm underside LED bar, and two asymmetric-height hyperbolic triangular sail wings with concave catenary leech edges, stitched panel seams, dark reinforced border webbing, stainless clew rings and taut traction cables; `open` (0–1) smoothly unrolls/furls both wings onto the diagonal roller (`setShadeTarget`, `papillon-canopy`); `joined` spans the whole footprint as one continuous retractable sail. `models/area-layout` `papillonFan` / `papillonsOf` / `pergolaParts` draw the concave four-corner sail outline and diagonal boom/cables; copy updated in `UrbanThermometer` and `ArLive`.
- Retractable hypar shade sails on inclined wooden posts with free orientation (2026-11-24, as asked "dobbiamo fare delle vele retrattili al posto degli ombrelloni nei modelli 3D realistici", "no te la passo io", "questa fammi vedere uno screenshot prima", "poi quando posiziono l'orientamento deve essere libero non automatica", with reference photos): `views/ar-papillon` `realPapillons` implements continuous 4-point hyperbolic paraboloid ("hypar" saddle) retractable shade sails with deep concave catenary leech edges, subtle corner tension pleats, and warm taupe woven fabric (`#ffffff` on canvas texture, warm emissive `#7a7266` so the underside remains luminous); each bay is supported by two outward-leaning warm timber posts (`woodPostBetween` chamfered square section with grain texture, satin ground sockets, eye-caps, twin V-bridle white cords to high corners, and external guy tie-rods to ground stakes) and two low corners pulled taut to discreet ground anchor pegs. Shared grid posts across multi-bay blocks share exact bases without duplicate or offset footings. Retraction (`open` 0–1) smoothly draws the two low corners along the hypar ridge into a compact furled sleeve between the high post tips. Simple 2D and 3D shapes (`models/area-layout` `papillonsOf`, `pergolaParts`, `PERGOLA_COLORS`, `SAIL_FABRICS`) reflect the 4-point saddle and matching wood/tortora palette.
- Free orientation (never automatic) for sails, beds, and zones (2026-11-24, as asked): in `SimMap` / `SatelliteMap2D` "Sposta elementi" mode, beside each block and zone handle a round turning handle (`rotateHandleElement` "⟳") allows dragging 360° to freely set orientation (`lawnAngle`, `shadeAngle`, `zoneAngle`) without auto-snapping to longest edges or cardinal grid strips; `rotatedBlockStrips` lays out cells rotated by `angle`; `drawn-area` `rotateZone` turns zones and rings; `ar-plan` `rectOf(pts, dirAng)` and `pergolaBlocks(..., dirs)` preserve explicit angles; in AR (`ArLive` `placementOf`) automatic camera-facing yaw is removed (`yaw` preserves the last two-finger turn per kind or 0), giving fully free placement and manual two-finger rotation. Verified with focused tests (`src/test/free-orientation.test.ts`) and full test suite passing.
- GPS «Ricalibra» (2026-11-24, as asked "ricalibra il GPS, la posizione di adesso non corrisponde"; on top of the step-by-step following of "real-time step-by-step GPS follow"): under the search, while the GPS follows, a row "GPS attivo · precisione ±n m" (the watch's last fix) with a turquoise "Ricalibra" (SimulationGrid LocationSearch recalibrate): a fresh fix asked now (maximumAge 0; after 6 s the watch's newest fix), the place put exactly there whatever the distance (name from Google, else the open map, else "La mia posizione (…)"), a toast "Posizione ricalibrata · Precisione ±n m" (saying when indoors makes it imprecise); "Seguimi di nuovo" does the same. The "ALPHA" tag beside Twin was already in place.
- Phone photos panel trimmed (2026-11-24, as asked with a screenshot): below 640 px the empty-state cards "File manager & Galleria" and "Fotocamera dispositivo" are hidden (LabMedia max-sm:hidden; the bottom bar's camera offers both), and Invia / Elimina sit side by side in two equal halves of the row, compact (h 2,25 rem, 13 px text; styles.css max-width 639.98px). The tablet-mode rule that made them 2,75 rem tall applied to phones too, since a phone also reads as coarse-pointer "slate": it now applies from 768 px only. Tablets unchanged.
